    ]),
}


@pytest.fixture
def money(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    for name in ("GIT_AUTHOR_NAME", "GIT_COMMITTER_NAME"):
        monkeypatch.setenv(name, "test")
    for name in ("GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL", "GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL"):
        monkeypatch.setenv(name, "test@example.com")
    root = tmp_path / "memory"
    (root / "money" / "tx").mkdir(parents=True)
    (root / "money" / "main.journal").write_text("include accounts.journal\ninclude tx/*.journal\n")
    (root / "money" / "accounts.journal").write_text("account assets  ; type: A\n")
    (root / "money" / "tx" / "opening.journal").write_text("; Opening balances.\n")
    (root / "money" / "categories.org").write_text(RULES)
    (root / "money.org").write_text(PLAN)
    subprocess.run(["git", "init", "--quiet", str(root)], check=True)
    subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(root), "add", "-A"], check=True)
    subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(root), "commit", "--quiet", "-m", "seed"], check=True)
    inbox = tmp_path / "inbox"
    inbox.mkdir()
    memory = Memory(root, AuditLog(tmp_path / "audit.jsonl"), remote=False)
    return Money(memory, inbox, AuditLog(tmp_path / "audit.jsonl"), lambda: date(2026, 11, 23))


def upload(money, names):
    for name in names:
        (money.inbox / name).write_text(FILES[name])


def journal(money):
    return (money.root / "tx" / "2026.journal").read_text()


def test_import_all_profiles(money):
    upload(money, FILES)
    report = money.import_inbox()
    assert "Kept" not in report, report
    text = journal(money)
    assert "expenses:food:groceries  AUD 12.34" in text
    assert "assets:bank:commbank:personal  AUD -12.34" in text
    assert "liabilities:afterpay  AUD 50.00" in text
    assert "assets:bank:coopnz:everyday  NZD -20.50" in text
    assert "assets:bank:wise:main  USD -30.00" in text
    assert list(money.inbox.iterdir()) == []


def test_import_masks_numbers(money):
    upload(money, ["commbank--personal.csv"])
    money.import_inbox()
    text = journal(money)
    assert "4111 1111" not in text and "12345678" not in text
    assert "************1111" in text


def test_second_import_adds_nothing(money):
    upload(money, ["commbank--personal.csv"])
    money.import_inbox()
    first = journal(money)
    upload(money, ["commbank--personal.csv"])
    report = money.import_inbox()
    assert "0 new, 3 already imported" in report
    assert journal(money) == first


def test_import_commits_and_never_commits_csv(money):
    upload(money, ["up--spending.csv"])
    money.import_inbox()
    files = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(money.memory.root), "ls-files"],
                           capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
    assert "money/tx/2026.journal" in files
    assert ".csv" not in files


def test_wrong_header_keeps_the_file(money):
    (money.inbox / "up--spending.csv").write_text("Date,Thing,Value\n2026-11-22,x,1\n")
    report = money.import_inbox()
    assert "Kept up--spending.csv" in report and "Send LUNA the header row" in report
    assert (money.inbox / "up--spending.csv").exists()


def test_wrong_column_count_keeps_the_file(money):
    (money.inbox / "commbank--personal.csv").write_text("20/11/2026,-1.00,X\n")
    assert "expects 4" in money.import_inbox()


def test_bad_file_name_is_refused(money):
    (money.inbox / "statement.csv").write_text("x\n")
    assert "<profile>--<account>.csv" in money.import_inbox()


def test_transfers_cancel(money):
    upload(money, ["commbank--personal.csv", "bendigo--savings.csv"])
    money.import_inbox()
    assert "assets:transfers" not in money.summary()


def test_summary(money):

    money.import_inbox()
    text = money.summary()
    assert "assets:bank:commbank:personal | AUD -522.33" in text
    # 3000 * 26 = 78000; minus 15600 costs, 6000 buffer, 10000 invest; / 52

def test_unknown_lists_masked_transactions(money):

def test_plan_reports_missing_income(tmp_path):

@pytest.mark.parametrize("pattern, account", [("a|b", "expenses:x"), ("(", "expenses:x"), ("ok", "Expenses")])
def test_bad_rules_are_refused(tmp_path, pattern, account):
    path = tmp_path / "categories.org"
    path.write_text(RULES)
    with pytest.raises(RuleError):

def test_old_main_journal_is_refused(money):
    money.main.write_text("~ every 4 weeks  pay\n    assets:income  AUD 0\n    income:fifo\n")
    upload(money, ["commbank--personal.csv"])
    assert "include tx/*.journal" in money.import_inbox()
    assert (money.inbox / "commbank--personal.csv").exists()


def test_add_rule_applies_to_next_import(money):
    assert "Added" in money.add_rule("netflix", "expenses:subscriptions")
    upload(money, ["commbank--personal.csv"])
    money.import_inbox()
    assert "expenses:subscriptions  AUD 9.99" in journal(money)

* Build: the Wise float

#+begin_quote
*WARNING:* A payment cannot be undone. Keep =mode = "dry-run"= until the approval flow works for some weeks.
Keep only a small amount in the Wise float. The float balance is the most that LUNA can lose.
#+end_quote

The Wise float is a Wise balance that only LUNA uses.
Coops puts money into the float by hand.
LUNA can request a payment from the float to a payee on the allowlist.

** Modes

| Mode      | Where Coops approves | Wise API calls              | Who sends the money                |
|-----------+----------------------+-----------------------------+------------------------------------|
| =off=     | -                    | none                        | Nobody. LUNA has no payment tool.  |
| =dry-run= | -                    | none                        | Nobody. LUNA records the request.  |
| =draft=   | The Wise app         | quote, transfer, status, cancel | Coops, in the Wise app         |
| =auto=    | Matrix (=approve <id>=) | quote, transfer, fund    | LUNA                               |

*The Wise app is the approval in =draft= mode.*
A Wise personal token cannot fund a transfer through the API.
LUNA makes the transfer, and no money moves.
LUNA tells Coops in Matrix to open the Wise app.
- To approve, fund the transfer in the Wise app. The app asks for its own authentication.
- To deny, cancel the transfer in the app, or do nothing.
  LUNA cancels a transfer that is not funded after 24 hours.
LUNA checks the status of each transfer every 5 minutes and reports the result.

The Wise app shows the payee and the amount from Wise.
A Matrix message is text from LUNA. The Wise screen is not.
Thus the Wise app is a stronger approval than a Matrix reply.

Wise Business has "payment approvals" with Approve and Reject in the app.
Wise does not allow those approval rules with a transfer from the API.
=auto= mode needs Wise Business, and keeps the approval in Matrix.

** Controls

1. Each payment needs an approval by Coops: funding in the Wise app (=draft=), or =approve <id>= in Matrix (=auto=).
2. The payee must be on the allowlist in the site values.
   LUNA cannot add a payee. A new payee needs a change to nyx and a deploy.
3. The currency must be the currency of the payee.
4. The amount must not be more than the limit of the payee or the limit per payment.
5. The total for the day must not be more than the limit per day.
6. In =auto= mode, LUNA checks the limits again when Coops approves.
   The day total counts transfers that wait for funding.
7. Each Wise transfer has an idempotency key from the approval ID. A retry cannot pay two times.
8. LUNA writes each payment to =/var/lib/luna/payments.jsonl= and to the audit log.

** Add a payee

1. In the Wise app, add the recipient. Make sure that the details are correct.
2. Find the recipient ID. In sandbox or live, =GET /v1/accounts?profile=<id>= lists the recipients.
3. Add the payee to =wise.payees= in the site values, with a limit.
4. Deploy.

** Payments module

#+begin_src python :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core/luna_core/payments.py
"""Wise float payments: limits, the payee allowlist, the Wise client, and the executor."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
import uuid
from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import UTC, date, datetime, timedelta
from decimal import Decimal, InvalidOperation
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import httpx

from .audit import AuditLog

MODES = ("off", "dry-run", "draft", "auto")
DRAFT_TTL = timedelta(hours=24)
# Statuses that do not count toward the day total.
CLOSED = frozenset({"failed", "cancelled", "expired"})
# Wise statuses after funding. Refer to the Wise transfer status list.
FUNDED = frozenset({"processing", "funds_converted", "outgoing_payment_sent"})
_REFERENCE = re.compile(r"[^A-Za-z0-9 ]")


class PaymentError(Exception):
    """The payment is not permitted, or Wise refused the payment."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Payee:
    name: str
    recipient_id: int
    currency: str
    max_per_payment: Decimal


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class WiseSettings:
    mode: str
    sandbox: bool
    profile_type: str
    max_per_payment: Decimal
    max_per_day: Decimal
    payees: dict[str, Payee]

    @classmethod
    def from_json(cls, text: str) -> WiseSettings:
        data = json.loads(text)
        mode = data.get("mode", "off")
        if mode not in MODES:
            raise PaymentError(f"The Wise mode {mode!r} is not one of {', '.join(MODES)}.")
        payees = {
            name: Payee(name, int(p["recipientId"]), p["currency"].upper(), Decimal(str(p["maxPerPayment"])))
            for name, p in data.get("payees", {}).items()
        }
        return cls(
            mode=mode,
            sandbox=bool(data.get("sandbox", True)),
            profile_type=data.get("profileType", "personal").lower(),
            max_per_payment=Decimal(str(data.get("maxPerPayment", 100))),
            max_per_day=Decimal(str(data.get("maxPerDay", 200))),
            payees=payees,
        )


def clean_reference(text: str) -> str:
    """Wise accepts a short reference. Keep letters, digits and spaces."""
    return _REFERENCE.sub("", text).strip()[:18].strip() or "LUNA"


def now_utc() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(UTC)


class PaymentLog:
    """The append-only record of payments. The last record of a payment is its state."""

    def __init__(self, path: Path) -> None:
        self.path = path

    def latest(self) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
        if not self.path.exists():
            return {}
        state: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
        for line in self.path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
            record = json.loads(line)
            previous = state.get(record["id"], {})
            # Keep the time of the first record: the 24-hour limit counts from the request.
            state[record["id"]] = {**previous, **record, "time": previous.get("time", record.get("time"))}
        return state

    def total_for(self, day: date, currency: str) -> Decimal:
        total = Decimal(0)
        for record in self.latest().values():
            if record["day"] == day.isoformat() and record["currency"] == currency and record["status"] not in CLOSED:
                total += Decimal(record["amount"])
        return total

    def write(self, **record: Any) -> None:
        record.setdefault("time", now_utc().isoformat())
        with self.path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
            handle.write(json.dumps(record, default=str, sort_keys=True) + "\n")


def check(settings: WiseSettings, log: PaymentLog, payee: str, amount_text: str, currency: str, day: date) -> tuple[Payee, Decimal]:
    """Apply every control. Return the payee and the amount, or raise PaymentError."""
    if settings.mode == "off":
        raise PaymentError("Payments are off.")
    if payee not in settings.payees:
        raise PaymentError(f"{payee!r} is not on the allowlist. Allowed: {', '.join(settings.payees) or 'none'}.")
    target = settings.payees[payee]
    try:
        amount = Decimal(amount_text).quantize(Decimal("0.01"))
    except InvalidOperation as error:
        raise PaymentError(f"{amount_text!r} is not an amount.") from error
    if currency.upper() != target.currency:
        raise PaymentError(f"{payee} takes {target.currency}, not {currency.upper()}.")
    if amount <= 0:
        raise PaymentError("The amount must be more than zero.")
    limit = min(target.max_per_payment, settings.max_per_payment)
    if amount > limit:
        raise PaymentError(f"{target.currency} {amount} is more than the limit of {target.currency} {limit}.")
    spent = log.total_for(day, target.currency)
    if spent + amount > settings.max_per_day:
        raise PaymentError(
            f"The day total would be {target.currency} {spent + amount}. "
            f"The limit per day is {target.currency} {settings.max_per_day}."
        )
    return target, amount


class WiseClient:
    """The few Wise API calls that the float uses."""

    def __init__(self, token: str, sandbox: bool, transport: httpx.AsyncBaseTransport | None = None) -> None:
        base = "https://api.wise-sandbox.com" if sandbox else "https://api.wise.com"
        self.http = httpx.AsyncClient(
            base_url=base, headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"}, timeout=30, transport=transport
        )

    async def _call(self, method: str, path: str, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
        response = await self.http.request(method, path, **kwargs)
        if response.status_code >= 400:
            raise PaymentError(f"Wise refused {method} {path}: HTTP {response.status_code} {response.text[:200]}")
        return response.json()

    async def profile_id(self, profile_type: str) -> int:
        for profile in await self._call("GET", "/v1/profiles"):
            if str(profile.get("type", "")).lower() == profile_type:
                return int(profile["id"])
        raise PaymentError(f"No {profile_type} profile on this Wise account.")

    async def quote(self, profile: int, currency: str, amount: Decimal) -> str:
        body = {"sourceCurrency": currency, "targetCurrency": currency, "targetAmount": float(amount)}
        return str((await self._call("POST", f"/v3/profiles/{profile}/quotes", json=body))["id"])

    async def transfer(self, recipient_id: int, quote_id: str, reference: str, key: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        body = {
            "targetAccount": recipient_id,
            "quoteUuid": quote_id,
            "customerTransactionId": key,
            "details": {"reference": reference},
        }
        return await self._call("POST", "/v1/transfers", json=body)

    async def fund(self, profile: int, transfer_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._call("POST", f"/v3/profiles/{profile}/transfers/{transfer_id}/payments", json={"type": "BALANCE"})

    async def status(self, transfer_id: int) -> str:
        return str((await self._call("GET", f"/v1/transfers/{transfer_id}"))["status"])

    async def cancel(self, transfer_id: int) -> None:
        await self._call("PUT", f"/v1/transfers/{transfer_id}/cancel")


Notify = Callable[[str], Awaitable[Any]]


class Payments:
    """Make payments within the controls, and watch the transfers that wait for Coops."""

    def __init__(self, settings: WiseSettings, log: PaymentLog, audit: AuditLog,
                 client: WiseClient | None, today: Callable[[], date],
                 now: Callable[[], datetime] = now_utc) -> None:
        self.settings = settings
        self.log = log
        self.audit = audit
        self.client = client
        self.today = today
        self.now = now
        self.notify: Notify | None = None

    async def _tell(self, text: str) -> None:
        if self.notify is not None:
            await self.notify(text)

    def summary(self, payee: str, amount: Decimal, currency: str, reference: str, reason: str) -> str:
        return (f"Pay **{payee}** {currency} {amount}, reference \"{reference}\".\nReason: {reason}\n"
                "Auto: after your approval, LUNA makes and funds the transfer.")

    async def _send(self, params: dict[str, Any], fund: bool) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if self.client is None:
            raise PaymentError("No Wise token. Add wise-api-token to the secrets.")
        target = self.settings.payees[params["payee"]]
        profile = await self.client.profile_id(self.settings.profile_type)
        quote = await self.client.quote(profile, target.currency, Decimal(params["amount"]))
        transfer = await self.client.transfer(target.recipient_id, quote, params["reference"], params["key"])
        if fund:
            await self.client.fund(profile, int(transfer["id"]))
        return transfer

    async def request(self, payee: str, amount: str, currency: str, reference: str, key: str) -> str:
        """The tool path for dry-run and draft modes. Draft: the Wise app is the approval."""
        day = self.today()
        target, value = check(self.settings, self.log, payee, amount, currency, day)
        params = {"payee": target.name, "amount": str(value), "currency": target.currency,
                  "reference": clean_reference(reference), "key": key}
        record = dict(id=key, day=day.isoformat(), payee=target.name, amount=str(value),
                      currency=target.currency, mode=self.settings.mode, time=self.now().isoformat())
        if self.settings.mode == "dry-run":
            self.log.write(**record, status="dry-run")
            return (f"Dry run: LUNA would make a Wise transfer of {target.currency} {value} to {target.name}, "
                    "for you to approve in the Wise app. LUNA sent nothing.")
        try:
            transfer = await self._send(params, fund=False)
        except (PaymentError, httpx.HTTPError) as error:
            self.log.write(**record, status="failed", error=str(error))
            raise PaymentError(str(error)) from error
        self.log.write(**record, status="draft", transfer_id=transfer.get("id"))
        self.audit.write("payment.draft", payee=target.name, amount=str(value), transfer_id=transfer.get("id"))
        text = (f"**Approve in the Wise app:** transfer {transfer.get('id')}, {target.currency} {value} "
                f"to {target.name}. To deny, cancel it in the app or do nothing. "
                "LUNA cancels it after 24 hours.")
        await self._tell(text)
        return text

    async def execute(self, params: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        """The Matrix approval executor, for auto mode. Check the limits again first."""
        day = self.today()
        target, value = check(self.settings, self.log, params["payee"], params["amount"], params["currency"], day)
        record = dict(id=params["key"], day=day.isoformat(), payee=target.name, amount=str(value),
                      currency=target.currency, mode=self.settings.mode, time=self.now().isoformat())
        try:
            transfer = await self._send(params, fund=True)
        except (PaymentError, httpx.HTTPError) as error:
            self.log.write(**record, status="failed", error=str(error))
            raise
        self.log.write(**record, status="funded", transfer_id=transfer.get("id"))
        self.audit.write("payment.funded", payee=target.name, amount=str(value), transfer_id=transfer.get("id"))
        return f"Sent {target.currency} {value} to {target.name}. Wise transfer {transfer.get('id')}."

    async def watch_once(self) -> None:
        """Report funded drafts. Cancel drafts that wait for more than 24 hours."""
        if self.client is None:
            return
        for key, record in self.log.latest().items():
            if record.get("status") != "draft":
                continue
            transfer_id = int(record["transfer_id"])
            status = await self.client.status(transfer_id)
            label = f"{record['currency']} {record['amount']} to {record['payee']} (transfer {transfer_id})"
            if status in FUNDED:
                self.log.write(id=key, status="funded", wise_status=status)
                self.audit.write("payment.funded", transfer_id=transfer_id)
                await self._tell(f"You approved {label} in the Wise app. Wise is sending it.")
            elif status == "cancelled":
                self.log.write(id=key, status="cancelled")
                await self._tell(f"You cancelled {label}.")
            elif self.now() - datetime.fromisoformat(record["time"]) > DRAFT_TTL:
                await self.client.cancel(transfer_id)
                self.log.write(id=key, status="expired")
                self.audit.write("payment.expired", transfer_id=transfer_id)
                await self._tell(f"LUNA cancelled {label}. Nobody funded it in 24 hours.")


def payment_key() -> str:
    """The idempotency key for one payment. Wise uses it to refuse a second transfer."""
    return str(uuid.uuid4())
#+end_src

** Payment tool

The tool is tier 2. The money moves only after the approval by Coops.

#+begin_src python :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core/luna_core/payment_tools.py
"""The payment tool. In draft mode the Wise app is the approval; in auto mode, Matrix."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from anthropic import beta_async_tool

from .approvals import Approvals
from .audit import AuditLog
from .payments import PaymentError, Payments, check, clean_reference, payment_key


def build_payment_tools(payments: Payments, approvals: Approvals, audit: AuditLog) -> list[Any]:
    approvals.register("wise_transfer", payments.execute)

    @beta_async_tool
    async def payment_request(payee: str, amount: str, currency: str, reference: str, reason: str) -> str:
        """Request a payment from the Wise float to a payee on the allowlist.

        No money moves now. Coops approves in the Wise app (draft mode) or in Matrix (auto mode).

        Args:
            payee: The name of the payee on the allowlist.
            amount: The amount, for example "45.00".
            currency: The currency code of the payee, for example "AUD".
            reference: The payment reference. Wise keeps 18 letters, digits and spaces.
            reason: Why LUNA asks for this payment, in one sentence.
        """
        audit.write("tool", name="payment_request", payee=payee, amount=amount, currency=currency, reason=reason)
        try:
            if payments.settings.mode in ("dry-run", "draft"):
                return await payments.request(payee, amount, currency, reference, payment_key())
            target, value = check(payments.settings, payments.log, payee, amount, currency, payments.today())
        except PaymentError as error:
            return f"Refused: {error}"
        ref = clean_reference(reference)
        params = {"payee": target.name, "amount": str(value), "currency": target.currency,
                  "reference": ref, "key": payment_key()}
        item = await approvals.request("wise_transfer", params,
                                       payments.summary(target.name, value, target.currency, ref, reason))
        return f"Approval request {item.id} sent to Coops in Matrix."

    return [payment_request]
#+end_src

** Payment tests

The tests use a fake Wise server. They make no network call.

#+begin_src python :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core/tests/test_payments.py
import asyncio
import json
from datetime import UTC, date, datetime, timedelta

import httpx
import pytest

from luna_core.approvals import Approvals
from luna_core.audit import AuditLog
from luna_core.payment_tools import build_payment_tools
from luna_core.payments import PaymentError, PaymentLog, Payments, WiseClient, WiseSettings, check, clean_reference

TODAY = date(2026, 11, 23)


def settings(mode):
    return WiseSettings.from_json(json.dumps({
        "mode": mode, "sandbox": True, "maxPerPayment": 100, "maxPerDay": 150,
        "payees": {"landlord": {"recipientId": 42, "currency": "AUD", "maxPerPayment": 80}},
    }))


class FakeWise:
    def __init__(self):
        self.calls = []
        self.state = "incoming_payment_waiting"

    def handler(self, request):
        path = request.url.path
        self.calls.append((request.method, path))
        if path == "/v1/profiles":
            return httpx.Response(200, json=[{"id": 7, "type": "PERSONAL"}])
        if path.endswith("/quotes"):
            return httpx.Response(200, json={"id": "q-1"})
        if path == "/v1/transfers":
            body = json.loads(request.content)
            assert body["targetAccount"] == 42 and body["quoteUuid"] == "q-1"
            return httpx.Response(200, json={"id": 555, "status": "incoming_payment_waiting"})
        if path == "/v1/transfers/555" and request.method == "GET":
            return httpx.Response(200, json={"id": 555, "status": self.state})
        if path == "/v1/transfers/555/cancel":
            self.state = "cancelled"
            return httpx.Response(200, json={"id": 555, "status": "cancelled"})
        if path.endswith("/payments"):
            return httpx.Response(200, json={"status": "COMPLETED"})
        return httpx.Response(404)


class Clock:
    def __init__(self):
        self.value = datetime(2026, 11, 23, 9, 0, tzinfo=UTC)

    def __call__(self):
        return self.value


def make(tmp_path, mode):
    fake, clock, told = FakeWise(), Clock(), []
    client = WiseClient("token", True, transport=httpx.MockTransport(fake.handler))
    audit = AuditLog(tmp_path / "audit.jsonl")
    payments = Payments(settings(mode), PaymentLog(tmp_path / "payments.jsonl"), audit, client, lambda: TODAY, clock)

    async def notify(text):
        told.append(text)

    payments.notify = notify
    return payments, fake, clock, told


def run(coro):
    return asyncio.run(coro)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("payee, amount, currency, message", [
    ("stranger", "10", "AUD", "not on the allowlist"),
    ("landlord", "10", "NZD", "takes AUD"),
    ("landlord", "0", "AUD", "more than zero"),
    ("landlord", "90", "AUD", "limit of AUD 80"),
    ("landlord", "abc", "AUD", "not an amount"),
])
def test_controls(tmp_path, payee, amount, currency, message):
    payments, *_ = make(tmp_path, "draft")
    with pytest.raises(PaymentError, match=message):
        check(payments.settings, payments.log, payee, amount, currency, TODAY)


def test_off_refuses(tmp_path):
    payments, *_ = make(tmp_path, "off")
    with pytest.raises(PaymentError, match="off"):
        check(payments.settings, payments.log, "landlord", "10", "AUD", TODAY)


def test_dry_run_calls_nothing(tmp_path):
    payments, fake, *_ = make(tmp_path, "dry-run")
    assert "Dry run" in run(payments.request("landlord", "50", "AUD", "Rent", "k-1"))
    assert fake.calls == []


def test_draft_makes_a_transfer_and_never_funds(tmp_path):
    payments, fake, _, told = make(tmp_path, "draft")
    text = run(payments.request("landlord", "50", "AUD", "Rent", "k-1"))
    assert "Approve in the Wise app" in text and told == [text]
    assert ("POST", "/v1/transfers") in fake.calls
    assert not any(path.endswith("/payments") for _, path in fake.calls)


def test_drafts_count_toward_the_day_limit(tmp_path):
    payments, *_ = make(tmp_path, "draft")
    run(payments.request("landlord", "80", "AUD", "Rent", "k-1"))
    with pytest.raises(PaymentError, match="limit per day"):
        check(payments.settings, payments.log, "landlord", "80", "AUD", TODAY)


def test_watch_reports_funding(tmp_path):
    payments, fake, _, told = make(tmp_path, "draft")
    run(payments.request("landlord", "50", "AUD", "Rent", "k-1"))
    fake.state = "processing"
    run(payments.watch_once())
    assert "You approved" in told[-1]
    assert payments.log.latest()["k-1"]["status"] == "funded"
    run(payments.watch_once())
    assert len(told) == 2


def test_watch_cancels_after_24_hours(tmp_path):
    payments, fake, clock, told = make(tmp_path, "draft")
    run(payments.request("landlord", "50", "AUD", "Rent", "k-1"))
    clock.value += timedelta(hours=23)
    run(payments.watch_once())
    assert ("PUT", "/v1/transfers/555/cancel") not in fake.calls
    clock.value += timedelta(hours=2)
    run(payments.watch_once())
    assert ("PUT", "/v1/transfers/555/cancel") in fake.calls
    assert "Nobody funded it" in told[-1]
    assert payments.log.total_for(TODAY, "AUD") == 0


def test_watch_sees_a_cancel_in_the_app(tmp_path):
    payments, fake, _, told = make(tmp_path, "draft")
    run(payments.request("landlord", "50", "AUD", "Rent", "k-1"))
    fake.state = "cancelled"
    run(payments.watch_once())
    assert "You cancelled" in told[-1]


def test_auto_needs_a_matrix_approval(tmp_path):
    payments, fake, *_ = make(tmp_path, "auto")
    (tmp_path / "approvals").mkdir()
    approvals = Approvals(tmp_path / "approvals", payments.audit)
    tools = build_payment_tools(payments, approvals, payments.audit)
    assert tools
    item = run(approvals.request("wise_transfer", {"payee": "landlord", "amount": "50.00", "currency": "AUD",
                                                   "reference": "Rent", "key": "k-1"}, "test"))
    assert fake.calls == []
    assert "Sent AUD 50.00" in run(approvals.approve(item.id))
    assert ("POST", "/v3/profiles/7/transfers/555/payments") in fake.calls


def test_reference_is_cleaned():
    assert clean_reference("Rent; DROP TABLE <x> 2026-11") == "Rent DROP TABLE x"
#+end_src

* Build: the core API

The core API is the contract between the Phoenix app and the core.
The Python core serves the core API until stage E5.
The Elixir core serves the same contract after E5, so the Phoenix app does not change.
Do not add features to the Python side. Add each new feature to the contract first.

** Contract

The core API is JSON over HTTP on the unix socket =/run/luna-api/api.sock=.

| Request                           | Body                      | Reply                                    |
|-----------------------------------+---------------------------+------------------------------------------|
| =GET /api/history=                |                           | ={"messages": [{"role", "text"}]}=       |
| =POST /api/chat=                  | ={"text", "image"?, "channel"?}= | ={"reply"}=                       |
| =GET /api/approvals=              |                           | ={"approvals": [{"id", "summary", "created"}]}= |
| =POST /api/approvals/{id}/{action}= | ={}=, action is =approve= or =deny= | ={"result"}=                   |
| =GET /api/money=                  |                           | ={"text"}=                               |
| =GET /api/memory/graph=           |                           | ={"nodes": [{"id", "title", "tags", "owner", "file"}], "links": [{"source", "target"}]}= |
| =GET /api/system=                 |                           | ={"started", "update", "memory", "settings", "pending_approvals", "events"}= |

=image= is ={"media_type", "data"}=. =data= is base64.
=channel= is =text= (the default) or =voice=.
The core puts =[voice]= before a voice message. The system prompt tells LUNA to reply for the ear.
LUNA accepts JPEG, PNG or WebP, to 5 MB.
An error reply has the status 400 and ={"error"}=.

** Access

The core API has no login check. The file permissions are the access control.
The directory =/run/luna-api= has the mode 2750, the owner =luna= and the group =luna-api=.
The setgid bit gives the socket the group =luna-api=.
The umask of the core gives the socket the mode 0770.
The system user =luna_web= is the only member of =luna-api=.

Thus only the core and the Phoenix app can open the socket.
A different local process cannot send a question to the agent, or approve a request.

** Server

#+begin_src python :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core/luna_core/web.py
"""The core API: chat, approvals, money and system, on a unix socket.

The Phoenix app is the only client. Refer to "Build: the core API" in LUNA.org.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import binascii
import json
import subprocess
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from aiohttp import web

from .approvals import Approvals
from .audit import AuditLog
from .sysinfo import update_notice

IMAGE_TYPES = frozenset({"image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/webp"})
MAX_IMAGE_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024


class WebApp:
    def __init__(self, agent: Any, approvals: Approvals, audit: AuditLog,
                 money: Any = None, settings: dict[str, Any] | None = None, memory_root: Any = None) -> None:
        self.agent = agent
        self.approvals = approvals
        self.audit = audit
        self.money = money
        self.settings = settings or {}
        self.memory_root = memory_root
        self.started = datetime.now(UTC)

    def app(self) -> web.Application:
        app = web.Application(client_max_size=8 * 1024 * 1024)
        app.add_routes([
            web.get("/api/history", self.history),
            web.post("/api/chat", self.chat),
            web.get("/api/approvals", self.list_approvals),
            web.post("/api/approvals/{id}/{action}", self.decide),
            web.get("/api/money", self.money_panel),
            web.get("/api/system", self.system),
        ])
        return app

    async def history(self, _: web.Request) -> web.Response:
        return web.json_response({"messages": self.agent.transcript()})

    async def chat(self, request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
        body = await request.json()
        text = str(body.get("text", "")).strip()
        image = body.get("image")
        images: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
        if image:
            media_type = str(image.get("media_type", ""))
            data = str(image.get("data", ""))
            if media_type not in IMAGE_TYPES:
                return web.json_response({"error": "LUNA accepts JPEG, PNG or WebP images."}, status=400)
            try:
                size = len(base64.b64decode(data, validate=True))
            except (binascii.Error, ValueError):
                return web.json_response({"error": "The image data is not valid base64."}, status=400)
            if size > MAX_IMAGE_BYTES:
                return web.json_response({"error": "The image is more than 5 MB."}, status=400)
            images.append((media_type, data))
        if not text and not images:
            return web.json_response({"error": "Write a message."}, status=400)
        voice = body.get("channel") == "voice"
        if voice:
            text = "[voice] " + text
        self.audit.write("web.chat", chars=len(text), images=len(images), voice=voice)
        reply = await self.agent.ask(text or "What do you see?", images=images)
        return web.json_response({"reply": reply})

    async def list_approvals(self, _: web.Request) -> web.Response:
        items = [{"id": i.id, "summary": i.summary, "created": i.created} for i in self.approvals.pending()]
        return web.json_response({"approvals": items})

    async def decide(self, request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
        request_id, action = request.match_info["id"], request.match_info["action"]
        if action == "approve":
            result = await self.approvals.approve(request_id)
        elif action == "deny":
            result = self.approvals.deny(request_id)
        else:
            return web.json_response({"error": "Use approve or deny."}, status=400)
        self.audit.write("web.decision", id=request_id, action=action)
        return web.json_response({"result": result})

    async def money_panel(self, _: web.Request) -> web.Response:
        if self.money is None:
            return web.json_response({"text": "Money is off. Refer to 'The gate for phase 2' in LUNA.org."})
        return web.json_response({"text": self.money.summary()})

    async def system(self, _: web.Request) -> web.Response:
        today = datetime.now(UTC).date()
        last_commit = ""
        if self.memory_root is not None:
            result = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(self.memory_root), "log", "-1", "--format=%h %s (%cr)"],
                                    capture_output=True, text=True, check=False)
            last_commit = result.stdout.strip()
        events = []
        try:
            lines = self.audit.path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()[-15:]
        except OSError:
            lines = []
        for line in lines:
            record = json.loads(line)
            events.append({"time": record.get("time", ""), "event": record.get("event", "")})
        return web.json_response({
            "started": self.started.isoformat(),
            "update": update_notice(today) or "nixpkgs is less than 14 days old.",
            "memory": last_commit,
            "settings": self.settings,
            "pending_approvals": len(self.approvals.pending()),
            "events": events,
        })


async def serve(webapp: WebApp, path: Path) -> None:
    """Run the core API on the unix socket at path until the process stops."""
    runner = web.AppRunner(webapp.app(), access_log=None)
    await runner.setup()
    # A socket from an earlier run stops the bind.
    path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
    site = web.UnixSite(runner, str(path))
    await site.start()
    webapp.audit.write("api.ready", path=str(path))
#+end_src

** Core API tests

The Phoenix tests cover the login, the page and the origin.
These tests cover the core side of the contract.

#+begin_src python :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core/tests/test_web.py
import asyncio
import base64
import stat

import aiohttp
from aiohttp.test_utils import TestClient, TestServer

from luna_core.approvals import Approvals
from luna_core.audit import AuditLog
from luna_core.web import WebApp, serve


class FakeAgent:
    def __init__(self):
        self.asked = []

    async def ask(self, text, images=()):
        self.asked.append((text, list(images)))
        return "reply: " + text

    def transcript(self):
        return [{"role": "user", "text": "hello"}, {"role": "assistant", "text": "hi"}]


def make(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "approvals").mkdir()
    audit = AuditLog(tmp_path / "audit.jsonl")
    (tmp_path / "audit.jsonl").write_text("")
    approvals = Approvals(tmp_path / "approvals", audit)
    calls = []

    async def executor(params):
        calls.append(params)
        return "done"

    approvals.register("test", executor)
    agent = FakeAgent()
    return WebApp(agent, approvals, audit), agent, approvals, calls


def run(webapp, scenario):
    async def go():
        async with TestClient(TestServer(webapp.app())) as client:
            return await scenario(client)
    return asyncio.run(go())


def test_history(tmp_path):
    webapp, *_ = make(tmp_path)
    async def scenario(client):
        return await (await client.get("/api/history")).json()
    assert run(webapp, scenario)["messages"][1] == {"role": "assistant", "text": "hi"}


def test_chat_with_an_image(tmp_path):
    webapp, agent, *_ = make(tmp_path)
    data = base64.b64encode(b"\xff\xd8fake-jpeg").decode()
    async def scenario(client):
        response = await client.post("/api/chat",
                                     json={"text": "what is this?", "image": {"media_type": "image/jpeg", "data": data}})
        return response.status, await response.json()
    status, body = run(webapp, scenario)
    assert status == 200 and body["reply"] == "reply: what is this?"
    assert agent.asked == [("what is this?", [("image/jpeg", data)])]


def test_a_voice_message_is_marked(tmp_path):
    webapp, agent, *_ = make(tmp_path)
    async def scenario(client):
        return (await client.post("/api/chat", json={"text": "next swing?", "channel": "voice"})).status
    assert run(webapp, scenario) == 200
    assert agent.asked == [("[voice] next swing?", [])]


def test_bad_image_is_refused(tmp_path):
    webapp, agent, *_ = make(tmp_path)
    async def scenario(client):
        a = await client.post("/api/chat", json={"text": "x", "image": {"media_type": "image/gif", "data": "AA=="}})
        b = await client.post("/api/chat", json={"text": "x", "image": {"media_type": "image/png", "data": "not base64!"}})
        return a.status, b.status
    assert run(webapp, scenario) == (400, 400)
    assert agent.asked == []


def test_approve(tmp_path):
    webapp, _, approvals, calls = make(tmp_path)
    item = asyncio.run(approvals.request("test", {"x": 1}, "Test payment"))
    async def scenario(client):
        listed = await (await client.get("/api/approvals")).json()
        result = await (await client.post(f"/api/approvals/{item.id}/approve", json={})).json()
        return listed, result
    listed, result = run(webapp, scenario)
    assert listed["approvals"][0]["summary"] == "Test payment"
    assert result["result"] == "done" and calls == [{"x": 1}]


def test_money_is_off_by_default(tmp_path):
    webapp, *_ = make(tmp_path)
    async def scenario(client):
        return await (await client.get("/api/money")).json()
    assert "Money is off" in run(webapp, scenario)["text"]


def test_system(tmp_path):
    webapp, *_ = make(tmp_path)
    async def scenario(client):
        return await (await client.get("/api/system")).json()
    data = run(webapp, scenario)
    assert data["pending_approvals"] == 0 and "update" in data


def test_serve_on_a_unix_socket(tmp_path):
    webapp, *_ = make(tmp_path)
    path = tmp_path / "api.sock"
    path.write_text("an old socket")
    async def go():
        await serve(webapp, path)
        connector = aiohttp.UnixConnector(path=str(path))
        async with aiohttp.ClientSession(connector=connector) as session:
            async with session.get("http://core/api/money") as response:
                return await response.json()
    assert "Money is off" in asyncio.run(go())["text"]
    assert stat.S_ISSOCK(path.stat().st_mode)
#+end_src

* Build: the Elixir core

This section is stage E5 of [[*LUNA on Elixir][LUNA on Elixir]].
The Elixir core =luna_core= replaces the Python core in =pkgs/luna-core=.
It is a separate release, and it runs as the user =luna=, as the Python core does.
It serves the same [[*Build: the core API][core API]] on the same socket, so the Phoenix app does not change.

The port goes module by module. The tests of each module come first.
The Python core stays in service until the cutover.
At the cutover, all ported tests pass, and the Python core retires.

** Port progress

| Step | Modules                                   | Python tests            | Elixir tests | State   |
|------+-------------------------------------------+-------------------------+--------------+---------|
| 1    | Config, Audit, Approvals, Privacy, Sysinfo | approvals, privacy, sysinfo | 32       | Done    |
| 2    | Memory                                    | memory                  | 10           | Done    |
| 3    | The core API server                       | web                     | 9            | Done    |
| 4    | Agent, the Claude client and tools        | (none)                  | 10 (new)     | Done    |
| 5    | Matrix bot, Telegram                      | (none)                  | 15 (new)     | Done    |
| 6    | Scheduler                                 | scheduler               | 8            | Done    |
| 7    | Money and the Wise float                  | money, payments         | 35           | Done    |
| 8    | GEV and REA tools (new)                   |                         |              |         |
| —    | Cutover: Config, Main, the unit switch    |                         | 2            | Done    |

** Changes from the Python core

- =Approvals= is a GenServer. It takes a request from disk before the executor runs.
  Thus two approvals of one request run the executor once. A test makes sure of this.
- =Memory= is a GenServer. It runs one git operation at a time.
  The chat and the morning brief can run at the same time, so this matters.
- =Memory.resolve/2= refuses a path through a symlink.
  The Python core followed a symlink and then checked the result.
- The time in an audit line ends in =Z= in place of =+00:00=. The two forms give the same time.
- The Python agent had no tests. The Elixir agent has 10, against a fake Messages API.
- Each client tool has =strict: true=, so the tool input always matches its schema.
- The Matrix bot uses the client-server API directly. Elixir has no matrix-nio.
- Each owner message runs in its own task. Thus =approve= works while the agent answers a question.
  In the Python bot, a long question stopped the commands until the reply.
- After a 401 on sync, the bot deletes the session file and logs in again, once.
- The core never calls =File.chmod=. Erlang also calls chown(2) there, and the seccomp filter of the
  unit (=~@privileged=) kills the process. The core runs chmod(1) in its place.
- The bot renders Markdown with its own small renderer. Refer to [[*Matrix messages][Matrix messages]].
- The money code uses =decimal= 3.2. Earlier versions had two DoS advisories
  (EEF-CVE-2026-32686, EEF-CVE-2026-97853). Run =make deps-audit= after each lock change.
- The transaction IDs use the same SHA-256 key as the Python core. A test makes sure of this.
- The payment tool refuses a payment over a limit before any approval request.
  In the Python core this was so too, but no test made sure of it.

** Package

The check compiles the dependencies for the test environment before the tests.
=mixRelease= compiles them for =prod= only.
The tests of =Memory= set =GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL= to =/dev/null=, so the git configuration of the user (for example commit signing) cannot change a test.

#+begin_src nix :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/default.nix
{ lib, beamPackages, git, hledger }:

let
  pname = "luna-core-ex";
  version = "0.1.0";
  src = lib.fileset.toSource {
    root = ./.;
    fileset = lib.fileset.unions [ ./mix.exs ./mix.lock ./config ./lib ./test ];
  };
in
beamPackages.mixRelease {
  inherit pname version src;

  # Change the hash after each change to mix.lock. Refer to "Dependencies"
  # in luna/LUNA.org.
  mixFodDeps = beamPackages.fetchMixDeps {
    pname = "${pname}-deps";
    inherit version src;
    hash = "sha256-TvDqfzeeC55gKZO2R2o4juDH99tV9pl7gdQ7+lwnp1E=";
  };

  doCheck = true;
  # The money tests run hledger. The memory tests run git.
  nativeCheckInputs = [ git hledger ];
  checkPhase = ''
    runHook preCheck
    # The deps are compiled for prod only. Compile them for test from the
    # sources in deps/. This step needs no network.
    MIX_ENV=test mix deps.compile
    MIX_ENV=test mix test --no-deps-check
    runHook postCheck
  '';

  meta = {
    description = "The LUNA core, in Elixir. It replaces pkgs/luna-core at the cutover.";
    mainProgram = "luna_core";
  };
}
#+end_src

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/mix.exs
defmodule LunaCore.MixProject do
  use Mix.Project

  def project do
    [
      app: :luna_core,
      version: "0.1.0",
      elixir: "~> 1.18",
      elixirc_paths: if(Mix.env() == :test, do: ["lib", "test/support"], else: ["lib"]),
      start_permanent: Mix.env() == :prod,
      deps: deps(),
      releases: [luna_core: [include_executables_for: [:unix]]]
    ]
  end

  def application do
    [mod: {LunaCore.Application, []}, extra_applications: [:logger, :crypto]]
  end

  # No dependency is for one environment only, so one fetchMixDeps hash
  # serves the release and the tests.
  defp deps do
    [
      {:bandit, "~> 1.15"},
      # 3.1.2 and later fix EEF-CVE-2026-32686 and EEF-CVE-2026-97853.
      {:decimal, "~> 3.2"},
      {:jason, "~> 1.4"},
      {:nimble_csv, "~> 1.2"},
      {:plug, "~> 1.18"},
      {:req, "~> 0.7"},
      {:tz, "~> 0.28"}
    ]
  end
end

import Config

config :logger, :default_formatter, format: "[$level] $message\n"

# The owner time zone. tz bundles the IANA data and does not fetch updates.
config :elixir, :time_zone_database, Tz.TimeZoneDatabase

# The tests start no service. Each test starts the parts that it needs.
config :luna_core, start_services: config_env() != :test

** Cutover

The site value =core= selects the core that runs as =luna-core=. The unit is the same for the two cores:
the same user, secrets, state, memory and socket. The Elixir core reads the same files as the Python core:
the Matrix session, the owner room, the approvals, the audit log and the payment log.

To go back to the Python core:
1. In =hosts/luna/site.nix=, set =core = "python";=.
2. Run =make deploy= in =nyx/luna=.

The conversation is in memory only, in each core. A switch starts a new conversation.

The brief agent has no payment tool. Thus a scheduled job cannot ask for a tier 2 action when no human is present (backlog #10).

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/lib/luna_core/config.ex
defmodule LunaCore.Config do
  @moduledoc """
  Reads the LUNA configuration from the environment and from the systemd
  credentials. The names are the same as for the Python core, so the unit
  in core.nix serves both.
  """

  @doc "The configuration map. Raises when a required setting is missing."
  def load(env \\ System.get_env()) do
    state_dir = Map.get(env, "LUNA_STATE_DIR", "/var/lib/luna")

    %{
      state_dir: state_dir,
      homeserver: required(env, "LUNA_HOMESERVER"),
      matrix_user: required(env, "LUNA_MATRIX_USER"),
      owner: required(env, "LUNA_OWNER"),
      owner_timezone: Map.get(env, "LUNA_OWNER_TIMEZONE", "Etc/UTC") |> zone(),
      brief_time: Map.get(env, "LUNA_BRIEF_TIME", "07:00") |> brief_time(),
      model: Map.get(env, "LUNA_MODEL", "claude-opus-5-5"),
      effort: Map.get(env, "LUNA_EFFORT", "medium"),
      anthropic_api_key: credential(env, "anthropic_api_key") || raise("LUNA: the setting anthropic_api_key is missing."),
      matrix_password: credential(env, "matrix_password") || raise("LUNA: the setting matrix_password is missing."),
      telegram_token: credential(env, "telegram_token"),
      telegram_chat_id: credential(env, "telegram_chat_id"),
      money_enabled: Map.get(env, "LUNA_MONEY", "0") == "1",
      api_socket: Map.get(env, "LUNA_API_SOCKET", "/run/luna-api/api.sock"),
      wise_json: Map.get(env, "LUNA_WISE", ~s({"mode": "off"})),
      wise_token: credential(env, "wise_api_token"),
      memory_dir: Path.join(state_dir, "memory"),
      approvals_dir: Path.join(state_dir, "approvals"),
      inbox_dir: Path.join(state_dir, "inbox"),
      audit_file: Path.join([state_dir, "audit", "audit.jsonl"]),
      payments_log: Path.join(state_dir, "payments.jsonl")
    }
  end

  defp required(env, name) do
    case Map.get(env, name) do
      value when value in [nil, ""] -> raise "LUNA: the setting #{name} is missing."
      value -> value
    end
  end

  # A secret from $CREDENTIALS_DIRECTORY, or from the environment for a local test.
  defp credential(env, name) do
    from_file =
      with dir when is_binary(dir) <- Map.get(env, "CREDENTIALS_DIRECTORY"),
           {:ok, text} <- File.read(Path.join(dir, name)) do
        String.trim(text)
      else
        _ -> nil
      end

    from_file || Map.get(env, "LUNA_" <> String.upcase(name))
  end

  # The Python core accepted "UTC". tz names it "Etc/UTC".
  defp zone("UTC"), do: "Etc/UTC"
  defp zone(name), do: name

  defp brief_time(text) do
    [h, m] = text |> String.split(":") |> Enum.map(&String.to_integer/1)
    Time.new!(h, m, 0)
  end
end
#+end_src

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/lib/luna_core/main.ex
defmodule LunaCore.Main do
  @moduledoc """
  The supervision tree of the LUNA core: the same parts as main.py of the
  Python core. children/1 is a pure function of the configuration, so a test
  can start the whole core against fake services.
  """

  alias LunaCore.{Agent, Api, Approvals, Audit, Matrix, Memory, Money, Payments, Scheduler, Sysinfo, Telegram, Tools}

  def children(config) do
    audit = config.audit_file
    zone = config.owner_timezone
    today = fn -> DateTime.now!(zone) |> DateTime.to_date() end
    wise = Payments.settings(config.wise_json)
    nudge = fn text -> Telegram.nudge(config.telegram_token, config.telegram_chat_id, text, audit) end

    money =
      if config.money_enabled,
        do: %{root: config.memory_dir, memory: Memory, inbox: config.inbox_dir, audit: audit, today: today},
        else: nil

    payments =
      if wise.mode != "off" do
        %{settings: wise, log: config.payments_log, audit: audit, today: today, now: &DateTime.utc_now/0,
          # In draft mode, LUNA tells the owner in Matrix about each draft.
          notify: fn text -> Matrix.Bot.send_owner(text) end,
          wise: if(config.wise_token, do: [token: config.wise_token] ++ Map.get(config, :wise_opts, []), else: nil)}
      end

    agent_opts = [
      api_key: config.anthropic_api_key, model: config.model, effort: config.effort, zone: zone, audit: audit
    ] ++ Map.get(config, :agent_opts, [])

    roam = %{root: config.memory_dir, memory: Memory, audit: audit, now: fn -> DateTime.now!(zone) end}
    base_tools = Tools.memory_tools(Memory, audit, fn -> DateTime.now!(zone) end) ++ LunaCore.Roam.Tools.tools(roam)
    money_tools = if money, do: Money.tools(money), else: []

    [
      {Memory, root: config.memory_dir, audit: audit, remote: Map.get(config, :remote, true)},
      {Approvals, dir: config.approvals_dir, audit: audit},
      # The chat agent gets the payment tool after the approvals start. Refer to init_payments/1.
      {Agent, agent_opts ++ [tools: base_tools ++ money_tools]},
      Supervisor.child_spec({Task, fn -> init_payments(payments) end}, id: :init_payments),
      {Api,
       socket: config.api_socket,
       core: [
         agent: {Agent, Agent},
         approvals: Approvals,
         audit: audit,
         money: if(money, do: {Money, money}, else: nil),
         settings: %{model: config.model, money: config.money_enabled, wise_mode: wise.mode, wise_sandbox: wise.sandbox},
         memory_root: config.memory_dir,
         roam_root: config.memory_dir,
         started: DateTime.utc_now()
       ]},
      {Matrix.Bot,
       homeserver: config.homeserver, user: config.matrix_user, owner: config.owner, password: config.matrix_password,
       state_dir: config.state_dir, audit: audit, agent: {Agent, Agent}, approvals: Approvals, nudge: nudge},
      {Scheduler.Brief,
       at: config.brief_time, zone: zone, audit: audit,
       # A fresh agent with no payment tool: a scheduled job cannot ask for a
       # tier 2 action when no human is present (backlog #10).
       ask: fn prompt -> brief_ask(agent_opts ++ [tools: base_tools ++ money_tools], prompt) end,
       send: &Matrix.Bot.send_owner/1,
       notice: &Sysinfo.update_notice/1,
       money: if(money, do: fn -> Money.summary(money) end, else: nil)},
      {Scheduler.Sync, memory: Memory, audit: audit}
    ] ++
      if(payments && wise.mode == "draft", do: [{Scheduler.Watch, payments: payments, audit: audit}], else: []) ++
      [Supervisor.child_spec({Task, fn -> Audit.write(audit, "luna.start", %{model: config.model, core: "elixir"}) end}, id: :start_event)]
  end

  defp init_payments(nil), do: :ok

  defp init_payments(payments) do
    Agent.add_tools(Agent, Payments.tools(payments, Approvals))
    Audit.write(payments.audit, "payments.ready", %{mode: payments.settings.mode, sandbox: payments.settings.sandbox,
                                                     payees: payments.settings.payees |> Map.keys() |> Enum.sort()})
  end

  defp brief_ask(opts, prompt) do
    {:ok, pid} = Agent.start_link(Keyword.put(opts, :name, nil))

    try do
      Agent.ask(pid, prompt)
    after
      GenServer.stop(pid)
    end
  end
end
#+end_src

** Application

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/lib/luna_core/application.ex
defmodule LunaCore.Application do
  @moduledoc """
  Starts the LUNA core from the environment. The tests set start_services to
  false and start the parts that they need.
  """
  use Application

  @impl true
  def start(_type, _args) do
    children =
      if Application.get_env(:luna_core, :start_services, true),
        do: LunaCore.Main.children(LunaCore.Config.load()),
        else: []

    # A crash of one part restarts that part only. The Matrix bot and the agent
    # wait 30 s and more between starts, as the Python unit did.
    Supervisor.start_link(children, strategy: :one_for_one, name: LunaCore.Supervisor, max_restarts: 10, max_seconds: 600)
  end
end
#+end_src

** Audit log

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/lib/luna_core/audit.ex
defmodule LunaCore.Audit do
  @moduledoc """
  Writes one JSON line for each event to the append-only audit log.
  The file has the attribute +a, so the core can only add lines.
  """

  @doc "Writes one event. The keys are in sorted order, as in the Python core."
  def write(path, event, fields \\ %{}) when is_binary(event) do
    record =
      fields
      |> Map.new(fn {k, v} -> {to_string(k), json_safe(v)} end)
      |> Map.merge(%{"time" => DateTime.utc_now() |> DateTime.to_iso8601(), "event" => event})
      |> Enum.sort()
      |> Jason.OrderedObject.new()

    line = Jason.encode!(record) <> "\n"
    {:ok, file} = File.open(path, [:append, :binary])

    try do
      :ok = IO.binwrite(file, line)
      :ok = :file.datasync(file)
    after
      File.close(file)
    end
  end

  # The Python core wrote str() of a value that JSON cannot hold.
  defp json_safe(v) when is_binary(v) or is_number(v) or is_boolean(v) or is_nil(v), do: v
  defp json_safe(v) when is_atom(v), do: Atom.to_string(v)
  defp json_safe(v) when is_list(v), do: Enum.map(v, &json_safe/1)
  defp json_safe(%{__struct__: _} = v), do: inspect(v)
  defp json_safe(v) when is_map(v), do: Map.new(v, fn {k, x} -> {to_string(k), json_safe(x)} end)
  defp json_safe(v), do: inspect(v)
end
#+end_src

** Approvals

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/lib/luna_core/approvals.ex
defmodule LunaCore.Approvals do
  @moduledoc """
  Holds tier 2 actions until the owner approves them.

  A tier 2 tool calls request/4. The request goes to disk.
  Only the bot and the core API call approve/2 or deny/2, when the owner acts.
  The model has no path to approve/2 or deny/2.

  The GenServer takes each request from disk before its executor runs.
  Thus two approvals of one request cannot run the executor twice.
  """
  use GenServer

  @ttl_seconds 24 * 60 * 60

  defmodule Request do
    @moduledoc false
    defstruct [:id, :action, :params, :summary, :created]
  end

  def start_link(opts), do: GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, opts, name: Keyword.get(opts, :name, __MODULE__))

  @doc "Registers the executor of an action. The executor takes the params and returns text."
  def register(server \\ __MODULE__, action, executor) when is_function(executor, 1),
    do: GenServer.call(server, {:register, action, executor})

  @doc "Adds a function that gets each new request, for example to send a Matrix message."
  def notify(server \\ __MODULE__, fun) when is_function(fun, 1), do: GenServer.call(server, {:notify, fun})

  def request(server \\ __MODULE__, action, params, summary) do
    case GenServer.call(server, {:request, action, params, summary}) do
      {:ok, item, notifiers} ->
        Enum.each(notifiers, & &1.(item))
        {:ok, item}

      error ->
        error
    end
  end

  def get(server \\ __MODULE__, id), do: GenServer.call(server, {:get, id})
  def pending(server \\ __MODULE__), do: GenServer.call(server, :pending)

  @doc "Approves a request and runs its executor in the process of the caller."
  def approve(server \\ __MODULE__, id) do
    case GenServer.call(server, {:take, id}) do
      {:error, :none} ->
        "There is no request #{id}."

      {:ok, item, executor, audit} ->
        if expired?(item) do
          LunaCore.Audit.write(audit, "approval.expired", %{id: item.id, action: item.action})
          "The request #{item.id} expired. Ask LUNA again."
        else
          LunaCore.Audit.write(audit, "approval.approved", %{id: item.id, action: item.action})
          run(executor, item, audit)
        end
    end
  end

  def deny(server \\ __MODULE__, id) do
    case GenServer.call(server, {:take, id}) do
      {:error, :none} ->
        "There is no request #{id}."

      {:ok, item, _executor, audit} ->
        LunaCore.Audit.write(audit, "approval.denied", %{id: item.id, action: item.action})
        "Denied: #{item.summary}"
    end
  end

  # The owner must see each failure.
  defp run(executor, item, audit) do
    result = executor.(item.params)
    LunaCore.Audit.write(audit, "approval.done", %{id: item.id, action: item.action, result: result})
    result
  rescue
    error ->
      LunaCore.Audit.write(audit, "approval.failed", %{id: item.id, action: item.action, error: inspect(error)})
      "The action #{item.action} failed: #{Exception.message(error)}"
  end

  defp expired?(item) do
    {:ok, created, _} = DateTime.from_iso8601(item.created)
    DateTime.diff(DateTime.utc_now(), created) > @ttl_seconds
  end

  @impl true
  def init(opts) do
    {:ok, %{dir: Keyword.fetch!(opts, :dir), audit: Keyword.fetch!(opts, :audit), executors: %{}, notifiers: []}}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_call({:register, action, executor}, _from, state),
    do: {:reply, :ok, put_in(state.executors[action], executor)}

  def handle_call({:notify, fun}, _from, state), do: {:reply, :ok, %{state | notifiers: state.notifiers ++ [fun]}}

  def handle_call({:request, action, params, summary}, _from, state) do
    if Map.has_key?(state.executors, action) do
      item = %Request{
        id: :crypto.strong_rand_bytes(3) |> Base.encode16(case: :lower),
        action: action,
        params: params,
        summary: summary,
        created: DateTime.utc_now() |> DateTime.to_iso8601()
      }

      File.write!(path(state, item.id), item |> Map.from_struct() |> Jason.encode!())
      LunaCore.Audit.write(state.audit, "approval.requested", %{id: item.id, action: action, params: params})
      {:reply, {:ok, item, state.notifiers}, state}
    else
      {:reply, {:error, "No executor for the action #{action}."}, state}
    end
  end

  def handle_call({:get, id}, _from, state), do: {:reply, read(state, id), state}

  def handle_call(:pending, _from, state) do
    items =
      Path.wildcard(Path.join(state.dir, "*.json"))
      |> Enum.map(&decode(File.read!(&1)))
      |> Enum.sort_by(& &1.created)

    {:reply, items, state}
  end

  def handle_call({:take, id}, _from, state) do
    case read(state, id) do
      nil ->
        {:reply, {:error, :none}, state}

      item ->
        File.rm!(path(state, item.id))
        {:reply, {:ok, item, Map.get(state.executors, item.action), state.audit}, state}
    end
  end

  defp read(state, id) do
    with true <- valid_id?(id),
         {:ok, text} <- File.read(path(state, id)) do
      decode(text)
    else
      _ -> nil
    end
  end

  defp valid_id?(id), do: is_binary(id) and Regex.match?(~r/\A[[:alnum:]]+\z/, id)
  defp path(state, id), do: Path.join(state.dir, id <> ".json")

  defp decode(text) do
    map = Jason.decode!(text)
    %Request{id: map["id"], action: map["action"], params: map["params"], summary: map["summary"], created: map["created"]}
  end
end
#+end_src

** Privacy

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/lib/luna_core/privacy.ex
defmodule LunaCore.Privacy do
  @moduledoc """
  Classifies memory paths, and masks account numbers.

  The taint of a conversation is a value in the agent: nil (clean) or the
  reason. A conversation that read money data cannot use the outbound tools,
  and can write money paths only.
  """

  @sensitive_files ["money.org"]
  @sensitive_dirs ["money/", "briefs/"]

  # Tools that send data out of LUNA. A tainted conversation cannot use them.
  @outbound_tools ["web_search", "web_fetch"]

  def outbound_tools, do: @outbound_tools

  def sensitive?(path) do
    clean = path |> Path.expand("/") |> String.trim_leading("/")
    clean in @sensitive_files or String.starts_with?(clean, @sensitive_dirs)
  end

  @doc "Returns the refusal text when a tainted conversation writes a clean path."
  def write_refusal(nil, _path), do: nil

  def write_refusal(_taint, path) do
    if sensitive?(path) do
      nil
    else
      "Error: this conversation read money data. LUNA can write to " <>
        "money.org, money/ or briefs/ only. Start a new conversation with /new."
    end
  end

  # A run of digits with single spaces or dashes between groups. A letter can
  # touch the run, and the mask still applies. A decimal point or a comma next
  # to the run means an amount, so the mask skips it.
  @digit_run ~r/(?<![\d.,])\d(?:[ -]?\d)*(?!\d|[.,]\d)/

  @doc "Masks account and card numbers. Keeps the last four digits. Use it on description text only."
  def mask_numbers(text, min_digits \\ 6) do
    Regex.replace(@digit_run, text, fn run ->
      digits = String.replace(run, ~r/\D/, "")

      if String.length(digits) < min_digits,
        do: run,
        else: String.duplicate("*", String.length(digits) - 4) <> String.slice(digits, -4, 4)
    end)
  end
end
#+end_src

** System age

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/lib/luna_core/sysinfo.ex
defmodule LunaCore.Sysinfo do
  @moduledoc "Tells the owner when the nixpkgs on luna is old."

  @version_file "/run/current-system/nixos-version"
  @max_age_days 14

  def nixpkgs_date(version) do
    case Regex.run(~r/\.(\d{4})(\d{2})(\d{2})\./, version) do
      [_, y, m, d] -> Date.new!(String.to_integer(y), String.to_integer(m), String.to_integer(d))
      nil -> nil
    end
  end

  @doc "Returns a warning when nixpkgs is older than 14 days, or nil."
  def update_notice(today, version \\ nil), do: notice(today, version || read_version())

  defp notice(_today, :unreadable), do: "System notice: LUNA cannot read the NixOS version."

  defp notice(today, version) do
    case nixpkgs_date(version) do
      nil -> "System notice: LUNA cannot read a date in the NixOS version #{inspect(version)}."
      built -> age_notice(built, Date.diff(today, built))
    end
  end

  defp age_notice(_built, age) when age <= @max_age_days, do: nil

  defp age_notice(built, age) do
    "**System notice:** luna runs nixpkgs from #{Date.to_iso8601(built)}, #{age} days ago. " <>
      "Security fixes are waiting. In nyx, run `nix flake update`, then `make deploy` in luna/."
  end

  defp read_version do
    case File.read(@version_file) do
      {:ok, text} -> String.trim(text)
      {:error, _} -> :unreadable
    end
  end
end
#+end_src

** Memory

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/lib/luna_core/memory.ex
defmodule LunaCore.Memory do
  @moduledoc """
  Reads, searches and appends to the org-mode memory in git.

  One GenServer runs each git operation in turn. Thus the chat and the
  morning brief cannot change the working copy at the same time.
  """
  use GenServer

  @allowed_suffixes [".org", ".journal"]
  @max_read_chars 200_000

  def start_link(opts), do: GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, opts, name: Keyword.get(opts, :name, __MODULE__))

  def files(server \\ __MODULE__), do: GenServer.call(server, :files)
  def read(server \\ __MODULE__, path), do: GenServer.call(server, {:read, path}, :timer.seconds(60))
  def search(server \\ __MODULE__, query, limit \\ 40), do: GenServer.call(server, {:search, query, limit}, :timer.seconds(60))
  def sync(server \\ __MODULE__), do: GenServer.call(server, :sync, :timer.seconds(60))

  def append(server \\ __MODULE__, path, heading, body, now),
    do: GenServer.call(server, {:append, path, heading, body, now}, :timer.seconds(60))

  @doc "Commits the files at the paths, then pushes. Does nothing when no file changed."
  def commit(server \\ __MODULE__, paths, message), do: GenServer.call(server, {:commit, paths, message}, :timer.seconds(60))

  @doc """
  Returns the absolute path for a path relative to the memory root, or an error.
  The path must stay in the root, must not touch .git, must not pass a symlink,
  and must end in .org or .journal.
  """
  def resolve(root, relative) do
    expanded = Path.expand(relative, root)

    cond do
      Path.type(relative) != :relative ->
        {:error, "Use a path that is relative to the memory root."}

      expanded != root and not String.starts_with?(expanded, root <> "/") ->
        {:error, "The path is outside the memory."}

      ".git" in Path.split(Path.relative_to(expanded, root)) or ".git" in Path.split(relative) ->
        {:error, "The path is outside the memory."}

      Path.extname(expanded) not in @allowed_suffixes ->
        {:error, "LUNA can open .org and .journal files only."}

      symlink_on_path?(root, expanded) ->
        {:error, "The path is outside the memory."}

      true ->
        {:ok, expanded}
    end
  end

  defp symlink_on_path?(root, path) do
    path
    |> Path.relative_to(root)
    |> Path.split()
    |> Enum.scan(root, &Path.join(&2, &1))
    |> Enum.any?(fn p -> match?({:ok, %File.Stat{type: :symlink}}, File.lstat(p)) end)
  end

  @impl true
  def init(opts) do
    {:ok,
     %{
       root: opts |> Keyword.fetch!(:root) |> Path.expand(),
       audit: Keyword.fetch!(opts, :audit),
       remote: Keyword.get(opts, :remote, true)
     }}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_call(:files, _from, s), do: {:reply, list_files(s), s}
  def handle_call(:sync, _from, s), do: {:reply, do_sync(s), s}

  def handle_call({:read, relative}, _from, s) do
    reply =
      with :ok <- do_sync(s),
           {:ok, path} <- resolve(s.root, relative),
           {:ok, text} <- read_file(path, relative) do
        if String.length(text) > @max_read_chars,
          do: {:ok, String.slice(text, 0, @max_read_chars) <> "\n[LUNA: the file is longer. The text stops here.]"},
          else: {:ok, text}
      end

    {:reply, reply, s}
  end

  def handle_call({:search, query, limit}, _from, s) do
    reply =
      with :ok <- do_sync(s) do
        needle = String.downcase(query)

        hits =
          list_files(s)
          |> Stream.flat_map(fn rel ->
            Path.join(s.root, rel)
            |> File.read!()
            |> String.split("\n")
            |> Stream.with_index(1)
            |> Stream.filter(fn {line, _} -> String.contains?(String.downcase(line), needle) end)
            |> Stream.map(fn {line, n} -> "#{rel}:#{n}: #{String.trim(line)}" end)
          end)
          |> Enum.take(limit)

        {:ok, hits}
      end

    {:reply, reply, s}
  end

  def handle_call({:append, relative, heading, body, now}, _from, s) do
    reply =
      with :ok <- do_sync(s),
           {:ok, path} <- resolve(s.root, relative) do
        File.mkdir_p!(Path.dirname(path))
        stamp = Calendar.strftime(now, "[%Y-%m-%d %a %H:%M]")
        title = heading |> String.replace("\n", " ") |> String.trim()
        File.write!(path, "\n* #{title}\n:PROPERTIES:\n:CREATED: #{stamp}\n:END:\n#{String.trim_trailing(body)}\n", [:append])
        rel = Path.relative_to(path, s.root)

        with {:ok, _} <- git(s, ["add", "--", rel]),
             {:ok, _} <- git(s, ["commit", "--quiet", "-m", "LUNA: add '#{title}' to #{rel}"]),
             :ok <- push(s) do
          LunaCore.Audit.write(s.audit, "memory.append", %{file: rel, heading: title})
          {:ok, "Added '#{title}' to #{rel}."}
        end
      end

    {:reply, reply, s}
  end

  def handle_call({:commit, paths, message}, _from, s) do
    reply =
      with {:ok, _} <- git(s, ["add", "--" | paths]),
           {:ok, status} <- git(s, ["status", "--porcelain", "--" | paths]) do
        if String.trim(status) == "" do
          :ok
        else
          with {:ok, _} <- git(s, ["commit", "--quiet", "-m", message, "--" | paths]), do: push(s)
        end
      end

    {:reply, reply, s}
  end

  defp list_files(s) do
    Path.wildcard(Path.join(s.root, "**/*"))
    |> Enum.filter(&(Path.extname(&1) in @allowed_suffixes))
    |> Enum.map(&Path.relative_to(&1, s.root))
    |> Enum.reject(&(".git" in Path.split(&1)))
    |> Enum.sort()
  end

  defp read_file(path, relative) do
    case File.read(path) do
      {:ok, text} -> {:ok, text}
      {:error, :enoent} -> {:error, "The file #{relative} does not exist."}
      {:error, reason} -> {:error, "LUNA cannot read #{relative}: #{reason}"}
    end
  end

  defp do_sync(%{remote: false}), do: :ok
  defp do_sync(s), do: with({:ok, _} <- git(s, ["pull", "--rebase", "--quiet"]), do: :ok)

  defp push(%{remote: false}), do: :ok
  defp push(s), do: with({:ok, _} <- git(s, ["push", "--quiet", "origin", "HEAD:main"]), do: :ok)

  defp git(s, [command | _] = args) do
    case System.cmd("git", ["-C", s.root | args], stderr_to_stdout: true) do
      {out, 0} -> {:ok, out}
      {out, _} -> {:error, "git #{command} failed: #{String.trim(out)}"}
    end
  end
end
#+end_src

** Core API server

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/lib/luna_core/api.ex
defmodule LunaCore.Api do
  @moduledoc """
  The core API: chat, approvals, money and system, on a unix socket.
  The Phoenix app is the only client. Refer to "Build: the core API" in LUNA.org.

  The options give the parts of the core:
    * :agent - {module, server}. The module has ask/3 and transcript/1.
    * :approvals - the LunaCore.Approvals server.
    * :audit - the path of the audit log.
    * :money - nil (money is off), or {module, server} with summary/1.
    * :settings - a map for the system panel.
    * :memory_root - the memory working copy, or nil.
    * :started - the start time, as a DateTime.
  """
  use Plug.Router, copy_opts_to_assign: :core

  @image_types ~w(image/jpeg image/png image/webp)
  @max_image_bytes 5 * 1024 * 1024

  plug :match
  plug Plug.Parsers, parsers: [:json], json_decoder: Jason, length: 8 * 1024 * 1024
  plug :dispatch

  get "/api/history" do
    {module, server} = conn.assigns.core[:agent]
    reply(conn, 200, %{messages: module.transcript(server)})
  end

  post "/api/chat" do
    core = conn.assigns.core
    body = conn.body_params
    text = body |> Map.get("text", "") |> to_string() |> String.trim()

    with {:ok, images} <- images(body["image"]),
         :ok <- if(text == "" and images == [], do: {:error, "Write a message."}, else: :ok) do
      voice = body["channel"] == "voice"
      text = if voice, do: "[voice] " <> text, else: text
      LunaCore.Audit.write(core[:audit], "web.chat", %{chars: String.length(text), images: length(images), voice: voice})
      {module, server} = core[:agent]
      prompt = if text == "", do: "What do you see?", else: text
      reply(conn, 200, %{reply: module.ask(server, prompt, images)})
    else
      {:error, message} -> reply(conn, 400, %{error: message})
    end
  end

  get "/api/approvals" do
    items =
      conn.assigns.core[:approvals]
      |> LunaCore.Approvals.pending()
      |> Enum.map(&%{id: &1.id, summary: &1.summary, created: &1.created})

    reply(conn, 200, %{approvals: items})
  end

  post "/api/approvals/:id/:action" do
    core = conn.assigns.core

    result =
      case action do
        "approve" -> {:ok, LunaCore.Approvals.approve(core[:approvals], id)}
        "deny" -> {:ok, LunaCore.Approvals.deny(core[:approvals], id)}
        _ -> :error
      end

    case result do
      {:ok, text} ->
        LunaCore.Audit.write(core[:audit], "web.decision", %{id: id, action: action})
        reply(conn, 200, %{result: text})

      :error ->
        reply(conn, 400, %{error: "Use approve or deny."})
    end
  end

  # The long-term memory graph, for the Memory panel. Titles, tags and links only:
  # the page reads no note text. The text of a money note stays on luna.
  get "/api/memory/graph" do
    case conn.assigns.core[:roam_root] do
      nil -> reply(conn, 200, %{nodes: [], links: []})
      root -> reply(conn, 200, LunaCore.Roam.graph(LunaCore.Roam.index(root)))
    end
  end

  get "/api/money" do
    case conn.assigns.core[:money] do
      nil -> reply(conn, 200, %{text: "Money is off. Refer to 'The gate for phase 2' in LUNA.org."})
      {module, server} -> reply(conn, 200, %{text: module.summary(server)})
    end
  end

  get "/api/system" do
    core = conn.assigns.core

    reply(conn, 200, %{
      started: DateTime.to_iso8601(core[:started]),
      update: LunaCore.Sysinfo.update_notice(Date.utc_today()) || "nixpkgs is less than 14 days old.",
      memory: last_commit(core[:memory_root]),
      settings: core[:settings] || %{},
      pending_approvals: length(LunaCore.Approvals.pending(core[:approvals])),
      events: events(core[:audit])
    })
  end

  match _ do
    reply(conn, 404, %{error: "No such request."})
  end

  defp images(nil), do: {:ok, []}
  defp images(image) when image == %{}, do: {:ok, []}

  defp images(%{} = image) do
    type = to_string(image["media_type"] || "")
    data = to_string(image["data"] || "")

    with :ok <- if(type in @image_types, do: :ok, else: {:error, "LUNA accepts JPEG, PNG or WebP images."}),
         {:ok, bytes} <- decode(data),
         :ok <- if(byte_size(bytes) <= @max_image_bytes, do: :ok, else: {:error, "The image is more than 5 MB."}) do
      {:ok, [{type, data}]}
    end
  end

  defp images(_), do: {:error, "The image is not valid."}

  defp decode(data) do
    case Base.decode64(data) do
      {:ok, bytes} -> {:ok, bytes}
      :error -> {:error, "The image data is not valid base64."}
    end
  end

  defp last_commit(nil), do: ""

  defp last_commit(root) do
    case System.cmd("git", ["-C", root, "log", "-1", "--format=%h %s (%cr)"], stderr_to_stdout: true) do
      {out, 0} -> String.trim(out)
      _ -> ""
    end
  end

  defp events(path) do
    case File.read(path) do
      {:ok, text} ->
        text
        |> String.split("\n", trim: true)
        |> Enum.take(-15)
        |> Enum.map(&Jason.decode!/1)
        |> Enum.map(&%{time: Map.get(&1, "time", ""), event: Map.get(&1, "event", "")})

      {:error, _} ->
        []
    end
  end

  defp reply(conn, status, body) do
    conn
    |> put_resp_content_type("application/json")
    |> send_resp(status, Jason.encode!(body))
  end

  @doc """
  A child spec for Bandit on the unix socket at path. An old socket file goes
  first. Erlang makes the socket with the mode 0700, so start_server/1 sets
  0660 after each start with chmod(1): the group luna-api (luna_web) must connect.
  """
  def child_spec(opts), do: %{id: __MODULE__, start: {__MODULE__, :start_server, [opts]}, type: :supervisor}

  def start_server(opts) do
    path = Keyword.fetch!(opts, :socket)
    File.rm(path)

    with {:ok, pid} <- Bandit.start_link(plug: {__MODULE__, Keyword.fetch!(opts, :core)}, ip: {:local, path}, port: 0, startup_log: false) do
      # Not File.chmod: Erlang also calls chown(2) there, and the seccomp
      # filter of the unit (~@privileged) kills the process for chown.
      {_, 0} = System.cmd("chmod", ["0660", path])
      {:ok, pid}
    end
  end
end
#+end_src

** Claude client

Elixir has no official Anthropic SDK, so the client sends the documented JSON to =POST /v1/messages=.
The client retries 408, 429, 5xx and connection errors two times, as the SDKs do.

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/lib/luna_core/claude.ex
defmodule LunaCore.Claude do
  @moduledoc """
  One request to the Claude Messages API, over HTTP with Req.
  Elixir has no official SDK, so this module sends the documented JSON.
  Refer to "Agent" in LUNA.org.
  """

  @version "2026-10-21"

  @doc """
  Sends the body to POST /v1/messages. Returns {:ok, message} or
  {:error, kind, detail}. kind is :rate_limit, :bad_request, :status or :connection.
  """
  def create(body, opts) do
    request =
      Req.new(
        base_url: Keyword.get(opts, :base_url, "https://api.anthropic.com"),
        url: "/v1/messages",
        json: body,
        headers: [
          {"x-api-key", Keyword.fetch!(opts, :api_key)},
          {"anthropic-version", @version},
          {"anthropic-beta", Enum.join(Keyword.get(opts, :betas, []), ",")}
        ],
        receive_timeout: :timer.minutes(10),
        # Retry 408, 429, 5xx and connection errors, as the SDKs do.
        retry: :transient,
        max_retries: Keyword.get(opts, :max_retries, 2),
