    @moduledoc "Watches the Wise drafts that wait for the approval of Coops in the Wise app. Options: :payments, :audit, :every (ms)."
    use GenServer

    def start_link(opts), do: GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, opts, name: Keyword.get(opts, :name, __MODULE__))

    @impl true
    def init(opts) do
      Process.send_after(self(), :run, Keyword.get(opts, :every, :timer.minutes(5)))
      {:ok, opts}
    end

    @impl true
    def handle_info(:run, opts) do
      try do
        LunaCore.Payments.watch_once(Keyword.fetch!(opts, :payments))
      rescue
        error -> LunaCore.Audit.write(Keyword.fetch!(opts, :audit), "payments.watch_error", %{error: Exception.message(error)})
      end

      Process.send_after(self(), :run, Keyword.get(opts, :every, :timer.minutes(5)))
      {:noreply, opts}
    end
  end
end
#+end_src

** Telegram

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/lib/luna_core/telegram.ex
defmodule LunaCore.Telegram do
  @moduledoc "Sends a short nudge to Telegram. A nudge contains no personal data."

  @api "https://api.telegram.org"

  @doc "Sends the text. Does nothing without a token and a chat ID. An error goes to the audit log."
  def nudge(token, chat, text, audit), do: nudge(token, chat, text, audit, @api)

  def nudge(nil, _chat, _text, _audit, _base_url), do: :ok
  def nudge(_token, nil, _text, _audit, _base_url), do: :ok

  def nudge(token, chat, text, audit, base_url) do
    case Req.post(base_url <> "/bot#{token}/sendMessage",
           json: %{chat_id: chat, text: text},
           retry: false,
           receive_timeout: 15_000
         ) do
      {:ok, %{status: 200}} -> :ok
      {:ok, %{status: status}} -> LunaCore.Audit.write(audit, "telegram.error", %{error: "status #{status}"})
      {:error, exception} -> LunaCore.Audit.write(audit, "telegram.error", %{error: inspect(exception.__struct__)})
    end
  end
end
#+end_src

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/test/support/fake_claude.ex
defmodule LunaCore.FakeClaude do
  @moduledoc """
  A fake Messages API. Each test starts its own. The test gives a list of
  replies: {status, body}. The fake keeps each request body and its headers.
  """
  @behaviour Plug
  import Plug.Conn

  def start(replies) do
    {:ok, state} = Agent.start_link(fn -> %{replies: replies, requests: []} end)
    {:ok, server} = Bandit.start_link(plug: {__MODULE__, state}, ip: {127, 0, 0, 1}, port: 0, startup_log: false)
    {:ok, {_ip, port}} = ThousandIsland.listener_info(server)
    %{state: state, base_url: "http://127.0.0.1:#{port}"}
  end

  def requests(%{state: state}), do: Agent.get(state, &Enum.reverse(&1.requests))

  @impl true
  def init(state), do: state

  @impl true
  def call(%{method: "POST", request_path: "/v1/messages"} = conn, state) do
    {:ok, raw, conn} = read_body(conn, length: 20_000_000)
    headers = Map.new(conn.req_headers)

    {status, body} =
      Agent.get_and_update(state, fn s ->
        {reply, rest} =
          case s.replies do
            [] -> {{500, %{"error" => %{"message" => "no reply left"}}}, []}
            [reply | rest] -> {reply, rest}
          end

        {reply, %{s | replies: rest, requests: [%{body: Jason.decode!(raw), headers: headers} | s.requests]}}
      end)

    conn |> put_resp_content_type("application/json") |> send_resp(status, Jason.encode!(body))
  end

  def call(conn, _state), do: send_resp(conn, 404, "")

  @doc "A message from the API."
  def message(stop_reason, content), do: %{"model" => "claude-opus-5-5", "stop_reason" => stop_reason, "content" => content, "usage" => %{"input_tokens" => 10, "output_tokens" => 5}}
  def text(t), do: %{"type" => "text", "text" => t}
  def tool_use(id, name, input), do: %{"type" => "tool_use", "id" => id, "name" => name, "input" => input}
end
#+end_src

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/test/api_test.exs
defmodule LunaCore.ApiTest do
  use ExUnit.Case, async: true
  import Plug.Test
  import Plug.Conn

  alias LunaCore.{Api, Approvals}

  @moduletag :tmp_dir

  defmodule FakeAgent do
    @moduledoc false
    def ask(test, text, images) do
      send(test, {:asked, text, images})
      "reply: " <> text
    end

    def transcript(_), do: [%{role: "user", text: "hello"}, %{role: "assistant", text: "hi"}]
  end

  setup %{tmp_dir: tmp} do
    dir = Path.join(tmp, "approvals")
    File.mkdir_p!(dir)
    audit = Path.join(tmp, "audit.jsonl")
    File.write!(audit, "")
    {:ok, approvals} = Approvals.start_link(dir: dir, audit: audit, name: nil)
    test = self()
    :ok = Approvals.register(approvals, "test", fn params -> send(test, {:ran, params}) && "done" end)

    core = [agent: {FakeAgent, self()}, approvals: approvals, audit: audit, started: DateTime.utc_now()]
    %{core: core, approvals: approvals, tmp: tmp}
  end

  defp call(core, method, path, body \\ nil) do
    conn = if body, do: conn(method, path, Jason.encode!(body)) |> put_req_header("content-type", "application/json"), else: conn(method, path)
    conn = Api.call(conn, Api.init(core))
    {conn.status, Jason.decode!(conn.resp_body)}
  end

  test "history", %{core: core} do
    {200, body} = call(core, :get, "/api/history")
    assert Enum.at(body["messages"], 1) == %{"role" => "assistant", "text" => "hi"}
  end

  test "chat with an image", %{core: core} do
    data = Base.encode64(<<0xFF, 0xD8>> <> "fake-jpeg")
    image = %{"media_type" => "image/jpeg", "data" => data}
    assert {200, %{"reply" => "reply: what is this?"}} = call(core, :post, "/api/chat", %{text: "what is this?", image: image})
    assert_received {:asked, "what is this?", [{"image/jpeg", ^data}]}
  end

  test "a voice message is marked", %{core: core} do
    assert {200, _} = call(core, :post, "/api/chat", %{text: "next swing?", channel: "voice"})
    assert_received {:asked, "[voice] next swing?", []}
  end

  test "a bad image is refused", %{core: core} do
    assert {400, _} = call(core, :post, "/api/chat", %{text: "x", image: %{media_type: "image/gif", data: "AA=="}})
    assert {400, _} = call(core, :post, "/api/chat", %{text: "x", image: %{media_type: "image/png", data: "not base64!"}})
    assert {400, %{"error" => "Write a message."}} = call(core, :post, "/api/chat", %{text: "  "})
    refute_received {:asked, _, _}
  end

  test "approve", %{core: core, approvals: approvals} do
    {:ok, item} = Approvals.request(approvals, "test", %{"x" => 1}, "Test payment")
    {200, listed} = call(core, :get, "/api/approvals")
    assert hd(listed["approvals"])["summary"] == "Test payment"
    assert {200, %{"result" => "done"}} = call(core, :post, "/api/approvals/#{item.id}/approve", %{})
    assert_received {:ran, %{"x" => 1}}
  end

  test "an unknown action is refused", %{core: core} do
    assert {400, _} = call(core, :post, "/api/approvals/abc/delete", %{})
  end

  test "the memory graph has titles and links, and no note text", %{core: core, tmp: tmp} do
    File.mkdir_p!(Path.join(tmp, "roam"))
    File.write!(Path.join(tmp, "roam/a.org"), ":PROPERTIES:\n:ID: 11111111-2222-4333-8444-555555555555\n:END:\n#+title: A\nSecret text. [[id:22222222-2222-4333-8444-555555555555][B]]\n")
    File.write!(Path.join(tmp, "roam/b.org"), ":PROPERTIES:\n:ID: 22222222-2222-4333-8444-555555555555\n:END:\n#+title: B\n")
    {200, graph} = call(Keyword.put(core, :roam_root, tmp), :get, "/api/memory/graph")
    assert Enum.map(graph["nodes"], & &1["title"]) == ["A", "B"]
    assert graph["links"] == [%{"source" => "11111111-2222-4333-8444-555555555555", "target" => "22222222-2222-4333-8444-555555555555"}]
    refute Jason.encode!(graph) =~ "Secret"
  end

  test "money is off by default", %{core: core} do
    {200, body} = call(core, :get, "/api/money")
    assert body["text"] =~ "Money is off"
  end

  test "system", %{core: core} do
    {200, data} = call(core, :get, "/api/system")
    assert data["pending_approvals"] == 0 and Map.has_key?(data, "update")
  end

  test "serve on a unix socket", %{core: core} do
    # A unix socket path has 107 bytes or less, so the path of tmp_dir is too long.
    path = Path.join(System.tmp_dir!(), "luna-api-#{System.unique_integer([:positive])}.sock")
    on_exit(fn -> File.rm(path) end)
    File.write!(path, "an old socket")
    start_supervised!({Api, socket: path, core: core})
    {:ok, %{status: 200, body: body}} = Req.get("http://core/api/money", unix_socket: path, retry: false)
    assert body["text"] =~ "Money is off"
    assert {:ok, %File.Stat{type: :other, mode: mode}} = File.lstat(path)
    # The group (luna-api) can read and write. Others cannot.
    assert Bitwise.band(mode, 0o777) == 0o660
  end
end
#+end_src

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/test/agent_test.exs
defmodule LunaCore.AgentTest do
  use ExUnit.Case, async: true

  alias LunaCore.{Agent, FakeClaude, Memory, Tools}
  import LunaCore.FakeClaude, only: [message: 2, text: 1, tool_use: 3]

  @moduletag :tmp_dir

  setup %{tmp_dir: tmp} do
    root = Path.join(tmp, "memory")
    File.mkdir_p!(Path.join(root, "money"))
    for {k, v} <- [{"GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL", "/dev/null"}, {"GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM", "1"},
                   {"GIT_AUTHOR_NAME", "t"}, {"GIT_COMMITTER_NAME", "t"},
                   {"GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL", "t@example.com"}, {"GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL", "t@example.com"}],
        do: System.put_env(k, v)
    {_, 0} = System.cmd("git", ["init", "--quiet", root])
    File.write!(Path.join(root, "notes.org"), "* Notes\nBali is cheap in March.\n")
    File.write!(Path.join(root, "money.org"), "* Money\nSafe to spend: 420\n")
    {_, 0} = System.cmd("git", ["-C", root, "add", "-A"])
    {_, 0} = System.cmd("git", ["-C", root, "commit", "--quiet", "-m", "seed"])
    audit = Path.join(tmp, "audit.jsonl")
    {:ok, memory} = Memory.start_link(root: root, audit: audit, remote: false, name: nil)
    tools = Tools.memory_tools(memory, audit, fn -> ~N[2026-11-21 07:00:00] end)
    %{audit: audit, tools: tools, root: root}
  end

  defp start_agent(ctx, replies) do
    fake = FakeClaude.start(replies)

    {:ok, agent} =
      Agent.start_link(api_key: "test-key", audit: ctx.audit, tools: ctx.tools, base_url: fake.base_url,
                       zone: "Australia/Perth", effort: "medium", max_retries: 0, name: nil)

    {agent, fake}
  end

  test "a plain reply, and the shape of the request", ctx do
    {agent, fake} = start_agent(ctx, [{200, message("end_turn", [text("Hi, Coops.")])}])
    assert Agent.ask(agent, "hello") == "Hi, Coops."

    [%{body: body, headers: headers}] = FakeClaude.requests(fake)
    assert headers["anthropic-version"] == "2026-10-21"
    assert headers["x-api-key"] == "test-key"
    assert headers["anthropic-beta"] == "server-side-fallback-2026-07-01,mid-conversation-tool-changes-2026-07-01"
    assert body["model"] == "claude-opus-5-5"
    assert body["fallbacks"] == "default"
    assert body["thinking"] == %{"type" => "adaptive"}
    assert body["output_config"] == %{"effort" => "medium"}
    assert Enum.map(body["tools"], & &1["name"]) == ~w(memory_files memory_search memory_read memory_append web_search web_fetch)
    [%{"role" => "user", "content" => content}] = body["messages"]
    assert content =~ ~r/^\[\w+day 20\d\d-\d\d-\d\d \d\d:\d\d AWST\]\nhello$/
  end

  test "the tool loop runs a tool and sends the result", ctx do
    {agent, fake} =
      start_agent(ctx, [
        {200, message("tool_use", [text("Let me look."), tool_use("toolu_1", "memory_read", %{"path" => "notes.org"})])},
        {200, message("end_turn", [text("Bali is cheap in March.")])}
      ])

    assert Agent.ask(agent, "where is cheap?") == "Bali is cheap in March."
    [_, %{body: second}] = FakeClaude.requests(fake)
    [_, %{"role" => "assistant"}, %{"role" => "user", "content" => [result]}] = second["messages"]
    assert result["tool_use_id"] == "toolu_1"
    assert result["content"] =~ "Bali is cheap"
  end

  test "money data taints the conversation, and the next request removes the web tools", ctx do
    {agent, fake} =
      start_agent(ctx, [
        {200, message("tool_use", [tool_use("toolu_1", "memory_read", %{"path" => "money.org"})])},
        {200, message("end_turn", [text("420.")])},
        {200, message("end_turn", [text("Still no web.")])}
      ])

    assert Agent.ask(agent, "safe to spend?") == "420."
    assert Agent.ask(agent, "and the web?") == "Still no web."
    [_, %{body: second}, %{body: third}] = FakeClaude.requests(fake)
    removals = Enum.filter(second["messages"], &(&1["role"] == "system"))
    assert [%{"content" => blocks}] = removals
    assert Enum.map(blocks, & &1["tool"]["name"]) == ["web_search", "web_fetch"]
    # One removal for the whole conversation.
    assert length(Enum.filter(third["messages"], &(&1["role"] == "system"))) == 1
    assert File.read!(ctx.audit) =~ "agent.tainted"
  end

  test "a tainted conversation cannot write a clean memory file", ctx do
    {agent, _fake} =
      start_agent(ctx, [
        {200, message("tool_use", [tool_use("t1", "memory_read", %{"path" => "money.org"})])},
        {200, message("tool_use", [tool_use("t2", "memory_append", %{"path" => "notes.org", "heading" => "Leak", "body" => "420"})])},
        {200, message("end_turn", [text("I cannot write that.")])}
      ])

    Agent.ask(agent, "copy the money to notes")
    refute File.read!(Path.join(ctx.root, "notes.org")) =~ "Leak"
    assert File.read!(ctx.audit) =~ "tool.refused"
  end

  test "a paused turn goes back without a new user message", ctx do
    paused = message("pause_turn", [%{"type" => "server_tool_use", "id" => "srvtoolu_1", "name" => "web_search", "input" => %{"query" => "x"}}])
    {agent, fake} = start_agent(ctx, [{200, paused}, {200, message("end_turn", [text("Found it.")])}])
    assert Agent.ask(agent, "search") == "Found it."
    [_, %{body: second}] = FakeClaude.requests(fake)
    assert Enum.map(second["messages"], & &1["role"]) == ["user", "assistant"]
  end

  test "the paused turns stop after five restarts", ctx do
    paused = message("pause_turn", [text("Still searching.")])
    {agent, fake} = start_agent(ctx, List.duplicate({200, paused}, 10))
    assert Agent.ask(agent, "search") == "Still searching."
    assert length(FakeClaude.requests(fake)) == 6
  end

  test "an unknown tool gets an error result", ctx do
    {agent, fake} =
      start_agent(ctx, [
        {200, message("tool_use", [tool_use("t1", "send_money", %{})])},
        {200, message("end_turn", [text("I have no such tool.")])}
      ])

    Agent.ask(agent, "pay")
    [_, %{body: second}] = FakeClaude.requests(fake)
    %{"content" => [result]} = List.last(second["messages"])
    assert result["is_error"] == true
  end

  test "the errors from the API become plain replies", ctx do
    {agent, _} = start_agent(ctx, [{429, %{}}])
    assert Agent.ask(agent, "x") =~ "rate limit"

    {agent, _} = start_agent(ctx, [{400, %{"error" => %{"message" => "bad history"}}}])
    assert Agent.ask(agent, "x") =~ "started a new conversation. (bad history)"
    assert Agent.transcript(agent) == []
  end

  test "no credit keeps the conversation", ctx do
    {agent, _} =
      start_agent(ctx, [
        {200, message("end_turn", [text("First.")])},
        {400, %{"error" => %{"message" => "Your credit balance is too low to access the Anthropic API."}}}
      ])

    Agent.ask(agent, "one")
    assert Agent.ask(agent, "two") =~ "no credit"
    assert length(Agent.transcript(agent)) == 3
  end

  test "a refusal and a reply at the length limit", ctx do
    {agent, _} = start_agent(ctx, [{200, message("refusal", [])}, {200, message("max_tokens", [text("Long")])}])
    assert Agent.ask(agent, "x") == "The model declined this request."
    assert Agent.ask(agent, "y") == "Long\n\n[The reply stopped at the length limit.]"
  end

  test "the transcript has the text only, without the time stamp", ctx do
    {agent, _} =
      start_agent(ctx, [
        {200, message("tool_use", [tool_use("t1", "memory_files", %{})])},
        {200, message("end_turn", [%{"type" => "thinking", "thinking" => ""}, text("Two files.")])}
      ])

    Agent.ask(agent, "files?", [{"image/png", "AA=="}])
    assert Agent.transcript(agent) == [%{role: "user", text: "files?\n[image]"}, %{role: "assistant", text: "Two files."}]
  end
end
#+end_src

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/test/support/fake_homeserver.ex
defmodule LunaCore.FakeHomeserver do
  @moduledoc """
  A fake Synapse. Each test starts its own. The test gives the sync bodies in
  order. After the last body, a sync waits 50 ms and returns no events.
  The fake keeps each request: {method, path, body}.
  """
  @behaviour Plug
  import Plug.Conn

  def start(syncs, opts \\ []) do
    {:ok, state} = Agent.start_link(fn -> %{syncs: syncs, requests: [], login_status: Keyword.get(opts, :login_status, 200)} end)
    {:ok, server} = Bandit.start_link(plug: {__MODULE__, state}, ip: {127, 0, 0, 1}, port: 0, startup_log: false)
    {:ok, {_ip, port}} = ThousandIsland.listener_info(server)
    %{state: state, url: "http://127.0.0.1:#{port}"}
  end

  def add_sync(%{state: state}, body), do: Agent.update(state, &%{&1 | syncs: &1.syncs ++ [body]})
  def requests(%{state: state}), do: Agent.get(state, &Enum.reverse(&1.requests))

  @doc "Waits up to 3 s for a request that matches the function."
  def await(fake, fun, tries \\ 60) do
    case Enum.find(requests(fake), fun) do
      nil when tries > 0 -> Process.sleep(50) && await(fake, fun, tries - 1)
      found -> found
    end
  end

  @impl true
  def init(state), do: state

  @impl true
  def call(conn, state) do
    {:ok, raw, conn} = read_body(conn)
    body = if raw == "", do: nil, else: Jason.decode!(raw)
    Agent.update(state, &%{&1 | requests: [{conn.method, conn.request_path, body} | &1.requests]})
    reply(conn, conn.method, conn.path_info, state)
  end

  defp reply(conn, "POST", ["_matrix", "client", "v3", "login"], state) do
    case Agent.get(state, & &1.login_status) do
      200 -> json(conn, 200, %{"user_id" => "@luna:test", "device_id" => "D1", "access_token" => "token-1"})
      status -> json(conn, status, %{"errcode" => "M_FORBIDDEN"})
    end
  end

  defp reply(conn, "GET", ["_matrix", "client", "v3", "sync"], state) do
    case Agent.get_and_update(state, fn s -> {List.first(s.syncs), %{s | syncs: Enum.drop(s.syncs, 1)}} end) do
      nil ->
        Process.sleep(50)
        json(conn, 200, %{"next_batch" => "idle"})

      {:status, status} ->
        json(conn, status, %{"errcode" => "M_UNKNOWN_TOKEN"})

      body ->
        json(conn, 200, body)
    end
  end

  defp reply(conn, "POST", ["_matrix", "client", "v3", "createRoom"], _),
    do: json(conn, 200, %{"room_id" => "!owner:test"})

  defp reply(conn, _method, _path, _state), do: json(conn, 200, %{"event_id" => "$e"})

  defp json(conn, status, body),
    do: conn |> put_resp_content_type("application/json") |> send_resp(status, Jason.encode!(body))
end
#+end_src

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/test/matrix_test.exs
defmodule LunaCore.MatrixTest do
  use ExUnit.Case, async: true

  alias LunaCore.{Approvals, FakeHomeserver}
  alias LunaCore.Matrix.Bot

  @moduletag :tmp_dir
  @owner "@coops:test"

  defmodule FakeAgent do
    @moduledoc false
    def ask(test, text, _images) do
      send(test, {:asked, text})
      "**Reply** to #{text}"
    end

    def reset(test), do: send(test, :reset)
  end

  setup %{tmp_dir: tmp} do
    dir = Path.join(tmp, "approvals")
    File.mkdir_p!(dir)
    audit = Path.join(tmp, "audit.jsonl")
    {:ok, approvals} = Approvals.start_link(dir: dir, audit: audit, name: nil)
    test = self()
    :ok = Approvals.register(approvals, "test", fn _ -> send(test, :ran) && "done" end)
    %{tmp: tmp, audit: audit, approvals: approvals}
  end

  defp start_bot(ctx, syncs, opts \\ []) do
    fake = FakeHomeserver.start(syncs, opts)
    test = self()

    {:ok, bot} =
      Bot.start_link(
        homeserver: fake.url, user: "@luna:test", owner: @owner, password: "pw",
        state_dir: ctx.tmp, audit: ctx.audit, agent: {FakeAgent, test}, approvals: ctx.approvals,
        nudge: fn text -> send(test, {:nudge, text}) end, sync_timeout: 0, name: nil
      )

    {bot, fake}
  end

  defp now, do: System.system_time(:millisecond) + 1_000

  defp message(room, sender, body, ts \\ nil) do
    %{"next_batch" => "b#{System.unique_integer([:positive])}",
      "rooms" => %{"join" => %{room => %{"timeline" => %{"events" => [
        %{"type" => "m.room.message", "sender" => sender, "origin_server_ts" => ts || now(),
          "content" => %{"msgtype" => "m.text", "body" => body}}]}}}}}
  end

  defp sent(fake, room) do
    FakeHomeserver.await(fake, fn {m, p, _} -> m == "PUT" and String.contains?(p, "/rooms/#{URI.encode_www_form(room)}/send/") end)
  end

  test "route sends the commands to code and the rest to the agent" do
    assert Bot.route("approve a1b2c3") == {:approve, "a1b2c3"}
    assert Bot.route("Deny a1b2c3") == {:deny, "a1b2c3"}
    assert Bot.route("pending") == :pending
    assert Bot.route("/new") == :new
    assert Bot.route("approve the plan for March") == {:ask, "approve the plan for March"}
    assert Bot.route("  what is safe to spend?  ") == {:ask, "what is safe to spend?"}
  end

  test "the content has Markdown as HTML" do
    content = Bot.content("**hi**")
    assert content["body"] == "**hi**"
    assert content["formatted_body"] =~ "<strong>hi</strong>"
  end

  test "login, the first sync, and the owner room", ctx do
    {_bot, fake} = start_bot(ctx, [%{"next_batch" => "s1"}])
    assert {"POST", "/_matrix/client/v3/createRoom", body} = FakeHomeserver.await(fake, fn {_, p, _} -> p =~ "createRoom" end)
    assert body["invite"] == [@owner] and body["room_version"] == "11"
    assert File.read!(Path.join(ctx.tmp, "owner-room")) == "!owner:test"
    assert File.read!(Path.join(ctx.tmp, "matrix-session.json")) =~ "token-1"
    assert {:ok, %{mode: mode}} = File.stat(Path.join(ctx.tmp, "matrix-session.json"))
    assert Bitwise.band(mode, 0o077) == 0
  end

  test "an owner message goes to the agent, and the reply comes back as HTML", ctx do
    {_bot, fake} = start_bot(ctx, [%{"next_batch" => "s1"}, message("!owner:test", @owner, "next swing?")])
    assert_receive {:asked, "next swing?"}, 3_000
    {"PUT", _, body} = sent(fake, "!owner:test")
    assert body["formatted_body"] =~ "<strong>Reply</strong>"
  end

  test "a message from another user, or from before the start, does not go to the agent", ctx do
    {_bot, _fake} =
      start_bot(ctx, [
        %{"next_batch" => "s1"},
        message("!owner:test", "@stranger:test", "hello"),
        message("!owner:test", @owner, "old", 1)
      ])

    refute_receive {:asked, _}, 500
    assert File.read!(ctx.audit) =~ "matrix.message_ignored"
  end

  test "approve in Matrix runs the executor in code", ctx do
    {:ok, item} = Approvals.request(ctx.approvals, "test", %{}, "Pay rent")
    {_bot, fake} = start_bot(ctx, [%{"next_batch" => "s1"}, message("!owner:test", @owner, "approve #{item.id}")])
    assert_receive :ran, 3_000
    refute_received {:asked, _}
    {"PUT", _, body} = sent(fake, "!owner:test")
    assert body["body"] == "done"
  end

  test "a new approval request goes to the owner room and to the nudge", ctx do
    {_bot, fake} = start_bot(ctx, [%{"next_batch" => "s1"}])
    FakeHomeserver.await(fake, fn {_, p, _} -> p =~ "createRoom" end)
    Process.sleep(100)
    {:ok, item} = Approvals.request(ctx.approvals, "test", %{}, "Pay rent")
    {"PUT", _, body} = sent(fake, "!owner:test")
    assert body["body"] =~ "approve #{item.id}"
    assert_receive {:nudge, "LUNA needs your approval. Open Matrix."}
  end

  test "an invite from the owner is joined, an invite from another user is not", ctx do
    invite = fn room, sender ->
      %{"invite_state" => %{"events" => [%{"type" => "m.room.member", "state_key" => "@luna:test",
        "sender" => sender, "content" => %{"membership" => "invite"}}]}}
      |> then(&%{"next_batch" => "s1", "rooms" => %{"invite" => %{room => &1}}})
    end

    {_bot, fake} = start_bot(ctx, [invite.("!a:test", @owner), invite.("!b:test", "@stranger:test")])
    assert FakeHomeserver.await(fake, fn {_, p, _} -> p =~ "/join/" end)
    Process.sleep(300)
    joins = for {"POST", p, _} <- FakeHomeserver.requests(fake), p =~ "/join/", do: p
    assert joins == ["/_matrix/client/v3/join/%21a%3Atest"]
  end

  test "the bot does not write to an encrypted room, and tells the owner", ctx do
    encrypted = %{"next_batch" => "s1", "rooms" => %{"join" => %{"!enc:test" => %{
      "state" => %{"events" => [%{"type" => "m.room.encryption", "content" => %{}}]},
      "timeline" => %{"events" => [%{"type" => "m.room.encrypted", "sender" => @owner, "origin_server_ts" => now(), "content" => %{}}]}}}}}
    {_bot, fake} = start_bot(ctx, [%{"next_batch" => "s0"}, encrypted, message("!enc:test", @owner, "hi")])
    {"PUT", _, body} = sent(fake, "!owner:test")
    assert body["body"] =~ "encrypted"
    Process.sleep(300)
    refute Enum.any?(FakeHomeserver.requests(fake), fn {m, p, _} -> m == "PUT" and p =~ "%21enc%3Atest/send" end)
    assert File.read!(ctx.audit) =~ "matrix.send_refused_encrypted"
  end

  test "a bad access token causes one new login", ctx do
    File.write!(Path.join(ctx.tmp, "matrix-session.json"), ~s({"access_token": "old"}))
    {_bot, fake} = start_bot(ctx, [%{"next_batch" => "s1"}, {:status, 401}])
    assert FakeHomeserver.await(fake, fn {m, p, _} -> m == "POST" and p =~ "/login" end)
    assert File.read!(Path.join(ctx.tmp, "matrix-session.json")) =~ "token-1"
  end
end
#+end_src

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/test/html_test.exs
defmodule LunaCore.Matrix.HtmlTest do
  use ExUnit.Case, async: true

  import LunaCore.Matrix.Html, only: [render: 1]

  test "bold, code, emphasis and line breaks" do
    assert render("**Safe:** `420`\nThat is *fine*.") ==
             "<strong>Safe:</strong> <code>420</code><br>That is <em>fine</em>."
  end

  test "a list" do
    assert render("Bills:\n- rent\n- phone") == "Bills:<br><ul><li>rent</li><li>phone</li></ul>"
  end

  test "a code fence keeps its text, escaped" do
    assert render("```sh\nrm -rf <x>\n```") == "<pre><code>rm -rf &lt;x&gt;\n</code></pre>"
  end

  test "HTML from a web page stays text" do
    html = render(~s|<img src=x onerror="alert(1)"> and <script>alert(2)</script>|)
    refute html =~ "<img"
    refute html =~ "<script"
    assert html =~ "&lt;img src=x onerror=&quot;alert(1)&quot;&gt;"
  end

  test "no tag has an attribute" do
    html = render(~s|**[a](javascript:alert(1))** `x" onclick="y`|)
    refute Regex.match?(~r/<[a-z0-9]+\s/, html)
  end
end
#+end_src

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/test/scheduler_test.exs
defmodule LunaCore.SchedulerTest do
  use ExUnit.Case, async: true

  alias LunaCore.Scheduler
  alias LunaCore.Scheduler.{Brief, Sync}

  @moduletag :tmp_dir

  defp perth(naive), do: DateTime.from_naive!(naive, "Australia/Perth")

  test "the next run is today before the time" do
    assert Scheduler.next_run(perth(~N[2026-11-20 06:00:00]), ~T[07:00:00]) == perth(~N[2026-11-20 07:00:00])
  end

  test "the next run is tomorrow after the time" do
    assert Scheduler.next_run(perth(~N[2026-11-20 08:00:00]), ~T[07:00:00]) == perth(~N[2026-11-21 07:00:00])
  end

  test "the next run is tomorrow at the time exactly" do
    assert Scheduler.next_run(perth(~N[2026-11-20 07:00:00]), ~T[07:00:00]) == perth(~N[2026-11-21 07:00:00])
  end

  test "the next run in a zone with daylight saving takes a valid time" do
    # Sydney skips 02:00 to 03:00 on 2026-10-04.
    now = DateTime.from_naive!(~N[2026-10-03 23:00:00], "Australia/Sydney")
    next = Scheduler.next_run(now, ~T[02:30:00])
    assert DateTime.to_date(next) == ~D[2026-10-04]
    assert next.hour == 3
  end

  test "compose puts the notice first and the money last" do
    assert Scheduler.compose("Brief.", "Notice.", "Money.") == "Notice.\n\nBrief.\n\nMoney."
    assert Scheduler.compose("Brief.", nil, nil) == "Brief."
  end

  test "the brief goes to the owner with the system notice", %{tmp_dir: tmp} do
    audit = Path.join(tmp, "audit.jsonl")
    test = self()

    opts = [
      zone: "Australia/Perth", audit: audit,
      ask: fn prompt -> send(test, {:prompt, prompt}) && "Day 3 of 14 on site." end,
      send: fn text -> send(test, {:sent, text}) end,
      notice: fn _date -> "System notice: old nixpkgs." end
    ]

    assert Brief.run_once(opts) == :ok
    assert_received {:prompt, prompt}
    assert prompt =~ "morning brief"
    assert_received {:sent, "System notice: old nixpkgs.\n\nDay 3 of 14 on site."}
    assert File.read!(audit) =~ "brief.sent"
  end

  test "a failed brief goes to the audit log, and run_once does not crash", %{tmp_dir: tmp} do
    audit = Path.join(tmp, "audit.jsonl")
    opts = [audit: audit, ask: fn _ -> raise "API down" end, send: fn _ -> :ok end]
    assert Brief.run_once(opts) == :error
    assert File.read!(audit) =~ "brief.error"
  end

  test "a failed memory sync goes to the audit log", %{tmp_dir: tmp} do
    audit = Path.join(tmp, "audit.jsonl")
    # A working copy with no remote: git pull fails.
    root = Path.join(tmp, "memory")
    {_, 0} = System.cmd("git", ["init", "--quiet", root])
    {:ok, memory} = LunaCore.Memory.start_link(root: root, audit: audit, remote: true, name: nil)
    Sync.run_once(memory: memory, audit: audit)
    assert File.read!(audit) =~ "memory.sync_error"
  end
end
#+end_src

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/test/money_test.exs
defmodule LunaCore.MoneyTest do
  use ExUnit.Case, async: true

  alias LunaCore.{Memory, Money}
  alias LunaCore.Money.{Plan, Rules}

  @moduletag :tmp_dir

  @rules Enum.join([
           "#+TITLE: Categories", "* Rules", "| Pattern | Account |", "|---------+---------|",
           "| woolworths | expenses:food:groceries |", "| transfer to | assets:transfers |",
           "| transfer from | assets:transfers |", "| afterpay | liabilities:afterpay |", ""
         ], "\n")

  @plan Enum.join([
          "* Income", "| Source | Net per pay (AUD) | Pays per year | Net per year |", "| FIFO | 3,000.00 | 26 | |",
          "* Annual costs", "| Category | Item | Per year (AUD) | Due | Bucket |",
          "| Phone | Plan | 600 | Monthly | Bills |", "| Housing | Stays | 15,000 | Each swing | Bills |",
          "* Targets", "| Target | Per year (AUD) |", "| Buffer | 6,000 |", "| Invest | 10,000 |", ""
        ], "\n")

  @files %{
    "commbank--personal.csv" => Enum.join([
      ~s(20/11/2026,"-12.34","WOOLWORTHS 1234 PERTH Card xx4111 1111 1111 1111","+100.00"),
      ~s(21/11/2026,"-500.00","Transfer to xx062000 12345678","-400.00"),
      ~s(21/11/2026,"-9.99","NETFLIX","-409.99")
    ], "\n"),
    "bendigo--savings.csv" => ~s(22/11/2026,"500.00","Transfer from CBA 12345678"\n),
    "up--spending.csv" => "Time,Payee,Description,Total (AUD)\n2026-11-22T09:15:00+08:00,Afterpay,Repayment,-50.00",
    "coopnz--everyday.csv" => "Date,Details,Amount\n19/11/2026,Countdown,-20.50\n",
    "wise--main.csv" => "TransferWise ID,Date,Amount,Currency,Description,Payee Name,Merchant\nTRANSFER-123456789,18-11-2026,-30.00,USD,Card transaction,,GitHub"
  }

  setup %{tmp_dir: tmp} do
    for {k, v} <- [{"GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL", "/dev/null"}, {"GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM", "1"}, {"GIT_AUTHOR_NAME", "t"},
                   {"GIT_COMMITTER_NAME", "t"}, {"GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL", "t@example.com"}, {"GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL", "t@example.com"}],
        do: System.put_env(k, v)

    root = Path.join(tmp, "memory")
    File.mkdir_p!(Path.join(root, "money/tx"))
    File.write!(Path.join(root, "money/main.journal"), "include accounts.journal\ninclude tx/*.journal\n")
    File.write!(Path.join(root, "money/accounts.journal"), "account assets  ; type: A\n")
    File.write!(Path.join(root, "money/tx/opening.journal"), "; Opening balances.\n")
    File.write!(Path.join(root, "money/categories.org"), @rules)
    File.write!(Path.join(root, "money.org"), @plan)
    {_, 0} = System.cmd("git", ["init", "--quiet", root])
    {_, 0} = System.cmd("git", ["-C", root, "add", "-A"])
    {_, 0} = System.cmd("git", ["-C", root, "commit", "--quiet", "-m", "seed"])
    inbox = Path.join(tmp, "inbox")
    File.mkdir_p!(inbox)
    audit = Path.join(tmp, "audit.jsonl")
    {:ok, memory} = Memory.start_link(root: root, audit: audit, remote: false, name: nil)
    cfg = %{root: root, memory: memory, inbox: inbox, audit: audit, today: fn -> ~D[2026-11-23] end}
    %{cfg: cfg, tmp: tmp}
  end

  defp upload(cfg, names), do: for(n <- names, do: File.write!(Path.join(cfg.inbox, n), @files[n]))
  defp journal(cfg), do: File.read!(Path.join(cfg.root, "money/tx/2026.journal"))

  test "import all profiles", %{cfg: cfg} do
    upload(cfg, Map.keys(@files))
    report = Money.import_inbox(cfg)
    refute report =~ "Kept", report
    text = journal(cfg)
    assert text =~ "expenses:food:groceries  AUD 12.34"
    assert text =~ "assets:bank:commbank:personal  AUD -12.34"
    assert text =~ "liabilities:afterpay  AUD 50.00"
    assert text =~ "assets:bank:coopnz:everyday  NZD -20.50"
    assert text =~ "assets:bank:wise:main  USD -30.00"
    assert File.ls!(cfg.inbox) == []
  end

  test "the import masks numbers", %{cfg: cfg} do
    upload(cfg, ["commbank--personal.csv"])
    Money.import_inbox(cfg)
    text = journal(cfg)
    refute text =~ "4111 1111" or text =~ "12345678"
    assert text =~ "************1111"
  end

  test "a second import adds nothing", %{cfg: cfg} do
    upload(cfg, ["commbank--personal.csv"])
    Money.import_inbox(cfg)
    first = journal(cfg)
    upload(cfg, ["commbank--personal.csv"])
    assert Money.import_inbox(cfg) =~ "0 new, 3 already imported"
    assert journal(cfg) == first
  end

  test "the import commits, and never commits a CSV file", %{cfg: cfg} do
    upload(cfg, ["up--spending.csv"])
    Money.import_inbox(cfg)
    {files, 0} = System.cmd("git", ["-C", cfg.root, "ls-files"])
    assert files =~ "money/tx/2026.journal"
    refute files =~ ".csv"
  end

  test "a wrong header keeps the file", %{cfg: cfg} do
    File.write!(Path.join(cfg.inbox, "up--spending.csv"), "Date,Thing,Value\n2026-11-22,x,1\n")
    report = Money.import_inbox(cfg)
    assert report =~ "Kept up--spending.csv" and report =~ "Send LUNA the header row"
    assert File.exists?(Path.join(cfg.inbox, "up--spending.csv"))
  end

  test "a wrong column count keeps the file", %{cfg: cfg} do
    File.write!(Path.join(cfg.inbox, "commbank--personal.csv"), "20/11/2026,-1.00,X\n")
    assert Money.import_inbox(cfg) =~ "expects 4"
  end

  test "a bad file name is refused", %{cfg: cfg} do
    File.write!(Path.join(cfg.inbox, "statement.csv"), "x\n")
    assert Money.import_inbox(cfg) =~ "<profile>--<account>.csv"
  end

  test "transfers cancel", %{cfg: cfg} do
    upload(cfg, ["commbank--personal.csv", "bendigo--savings.csv"])
    Money.import_inbox(cfg)
    refute Money.summary(cfg) =~ "assets:transfers"
  end

  test "the summary", %{cfg: cfg} do
    upload(cfg, Map.keys(@files))
    Money.import_inbox(cfg)
    text = Money.summary(cfg)
    assert text =~ "assets:bank:commbank:personal | AUD -522.33"
    # 3000 * 26 = 78000; minus 15600 costs, 6000 buffer, 10000 invest; / 52
    assert text =~ "Safe to spend per week: AUD 892.31"
    assert text =~ "no category"
  end

  test "unknown lists the masked transactions", %{cfg: cfg} do
    upload(cfg, ["commbank--personal.csv"])
    Money.import_inbox(cfg)
    text = Money.unknown(cfg)
    assert text =~ "NETFLIX"
    refute text =~ "WOOLWORTHS"
  end

  test "the plan reports missing income", %{tmp: tmp} do
    path = Path.join(tmp, "plan.org")
    File.write!(path, "* Income\n| Source | Net per pay (AUD) | Pays per year | Net per year |\n| FIFO | | | |\n")
    plan = Plan.load(path)
    assert Plan.spend_per_week(plan) == nil and plan.missing != []
  end

  for {pattern, account} <- [{"a|b", "expenses:x"}, {"(", "expenses:x"}, {"ok", "Expenses"}] do
    test "the bad rule #{pattern} #{account} is refused", %{tmp: tmp} do
      path = Path.join(tmp, "categories.org")
      File.write!(path, @rules)
      assert {:error, _} = Rules.add(path, unquote(pattern), unquote(account))
    end
  end

  test "an old main.journal is refused", %{cfg: cfg} do
    File.write!(Path.join(cfg.root, "money/main.journal"), "~ every 4 weeks  pay\n    assets:income  AUD 0\n    income:fifo\n")
    upload(cfg, ["commbank--personal.csv"])
    assert Money.import_inbox(cfg) =~ "include tx/*.journal"
    assert File.exists?(Path.join(cfg.inbox, "commbank--personal.csv"))
  end

  test "a new rule applies to the next import", %{cfg: cfg} do
    assert Money.add_rule(cfg, "netflix", "expenses:subscriptions") =~ "Added"
    upload(cfg, ["commbank--personal.csv"])
    Money.import_inbox(cfg)
    assert journal(cfg) =~ "expenses:subscriptions  AUD 9.99"
  end

  test "the IDs are the same as the IDs of the Python core" do
    # Python: sha256("commbank\x1fpersonal\x1f2026-11-21\x1f-9.99\x1fAUD\x1fNETFLIX\x1f1")[:16]
    key = Enum.join(["commbank", "personal", "2026-11-21", "-9.99", "AUD", "NETFLIX", "1"], <<0x1F>>)
    expected = :crypto.hash(:sha256, key) |> Base.encode16(case: :lower) |> binary_part(0, 16)
    {:ok, _, _, txns} = LunaCore.Money.Import.read_file(write_tmp("commbank--personal.csv", @files["commbank--personal.csv"]))
    assert Enum.at(txns, 2).id == expected
  end

  defp write_tmp(name, text) do
    dir = Path.join(System.tmp_dir!(), "luna-money-#{System.unique_integer([:positive])}")
    File.mkdir_p!(dir)
    path = Path.join(dir, name)
    File.write!(path, text)
    path
  end

  test "the money tools mark the taint", %{cfg: cfg} do
    for tool <- Money.tools(cfg), tool.name == "money_summary" do
      assert {:ok, _text, "money_summary"} = tool.run.(%{}, %{taint: nil})
    end
  end
end
#+end_src

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/test/support/fake_wise.ex
defmodule LunaCore.FakeWise do
  @moduledoc "A fake Wise API. Each test starts its own. It keeps each call: {method, path}."
  @behaviour Plug
  import Plug.Conn

  def start do
    {:ok, state} = Agent.start_link(fn -> %{calls: [], status: "incoming_payment_waiting"} end)
    {:ok, server} = Bandit.start_link(plug: {__MODULE__, state}, ip: {127, 0, 0, 1}, port: 0, startup_log: false)
    {:ok, {_ip, port}} = ThousandIsland.listener_info(server)
    %{state: state, url: "http://127.0.0.1:#{port}"}
  end

  def calls(%{state: s}), do: Agent.get(s, &Enum.reverse(&1.calls))
  def set_status(%{state: s}, status), do: Agent.update(s, &%{&1 | status: status})

  @impl true
  def init(state), do: state

  @impl true
  def call(conn, state) do
    {:ok, raw, conn} = read_body(conn)
    Agent.update(state, &%{&1 | calls: [{conn.method, conn.request_path} | &1.calls]})
    body = if raw == "", do: %{}, else: Jason.decode!(raw)

    case {conn.method, conn.path_info} do
      {"GET", ["v1", "profiles"]} -> json(conn, [%{"id" => 7, "type" => "PERSONAL"}])
      {"POST", ["v3", "profiles", _, "quotes"]} -> json(conn, %{"id" => "q-1"})
      {"POST", ["v1", "transfers"]} ->
        if body["targetAccount"] == 42 and body["quoteUuid"] == "q-1",
          do: json(conn, %{"id" => 555, "status" => "incoming_payment_waiting"}),
          else: send_resp(conn, 422, "bad transfer")
      {"GET", ["v1", "transfers", "555"]} -> json(conn, %{"id" => 555, "status" => Agent.get(state, & &1.status)})
      {"PUT", ["v1", "transfers", "555", "cancel"]} -> Agent.update(state, &%{&1 | status: "cancelled"}) && json(conn, %{"status" => "cancelled"})
      {"POST", ["v3", "profiles", _, "transfers", _, "payments"]} -> json(conn, %{"status" => "COMPLETED"})
      _ -> send_resp(conn, 404, "")
    end
  end

  defp json(conn, body), do: conn |> put_resp_content_type("application/json") |> send_resp(200, Jason.encode!(body))
end
#+end_src

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/test/payments_test.exs
defmodule LunaCore.PaymentsTest do
  use ExUnit.Case, async: true

  alias LunaCore.{Approvals, FakeWise, Payments}
  alias LunaCore.Payments.PaymentError

  @moduletag :tmp_dir
  @today ~D[2026-11-23]

  defp settings(mode) do
    Payments.settings(Jason.encode!(%{"mode" => mode, "sandbox" => true, "maxPerPayment" => 100, "maxPerDay" => 150,
      "payees" => %{"landlord" => %{"recipientId" => 42, "currency" => "AUD", "maxPerPayment" => 80}}}))
  end

  defp make(tmp, mode) do
    fake = FakeWise.start()
    {:ok, clock} = Agent.start_link(fn -> ~U[2026-11-23 09:00:00Z] end)
    test = self()

    cfg = %{settings: settings(mode), log: Path.join(tmp, "payments.jsonl"), audit: Path.join(tmp, "audit.jsonl"),
            today: fn -> @today end, now: fn -> Agent.get(clock, & &1) end,
            notify: fn text -> send(test, {:told, text}) end, wise: [token: "token", base_url: fake.url]}

    %{cfg: cfg, fake: fake, clock: clock}
  end

  defp later(clock, hours), do: Agent.update(clock, &DateTime.add(&1, hours * 3600))

  for {payee, amount, currency, message} <- [
        {"stranger", "10", "AUD", "not on the allowlist"},
        {"landlord", "10", "NZD", "takes AUD"},
        {"landlord", "0", "AUD", "more than zero"},
        {"landlord", "90", "AUD", "limit of AUD 80"},
        {"landlord", "abc", "AUD", "not an amount"}
      ] do
    test "the control: #{message}", %{tmp_dir: tmp} do
      %{cfg: cfg} = make(tmp, "draft")
      assert_raise PaymentError, ~r/#{unquote(message)}/, fn ->
        Payments.check(cfg.settings, cfg.log, unquote(payee), unquote(amount), unquote(currency), @today)
      end
    end
  end

  test "off refuses", %{tmp_dir: tmp} do
    %{cfg: cfg} = make(tmp, "off")
    assert_raise PaymentError, ~r/off/, fn -> Payments.check(cfg.settings, cfg.log, "landlord", "10", "AUD", @today) end
  end

  test "a bad mode is refused" do
    assert_raise PaymentError, fn -> Payments.settings(~s({"mode": "yolo"})) end
  end

  test "a dry run calls nothing", %{tmp_dir: tmp} do
    %{cfg: cfg, fake: fake} = make(tmp, "dry-run")
    assert Payments.request(cfg, "landlord", "50", "AUD", "Rent", "k-1") =~ "Dry run"
    assert FakeWise.calls(fake) == []
  end

  test "a draft makes a transfer and never funds it", %{tmp_dir: tmp} do
    %{cfg: cfg, fake: fake} = make(tmp, "draft")
    text = Payments.request(cfg, "landlord", "50", "AUD", "Rent", "k-1")
    assert text =~ "Approve in the Wise app"
    assert_received {:told, ^text}
    calls = FakeWise.calls(fake)
    assert {"POST", "/v1/transfers"} in calls
    refute Enum.any?(calls, fn {_, path} -> String.ends_with?(path, "/payments") end)
  end

  test "drafts count toward the day limit", %{tmp_dir: tmp} do
    %{cfg: cfg} = make(tmp, "draft")
    Payments.request(cfg, "landlord", "80", "AUD", "Rent", "k-1")
    assert_raise PaymentError, ~r/limit per day/, fn -> Payments.check(cfg.settings, cfg.log, "landlord", "80", "AUD", @today) end
  end

  test "the watch reports the funding", %{tmp_dir: tmp} do
    %{cfg: cfg, fake: fake} = make(tmp, "draft")
    Payments.request(cfg, "landlord", "50", "AUD", "Rent", "k-1")
    assert_received {:told, _}
    FakeWise.set_status(fake, "processing")
    Payments.watch_once(cfg)
    assert_received {:told, "You approved" <> _}
    assert Payments.latest(cfg.log)["k-1"]["status"] == "funded"
    Payments.watch_once(cfg)
    refute_received {:told, _}
  end

  test "the watch cancels after 24 hours", %{tmp_dir: tmp} do
    %{cfg: cfg, fake: fake, clock: clock} = make(tmp, "draft")
    Payments.request(cfg, "landlord", "50", "AUD", "Rent", "k-1")
    later(clock, 23)
    Payments.watch_once(cfg)
    refute {"PUT", "/v1/transfers/555/cancel"} in FakeWise.calls(fake)
    later(clock, 2)
    Payments.watch_once(cfg)
    assert {"PUT", "/v1/transfers/555/cancel"} in FakeWise.calls(fake)
    assert_received {:told, "LUNA cancelled" <> _}
    assert Decimal.eq?(Payments.total_for(cfg.log, @today, "AUD"), 0)
  end

  test "the watch sees a cancel in the app", %{tmp_dir: tmp} do
    %{cfg: cfg, fake: fake} = make(tmp, "draft")
    Payments.request(cfg, "landlord", "50", "AUD", "Rent", "k-1")
    FakeWise.set_status(fake, "cancelled")
    Payments.watch_once(cfg)
    assert_received {:told, "You cancelled" <> _}
  end

  test "auto mode needs an approval in Matrix", %{tmp_dir: tmp} do
    %{cfg: cfg, fake: fake} = make(tmp, "auto")
    dir = Path.join(tmp, "approvals")
    File.mkdir_p!(dir)
    {:ok, approvals} = Approvals.start_link(dir: dir, audit: cfg.audit, name: nil)
    [tool] = Payments.tools(cfg, approvals)

    assert {:ok, "Approval request " <> _} =
             tool.run.(%{"payee" => "landlord", "amount" => "50", "currency" => "AUD", "reference" => "Rent", "reason" => "Rent"}, %{taint: nil})

    assert FakeWise.calls(fake) == []
    [item] = Approvals.pending(approvals)
    assert Approvals.approve(approvals, item.id) =~ "Sent AUD 50.00"
    assert {"POST", "/v3/profiles/7/transfers/555/payments"} in FakeWise.calls(fake)
  end

  test "the tool refuses a payment over the limit, and no request goes to Matrix", %{tmp_dir: tmp} do
    %{cfg: cfg, fake: fake} = make(tmp, "auto")
    dir = Path.join(tmp, "approvals")
    File.mkdir_p!(dir)
    {:ok, approvals} = Approvals.start_link(dir: dir, audit: cfg.audit, name: nil)
    [tool] = Payments.tools(cfg, approvals)
    assert {:ok, "Refused: " <> _} = tool.run.(%{"payee" => "landlord", "amount" => "500", "currency" => "AUD", "reference" => "x", "reason" => "x"}, %{taint: nil})
    assert Approvals.pending(approvals) == []
    assert FakeWise.calls(fake) == []
  end

  test "the reference is cleaned" do
    assert Payments.clean_reference("Rent; DROP TABLE <x> 2026-11") == "Rent DROP TABLE x"
  end

  test "the payment key is a version 4 UUID" do
    assert Payments.payment_key() =~ ~r/^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-4[0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/
  end
end
#+end_src

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/test/main_test.exs
defmodule LunaCore.MainTest do
  # The whole core, with the named processes, so this test does not run with others that name them.
  use ExUnit.Case, async: false

  alias LunaCore.{Config, FakeClaude, FakeHomeserver, Main}

  @moduletag :tmp_dir

  test "the configuration from the environment" do
    env = %{"LUNA_HOMESERVER" => "http://hs", "LUNA_MATRIX_USER" => "@luna:x", "LUNA_OWNER" => "@coops:x",
            "LUNA_ANTHROPIC_API_KEY" => "k", "LUNA_MATRIX_PASSWORD" => "p", "LUNA_OWNER_TIMEZONE" => "UTC",
            "LUNA_BRIEF_TIME" => "07:30"}
    config = Config.load(env)
    assert config.owner_timezone == "Etc/UTC"
    assert config.brief_time == ~T[07:30:00]
    assert config.money_enabled == false
    assert config.audit_file == "/var/lib/luna/audit/audit.jsonl"
    assert_raise RuntimeError, ~r/LUNA_OWNER/, fn -> Config.load(Map.delete(env, "LUNA_OWNER")) end
  end

  test "the whole core starts: Matrix, the agent and the core API", %{tmp_dir: tmp} do
    for {k, v} <- [{"GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL", "/dev/null"}, {"GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM", "1"}, {"GIT_AUTHOR_NAME", "t"},
                   {"GIT_COMMITTER_NAME", "t"}, {"GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL", "t@example.com"}, {"GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL", "t@example.com"}],
        do: System.put_env(k, v)

    state = Path.join(tmp, "state")
    for d <- ["approvals", "audit", "inbox", "memory"], do: File.mkdir_p!(Path.join(state, d))
    {_, 0} = System.cmd("git", ["init", "--quiet", Path.join(state, "memory")])
    claude = FakeClaude.start([{200, FakeClaude.message("end_turn", [FakeClaude.text("Hi from Elixir.")])}])
    hs = FakeHomeserver.start([%{"next_batch" => "s1"}])
    socket = Path.join(System.tmp_dir!(), "luna-main-#{System.unique_integer([:positive])}.sock")

    config =
      Config.load(%{"LUNA_STATE_DIR" => state, "LUNA_HOMESERVER" => hs.url, "LUNA_MATRIX_USER" => "@luna:test",
                    "LUNA_OWNER" => "@coops:test", "LUNA_ANTHROPIC_API_KEY" => "k", "LUNA_MATRIX_PASSWORD" => "p",
                    "LUNA_API_SOCKET" => socket})
      |> Map.merge(%{remote: false, agent_opts: [base_url: claude.base_url, max_retries: 0]})

    {:ok, sup} = Supervisor.start_link(Main.children(config), strategy: :one_for_one)
    on_exit(fn -> File.rm(socket) end)

    assert FakeHomeserver.await(hs, fn {_, p, _} -> p =~ "createRoom" end)
    {:ok, %{status: 200, body: body}} = Req.post("http://core/api/chat", json: %{text: "hi"}, unix_socket: socket, retry: false)
    assert body["reply"] == "Hi from Elixir."
    {:ok, %{body: system}} = Req.get("http://core/api/system", unix_socket: socket, retry: false)
    assert system["settings"]["wise_mode"] == "off"
    Supervisor.stop(sup)
    audit = File.read!(config.audit_file)
    assert audit =~ "matrix.ready" and audit =~ ~s("core":"elixir")
  end
end

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/test/approvals_test.exs
defmodule LunaCore.ApprovalsTest do
  use ExUnit.Case, async: true

  alias LunaCore.Approvals

  @moduletag :tmp_dir

  setup %{tmp_dir: tmp} do
    dir = Path.join(tmp, "approvals")
    File.mkdir_p!(dir)
    audit = Path.join(tmp, "audit.jsonl")
    test = self()
    {:ok, server} = Approvals.start_link(dir: dir, audit: audit, name: nil)
    :ok = Approvals.register(server, "test", fn params -> send(test, {:ran, params}) && "done" end)
    %{server: server, dir: dir, audit: audit}
  end

  test "approve runs the executor once", %{server: s} do
    {:ok, item} = Approvals.request(s, "test", %{"x" => 1}, "Test action")
    assert Approvals.approve(s, item.id) == "done"
    assert_received {:ran, %{"x" => 1}}
    assert Approvals.approve(s, item.id) =~ "no request"
    refute_received {:ran, _}
  end

  test "deny does not run the executor", %{server: s} do
    {:ok, item} = Approvals.request(s, "test", %{}, "Test action")
    assert Approvals.deny(s, item.id) =~ "Denied"
    refute_received {:ran, _}
    assert Approvals.pending(s) == []
  end

  test "an expired request does not run", %{server: s, dir: dir} do
    {:ok, item} = Approvals.request(s, "test", %{}, "Old action")
    path = Path.join(dir, item.id <> ".json")
    old = DateTime.utc_now() |> DateTime.add(-2, :day) |> DateTime.to_iso8601()
    File.write!(path, path |> File.read!() |> Jason.decode!() |> Map.put("created", old) |> Jason.encode!())
    assert Approvals.approve(s, item.id) =~ "expired"
    refute_received {:ran, _}
  end

  test "a bad request ID is refused", %{server: s} do
    assert Approvals.approve(s, "../../etc/passwd") =~ "no request"
    refute_received {:ran, _}
  end

  test "an unknown action is refused", %{server: s} do
    assert {:error, _} = Approvals.request(s, "transfer_money", %{}, "No executor")
  end

  test "two approvals at the same time run the executor once", %{server: s} do
    {:ok, item} = Approvals.request(s, "test", %{}, "Race")
    results = 1..5 |> Enum.map(fn _ -> Task.async(fn -> Approvals.approve(s, item.id) end) end) |> Task.await_many()
    assert Enum.count(results, &(&1 == "done")) == 1
  end

  test "the audit log has one sorted JSON line for each event", %{server: s, audit: audit} do
    {:ok, item} = Approvals.request(s, "test", %{}, "Logged")
    Approvals.deny(s, item.id)
    lines = audit |> File.read!() |> String.split("\n", trim: true)
    assert Enum.map(lines, &Jason.decode!(&1)["event"]) == ["approval.requested", "approval.denied"]
    assert hd(lines) =~ ~r/^\{"action":/
  end
end
#+end_src

defmodule LunaCore.PrivacyTest do
  use ExUnit.Case, async: true

  import LunaCore.Privacy

  for path <- ["money.org", "money/main.journal", "./money/x.org", "briefs/2026.org"] do
    test "#{path} is sensitive", do: assert(sensitive?(unquote(path)))
  end

  for path <- ["notes.org", "travel.org", "moneyish.org", "roster.org"] do
    test "#{path} is not sensitive", do: refute(sensitive?(unquote(path)))
  end

  test "a path that climbs out of the memory is not money" do
    refute sensitive?("money/../notes.org")
  end

  test "the taint blocks writes to clean paths only" do
    assert write_refusal(nil, "notes.org") == nil
    taint = "memory_read money.org"
    assert write_refusal(taint, "notes.org") != nil
    assert write_refusal(taint, "money.org") == nil
  end

  for {text, expected} <- [
        {"Transfer to 062000 12345678", "Transfer to **********5678"},
        {"Card 4111 1111 1111 1111 Woolworths", "Card ************1111 Woolworths"},
        {"BSB 062-000", "BSB **2000"},
        {"Coles 1234.56", "Coles 1234.56"},
        {"Rent 12,345.67", "Rent 12,345.67"},
        {"Date 20/11/2026 ref 99", "Date 20/11/2026 ref 99"},
        {"Short 12345", "Short 12345"},
        {"Wise TRANSFER-123456789", "Wise TRANSFER-*****6789"},
        {"Card xx4111 1111 1111 1111", "Card xx************1111"},
        {"ACCT062000123456", "ACCT********3456"},
        {"Store 1234 Perth", "Store 1234 Perth"}
      ] do
    test "mask #{text}", do: assert(mask_numbers(unquote(text)) == unquote(expected))
  end
end
