defmodule LunaCore.MemoryTest do
  use ExUnit.Case, async: true

  alias LunaCore.Memory

  @moduletag :tmp_dir

  setup %{tmp_dir: tmp} do
    root = Path.join(tmp, "memory")
    File.mkdir_p!(root)
    # The git config of the user (commit signing, hooks) must not change a test.
    env = [{"GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL", "/dev/null"}, {"GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM", "1"},
           {"GIT_AUTHOR_NAME", "test"}, {"GIT_COMMITTER_NAME", "test"},
           {"GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL", "test@example.com"}, {"GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL", "test@example.com"}]
    for {k, v} <- env, do: System.put_env(k, v)
    {_, 0} = System.cmd("git", ["init", "--quiet", root])
    File.write!(Path.join(root, "notes.org"), "* Notes\nThe base costs AUD 900 per month.\n")
    {_, 0} = System.cmd("git", ["-C", root, "add", "-A"])
    {_, 0} = System.cmd("git", ["-C", root, "commit", "--quiet", "-m", "seed"])
    {:ok, server} = Memory.start_link(root: root, audit: Path.join(tmp, "audit.jsonl"), remote: false, name: nil)
    %{server: server, root: root, tmp: tmp}
  end

  for path <- ["/etc/passwd", "../outside.org", ".git/config", "notes.txt", "a/../../x.org", "a/../.git/x.org"] do
    test "#{path} is refused", %{root: root} do
      assert {:error, _} = Memory.resolve(root, unquote(path))
    end
  end

  test "a path through a symlink is refused", %{root: root, tmp: tmp} do
    File.mkdir_p!(Path.join(tmp, "outside"))
    File.write!(Path.join(tmp, "outside/secret.org"), "secret")
    File.ln_s!(Path.join(tmp, "outside"), Path.join(root, "link"))
    assert {:error, _} = Memory.resolve(root, "link/secret.org")
  end

  test "the search ignores case", %{server: s} do
    assert Memory.search(s, "BASE COSTS") == {:ok, ["notes.org:2: The base costs AUD 900 per month."]}
  end

  test "append makes a commit", %{server: s, root: root} do
    {:ok, _} = Memory.append(s, "travel.org", "Bali in March", "- Budget: AUD 60 per night", ~N[2026-11-20 07:00:00])
    {:ok, text} = Memory.read(s, "travel.org")
    assert text =~ "* Bali in March"
    assert text =~ ":CREATED: [2026-11-20 Fri 07:00]"
    {log, 0} = System.cmd("git", ["-C", root, "log", "--oneline"])
    assert log =~ "Bali in March"
  end

  test "the file list has org and journal files only", %{server: s, root: root} do
    File.write!(Path.join(root, "x.txt"), "no")
    File.mkdir_p!(Path.join(root, "money"))
    File.write!(Path.join(root, "money/main.journal"), "")
    assert Memory.files(s) == ["money/main.journal", "notes.org"]
  end
end
#+end_src

* Long-term memory

This section is stage M. Coops decided on 2026-11-21:
- *One shared graph.* The org-roam notes of Coops and of LUNA are in =roam/= in the memory hub.
  Emacs on beta uses a clone of the hub as =org-roam-directory=. =org-roam-ui= works on the laptop.
- *Rules, in code:*
  - A note that LUNA made has the property =:CREATED_BY: luna=. LUNA can change it.
  - Each other note belongs to Coops. LUNA can only add a dated section =* LUNA, [date]= at the end.
  - A note with the tag =readonly= does not change (backlog #8).
  - A note with the tag =money= is money data. A read taints the conversation, and a
    tainted conversation writes to money notes only.
- *Two views on the page:* a force graph, and a mind map around one note. Coops can switch.

The notes are normal org-roam files: an =:ID:= property, =#+title=, =#+filetags=, and links =[[id:...][...]]=.
A new LUNA note has the file name =<timestamp>-<slug>.org=, as org-roam makes.
LUNA does not need the SQLite database of org-roam. The core reads the files.

** Parts of stage M

| Part | Result                                                               | State |
|------+----------------------------------------------------------------------+-------|
| M1   | The roam engine and the memory tools in the core. The graph in the core API. | Done |
| M2   | The Memory panel: the force graph and the mind map                    | Done  |
| M3   | Move the notes of Coops to =roam/=. Point Emacs at the clone of the hub. Coops does this. | Next |
| M4   | An overnight job: the conversations of the day become notes and links (backlog #9) | |
| M5   | Semantic recall with a local embedding model (backlog #24)            |       |

** Memory panel

The Memory panel on the page has two views. Our own code draws them on a canvas.
The page loads no graph library.
- *Graph:* a force layout. Notes push apart, and links pull them together.
  A hub (3 links or more) always has a label.
- *Mind map:* one note in the centre. Its links and backlinks are on ring 1.
  Their neighbours are on ring 2.

The colour of a note comes from its first tag. A LUNA note has an accent ring.
Pan with a drag, zoom with the wheel or two fingers, and drag a note in the graph.
A tap on a note in the graph opens the mind map around it. A tap in the mind map
moves the centre to that note. Enter in the search box opens the first match.
The camera fits the notes until you pan or zoom.

The page gets titles, tags and links only. It never gets the text of a note.

** Roam engine

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/lib/luna_core/roam.ex
defmodule LunaCore.Roam do
  @moduledoc """
  The long-term memory: an org-roam graph in roam/ in the memory.
  Coops and LUNA share the graph. Emacs (org-roam) and LUNA read the same files.

  A node is a file or a heading with an :ID: property. A link is
  [[id:UUID]] or [[id:UUID][description]]. The rules (in code, not in the prompt):
    * A note that LUNA made has :CREATED_BY: luna. LUNA can change it.
    * Each other note belongs to Coops. LUNA can only add a section at the end.
    * A note with the tag "readonly" does not change.
    * A note with the tag "money" is money data, for the privacy taint.
  Refer to "Long-term memory" in LUNA.org.
  """

  @dir "roam"
  @link ~r/\[\[id:([0-9A-Fa-f-]{36})(?:\]\[([^\]]*))?\]\]/

  def dir, do: @dir

  @doc "Parses one org file. Returns the nodes: the file node first, then the heading nodes."
  def parse(rel, text) do
    lines = String.split(text, "\n")
    file_tags = file_tags(lines)
    file_title = Enum.find_value(lines, fn l -> match1(~r/^#\+title:\s*(.*)$/i, l) end)
    {file_props, _} = drawer(lines)

    file_node =
      if id = file_props["ID"] do
        [node(id, file_title || Path.basename(rel, ".org"), rel, 0, file_tags, file_props, text)]
      else
        []
      end

    heading_nodes =
      lines
      |> Enum.with_index()
      |> Enum.flat_map(fn {line, i} ->
        case Regex.run(~r/^(\*+)\s+(.*?)(?:\s+(:[\w@#%:]+:))?\s*$/, line) do
          [_, stars | rest] ->
            title = Enum.at(rest, 0)
            tags = rest |> Enum.at(1, "") |> to_string() |> String.split(":", trim: true)
            {props, _} = drawer(Enum.drop(lines, i + 1))

            if id = props["ID"] do
              body = section(lines, i, String.length(stars))
              [node(id, title, rel, i + 1, Enum.uniq(file_tags ++ tags), props, body)]
            else
              []
            end

          nil ->
            []
        end
      end)

    file_node ++ heading_nodes
  end

  defp node(id, title, rel, line, tags, props, body) do
    %{
      id: String.downcase(id),
      title: String.trim(title),
      file: rel,
      line: line,
      tags: tags,
      aliases: aliases(props["ROAM_ALIASES"]),
      owner: if(props["CREATED_BY"] == "luna", do: :luna, else: :coops),
      links: for([_, to | _] <- Regex.scan(@link, body), uniq: true, do: String.downcase(to)),
      body: body
    }
  end

  defp match1(regex, line), do: with([_, v] <- Regex.run(regex, line), do: String.trim(v), else: (_ -> nil))

  defp file_tags(lines) do
    case Enum.find_value(lines, fn l -> match1(~r/^#\+filetags:\s*(.*)$/i, l) end) do
      nil -> []
      tags -> String.split(tags, [":", " "], trim: true)
    end
  end

  # A :PROPERTIES: drawer at the top of the lines (blank lines can come first).
  defp drawer(lines) do
    lines = Enum.drop_while(lines, &(String.trim(&1) == ""))

    case lines do
      [first | rest] ->
        if String.trim(first) == ":PROPERTIES:" do
          {inside, after_} = Enum.split_while(rest, &(String.trim(&1) != ":END:"))

          props =
            for l <- inside, [_, k, v] <- [Regex.run(~r/^\s*:([\w_-]+):\s*(.*?)\s*$/, l)], into: %{},
                do: {String.upcase(k), v}

          {props, Enum.drop(after_, 1)}
        else
          {%{}, lines}
        end

      [] ->
        {%{}, []}
    end
  end

  defp section(lines, i, level) do
    lines
    |> Enum.drop(i)
    |> Enum.with_index()
    |> Enum.take_while(fn {l, n} -> n == 0 or not Regex.match?(~r/^\*{1,#{level}}\s/, l) end)
    |> Enum.map_join("\n", &elem(&1, 0))
  end

  defp aliases(nil), do: []

  defp aliases(text) do
    Regex.scan(~r/"([^"]+)"|(\S+)/, text) |> Enum.map(fn [_, q | r] -> if q != "", do: q, else: List.first(r) end)
  end

  @doc "Every node in roam/ of the memory root, by ID."
  def index(root) do
    base = Path.join(root, @dir)

    Path.wildcard(Path.join(base, "**/*.org"))
    |> Enum.reject(&(".git" in Path.split(&1)))
    |> Enum.flat_map(fn path -> parse(Path.relative_to(path, root), File.read!(path)) end)
    |> Map.new(&{&1.id, &1})
  end

  @doc "The IDs of the nodes that link to the node."
  def backlinks(index, id), do: for({from, n} <- index, id in n.links, do: from)

  def money?(node), do: "money" in node.tags
  def readonly?(node), do: "readonly" in node.tags

  @doc "Finds nodes for a query. Title, alias and tag matches come first, then the text."
  def recall(index, query, limit \\ 10) do
    words = query |> String.downcase() |> String.split(~r/\W+/u, trim: true)

    index
    |> Map.values()
    |> Enum.map(fn n -> {score(n, String.downcase(query), words), n} end)
    |> Enum.filter(fn {s, _} -> s > 0 end)
    |> Enum.sort_by(fn {s, n} -> {-s, n.title} end)
    |> Enum.take(limit)
    |> Enum.map(&elem(&1, 1))
  end

  defp score(n, query, words) do
    title = String.downcase(n.title)
    body = String.downcase(n.body)

    (if title == query, do: 20, else: 0) +
      (if String.contains?(title, query), do: 10, else: 0) +
      Enum.count(n.aliases, &String.contains?(String.downcase(&1), query)) * 8 +
      Enum.count(words, &(&1 in Enum.map(n.tags, fn t -> String.downcase(t) end))) * 5 +
      Enum.sum(for w <- words, do: min(length(String.split(body, w)) - 1, 3))
  end

  @doc "The graph for the page: nodes and links. The money nodes show only the title."
  def graph(index) do
    nodes = for {_, n} <- index, do: %{id: n.id, title: n.title, tags: n.tags, owner: n.owner, file: n.file}
    links = for {from, n} <- index, to <- n.links, Map.has_key?(index, to), do: %{source: from, target: to}
    %{nodes: Enum.sort_by(nodes, & &1.title), links: links}
  end

  @doc "A version 4 UUID, as org-roam makes."
  def uuid do
    <<a::32, b::16, c::16, d::16, e::48>> = :crypto.strong_rand_bytes(16)

    :io_lib.format("~8.16.0b-~4.16.0b-4~3.16.0b-~4.16.0b-~12.16.0b",
      [a, b, Bitwise.band(c, 0x0FFF), Bitwise.bor(Bitwise.band(d, 0x3FFF), 0x8000), e])
    |> IO.iodata_to_binary()
  end

  @doc "The text of a new LUNA note, in the org-roam file format."
  def new_note(id, title, body, tags, now) do
    tag_line = if tags == [], do: "", else: "#+filetags: :" <> Enum.join(tags, ":") <> ":\n"

    ":PROPERTIES:\n:ID:       #{id}\n:CREATED_BY: luna\n:CREATED: #{Calendar.strftime(now, "[%Y-%m-%d %a %H:%M]")}\n:END:\n" <>
      "#+title: #{title}\n" <> tag_line <> "\n" <> String.trim_trailing(body) <> "\n"
  end

  @doc "The file name of a new note: <timestamp>-<slug>.org, as org-roam makes."
  def file_name(title, now) do
    slug = title |> String.downcase() |> String.replace(~r/[^a-z0-9]+/u, "_") |> String.trim("_")
    Calendar.strftime(now, "%Y%m%d%H%M%S") <> "-" <> String.slice(if(slug == "", do: "note", else: slug), 0, 60) <> ".org"
  end

  @doc "An org link to a node."
  def link(node), do: "[[id:#{node.id}][#{node.title}]]"
end
#+end_src

** Memory tools

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/lib/luna_core/roam/tools.ex
defmodule LunaCore.Roam.Tools do
  @moduledoc """
  The long-term memory tools of the agent. The rules of LunaCore.Roam apply
  here, in code: LUNA changes only her own notes, appends to the notes of
  Coops, and changes no readonly note. A money note taints the conversation,
  and a tainted conversation writes to money notes only.
  """

  alias LunaCore.{Audit, Memory, Roam, Tools}

  @doc "cfg: :root, :memory, :audit, :now (a function)."
  def tools(cfg) do
    [
      %{name: "memory_recall",
        description: "Search the long-term memory (the shared org-roam graph) by title, alias, tag and text. Use it before you answer about a person, a place, a project or a past decision.",
        input_schema: Tools.schema(%{"query" => "Words to find."}),
        run: fn %{"query" => q}, _ -> recall(cfg, q) end},
      %{name: "memory_note",
        description: "Read one note of the long-term memory by its ID, with its links and backlinks.",
        input_schema: Tools.schema(%{"id" => "The ID of the note."}),
        run: fn %{"id" => id}, _ -> read(cfg, id) end},
      %{name: "memory_note_create",
        description: "Make a new note in the long-term memory: one note for one person, place, project, idea or decision. Link to other notes with [[id:ID][Title]]. Search first, so you do not make a second note for one thing.",
        input_schema: Tools.schema(%{"title" => "The title.", "body" => "The text, in org-mode.",
                                     "tags" => ~s(Tags, separated by spaces, for example "person fifo". Can be empty.)}),
        run: fn %{"title" => t, "body" => b, "tags" => tags}, ctx -> create(cfg, t, b, String.split(tags), ctx) end},
      %{name: "memory_note_append",
        description: "Add text to the end of a note. On a note of Coops, the text goes in a new dated section. LUNA cannot change the text of Coops.",
        input_schema: Tools.schema(%{"id" => "The ID of the note.", "text" => "The text, in org-mode."}),
        run: fn %{"id" => id, "text" => text}, ctx -> append(cfg, id, text, ctx) end},
      %{name: "memory_link",
        description: "Link one note to another. The link goes at the end of the first note.",
        input_schema: Tools.schema(%{"from_id" => "The note that gets the link.", "to_id" => "The note that the link goes to.",
                                     "why" => ~s(A few words about the relation, for example "works with".)}),
        run: fn %{"from_id" => f, "to_id" => t, "why" => why}, ctx -> link(cfg, f, t, why, ctx) end}
    ]
  end

  defp fresh_index(cfg) do
    _ = Memory.sync(cfg.memory)
    Roam.index(cfg.root)
  end

  defp taint_of(nodes), do: if(Enum.any?(nodes, &Roam.money?/1), do: "memory read a money note")

  defp ok(text, nil), do: {:ok, text}
  defp ok(text, taint), do: {:ok, text, taint}

  def recall(cfg, query) do
    Audit.write(cfg.audit, "tool", %{name: "memory_recall", query: query})
    index = fresh_index(cfg)

    case Roam.recall(index, query) do
      [] ->
        {:ok, "No note matches."}

      hits ->
        text =
          Enum.map_join(hits, "\n", fn n ->
            snippet = n.body |> String.replace(~r/:PROPERTIES:.*?:END:/s, "") |> String.replace(~r/\s+/, " ") |> String.slice(0, 160)
            "- #{Roam.link(n)} (#{Enum.join(n.tags, " ")}; #{length(Roam.backlinks(index, n.id))} backlinks): #{snippet}"
          end)

        ok(text, taint_of(hits))
    end
  end

  def read(cfg, id) do
    Audit.write(cfg.audit, "tool", %{name: "memory_note", id: id})
    index = fresh_index(cfg)

    case Map.get(index, String.downcase(id)) do
      nil ->
        {:ok, "Error: there is no note #{id}."}

      n ->
        out = for to <- n.links, l = index[to], l != nil, do: Roam.link(l)
        back = for from <- Roam.backlinks(index, n.id), do: Roam.link(index[from])

        text =
          "#{n.title} (#{n.file}, owner: #{n.owner}, tags: #{Enum.join(n.tags, " ")})\n\n#{n.body}\n\n" <>
            "Links: #{Enum.join(out, ", ")}\nBacklinks: #{Enum.join(back, ", ")}"

        ok(text, taint_of([n]))
    end
  end

  def create(cfg, title, body, tags, ctx) do
    Audit.write(cfg.audit, "tool", %{name: "memory_note_create", title: title, tags: tags})
    title = title |> String.replace("\n", " ") |> String.trim()
    tags = tags |> Enum.map(&String.replace(&1, ~r/[^\w@]/u, "")) |> Enum.reject(&(&1 == ""))

    cond do
      title == "" -> {:ok, "Error: a note needs a title."}
      ctx.taint != nil and "money" not in tags -> {:ok, refusal()}
      true -> write_new(cfg, title, body, tags)
    end
  end

  defp write_new(cfg, title, body, tags) do
    now = cfg.now.()
    id = Roam.uuid()
    rel = Path.join(Roam.dir(), Roam.file_name(title, now))
    path = Path.join(cfg.root, rel)
    File.mkdir_p!(Path.dirname(path))
    File.write!(path, Roam.new_note(id, title, body, tags, now))

    case Memory.commit(cfg.memory, [rel], "LUNA: add the note '#{title}'") do
      :ok ->
        Audit.write(cfg.audit, "roam.created", %{id: id, file: rel})
        {:ok, "Made the note [[id:#{id}][#{title}]] in #{rel}."}

      {:error, message} ->
        {:ok, "Error: the note is written, but the commit failed: #{message}"}
    end
  end

  def append(cfg, id, text, ctx) do
    Audit.write(cfg.audit, "tool", %{name: "memory_note_append", id: id})

    case writable(fresh_index(cfg), id, ctx) do
      {:ok, n} -> write_end(cfg, n, section(cfg, n, String.trim(text) <> "\n"), "LUNA: add to the note '#{n.title}'")
      {:refused, text} -> {:ok, text}
    end
  end

  def link(cfg, from_id, to_id, why, ctx) do
    Audit.write(cfg.audit, "tool", %{name: "memory_link", from: from_id, to: to_id})
    index = fresh_index(cfg)

    with {:ok, from} <- writable(index, from_id, ctx),
         {:ok, to} <- find(index, to_id) do
      why = why |> String.replace("\n", " ") |> String.trim()
      line = "- " <> if(why == "", do: "", else: why <> ": ") <> Roam.link(to) <> "\n"
      write_end(cfg, from, section(cfg, from, line), "LUNA: link '#{from.title}' to '#{to.title}'")
    else
      {:refused, text} -> {:ok, text}
    end
  end

  # The text of Coops does not change: on a note of Coops, LUNA adds a dated section.
  defp section(_cfg, %{owner: :luna}, text), do: "\n" <> text
  defp section(cfg, %{owner: :coops}, text), do: "\n* LUNA, #{Calendar.strftime(cfg.now.(), "[%Y-%m-%d %a]")}\n" <> text

  defp find(index, id) do
    case Map.get(index, String.downcase(id)) do
      nil -> {:refused, "Error: there is no note #{id}."}
      n -> {:ok, n}
    end
  end

  defp writable(index, id, ctx) do
    with {:ok, n} <- find(index, id) do
      cond do
        Roam.readonly?(n) -> {:refused, "Refused: the note \"#{n.title}\" is readonly."}
        ctx.taint != nil and not Roam.money?(n) -> {:refused, refusal()}
        # The end of the file is the end of a file node only.
        n.line != 0 -> {:refused, "Refused: \"#{n.title}\" is a heading in #{n.file}. Append to the file note."}
        true -> {:ok, n}
      end
    end
  end

  defp write_end(cfg, node, addition, message) do
    path = Path.join(cfg.root, node.file)
    File.write!(path, String.trim_trailing(File.read!(path), "\n") <> "\n" <> addition)

    case Memory.commit(cfg.memory, [node.file], message) do
      :ok -> {:ok, "Done: #{Roam.link(node)}."}
      {:error, m} -> {:ok, "Error: the text is written, but the commit failed: #{m}"}
    end
  end

  defp refusal,
    do: "Refused: this conversation read money data. LUNA can write to notes with the tag money only. Start a new conversation with /new."
end
#+end_src

** Memory tests

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-core-ex/test/roam_test.exs
defmodule LunaCore.RoamTest do
  use ExUnit.Case, async: true

  alias LunaCore.{Memory, Roam}
  alias LunaCore.Roam.Tools

  @moduletag :tmp_dir

  @coops_note """
  :PROPERTIES:
  :ID:       065388ea-0e05-40d5-9844-c968fcae26ea
  :ROAM_ALIASES: "Polaris" pol1
  :END:
  #+title: Polaris1
  #+filetags: :project:

  A FIFO site in the Pilbara. Links to [[id:11111111-2222-4333-8444-555555555555][Perth]].

  * Camp
  :PROPERTIES:
  :ID:       aaaaaaaa-bbbb-4ccc-8ddd-eeeeeeeeeeee
  :END:
  Room 14.
  """

  @perth """
  :PROPERTIES:
  :ID:       11111111-2222-4333-8444-555555555555
  :END:
  #+title: Perth
  #+filetags: :place:readonly:
  The fly-in fly-out airport.
  """

  @money """
  :PROPERTIES:
  :ID:       99999999-2222-4333-8444-555555555555
  :END:
  #+title: Savings plan
  #+filetags: :money:
  Put 10,000 a year in the index fund.
  """

  setup %{tmp_dir: tmp} do
    for {k, v} <- [{"GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL", "/dev/null"}, {"GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM", "1"}, {"GIT_AUTHOR_NAME", "t"},
                   {"GIT_COMMITTER_NAME", "t"}, {"GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL", "t@example.com"}, {"GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL", "t@example.com"}],
        do: System.put_env(k, v)

    root = Path.join(tmp, "memory")
    File.mkdir_p!(Path.join(root, "roam"))
    File.write!(Path.join(root, "roam/20261117200020-polaris1.org"), @coops_note)
    File.write!(Path.join(root, "roam/20261115210335-perth.org"), @perth)
    File.write!(Path.join(root, "roam/20261115210336-savings.org"), @money)
    {_, 0} = System.cmd("git", ["init", "--quiet", root])
    {_, 0} = System.cmd("git", ["-C", root, "add", "-A"])
    {_, 0} = System.cmd("git", ["-C", root, "commit", "--quiet", "-m", "seed"])
    audit = Path.join(tmp, "audit.jsonl")
    {:ok, memory} = Memory.start_link(root: root, audit: audit, remote: false, name: nil)
    cfg = %{root: root, memory: memory, audit: audit, now: fn -> ~N[2026-11-21 09:30:00] end}
    %{cfg: cfg, root: root}
  end

  defp clean, do: %{taint: nil}
  defp file(root, name), do: File.read!(Path.join([root, "roam", name]))

  test "parse finds the file node, the heading node, the aliases, the tags and the links", %{root: root} do
    index = Roam.index(root)
    polaris = index["065388ea-0e05-40d5-9844-c968fcae26ea"]
    assert polaris.title == "Polaris1"
    assert polaris.aliases == ["Polaris", "pol1"]
    assert polaris.tags == ["project"]
    assert polaris.owner == :coops
    assert polaris.links == ["11111111-2222-4333-8444-555555555555"]
    camp = index["aaaaaaaa-bbbb-4ccc-8ddd-eeeeeeeeeeee"]
    assert camp.title == "Camp" and camp.tags == ["project"] and camp.line > 0
    assert Roam.backlinks(index, "11111111-2222-4333-8444-555555555555") |> Enum.member?(polaris.id)
  end

  test "recall puts a title match first", %{root: root} do
    [first | _] = Roam.recall(Roam.index(root), "perth")
    assert first.title == "Perth"
  end

  test "the graph has the nodes and the links", %{root: root} do
    graph = Roam.graph(Roam.index(root))
    assert length(graph.nodes) == 4
    assert %{source: "065388ea-0e05-40d5-9844-c968fcae26ea", target: "11111111-2222-4333-8444-555555555555"} in graph.links
  end

  test "a new LUNA note has the org-roam format, and a commit", %{cfg: cfg, root: root} do
    {:ok, text} = Tools.create(cfg, "Chiang Mai", "A cheap swing place. Near [[id:11111111-2222-4333-8444-555555555555][Perth]].", ["place", "travel"], clean())
    assert text =~ "Made the note [[id:"
    [new] = Path.wildcard(Path.join(root, "roam/20261121093000-chiang_mai.org"))
    note = File.read!(new)
    assert note =~ ~r/^:PROPERTIES:\n:ID:       [0-9a-f-]{36}\n:CREATED_BY: luna\n/
    assert note =~ "#+title: Chiang Mai\n#+filetags: :place:travel:\n"
    {log, 0} = System.cmd("git", ["-C", root, "log", "--oneline"])
    assert log =~ "add the note 'Chiang Mai'"
    assert Roam.index(root) |> Map.values() |> Enum.find(&(&1.title == "Chiang Mai")) |> Map.get(:owner) == :luna
  end

  test "an append to a note of Coops goes in a dated LUNA section, and the old text stays", %{cfg: cfg, root: root} do
    {:ok, "Done:" <> _} = Tools.append(cfg, "065388ea-0e05-40d5-9844-c968fcae26ea", "The next swing starts on 5 December.", clean())
    text = file(root, "20261117200020-polaris1.org")
    assert String.starts_with?(text, String.trim_trailing(@coops_note, "\n"))
    assert text =~ "* LUNA, [2026-11-21 Sat]\nThe next swing starts on 5 December.\n"
  end

  test "a readonly note does not change", %{cfg: cfg, root: root} do
    assert {:ok, "Refused: " <> _} = Tools.append(cfg, "11111111-2222-4333-8444-555555555555", "x", clean())
    assert {:ok, "Refused: " <> _} = Tools.link(cfg, "11111111-2222-4333-8444-555555555555", "065388ea-0e05-40d5-9844-c968fcae26ea", "", clean())
    assert file(root, "20261115210335-perth.org") == @perth
  end

  test "a heading node takes no append", %{cfg: cfg} do
    assert {:ok, "Refused: " <> _} = Tools.append(cfg, "aaaaaaaa-bbbb-4ccc-8ddd-eeeeeeeeeeee", "x", clean())
  end

  test "a link goes at the end of the first note", %{cfg: cfg, root: root} do
    {:ok, _} = Tools.create(cfg, "Dan", "A mate at site.", ["person"], clean())
    dan = Roam.index(root) |> Map.values() |> Enum.find(&(&1.title == "Dan"))
    {:ok, "Done:" <> _} = Tools.link(cfg, dan.id, "065388ea-0e05-40d5-9844-c968fcae26ea", "works at", clean())
    assert Roam.index(root)[dan.id].links == ["065388ea-0e05-40d5-9844-c968fcae26ea"]
  end

  test "a money note taints the conversation", %{cfg: cfg} do
    assert {:ok, _, "memory read a money note"} = Tools.read(cfg, "99999999-2222-4333-8444-555555555555")
    assert {:ok, _, _} = Tools.recall(cfg, "index fund")
    assert {:ok, _} = Tools.read(cfg, "065388ea-0e05-40d5-9844-c968fcae26ea")
  end

  test "a tainted conversation writes to money notes only", %{cfg: cfg, root: root} do
    tainted = %{taint: "memory read a money note"}
    assert {:ok, "Refused: this conversation read money data" <> _} = Tools.create(cfg, "Leak", "10,000", ["travel"], tainted)
    assert {:ok, "Refused: this conversation read money data" <> _} = Tools.append(cfg, "065388ea-0e05-40d5-9844-c968fcae26ea", "10,000", tainted)
    assert {:ok, "Done:" <> _} = Tools.append(cfg, "99999999-2222-4333-8444-555555555555", "Checked.", tainted)
    refute file(root, "20261117200020-polaris1.org") =~ "10,000"
  end

  test "the UUID is version 4", do: assert(Roam.uuid() =~ ~r/^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-4[0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/)
end
#+end_src

* Build: the Phoenix app

This section is stages E1 and E2 of [[*LUNA on Elixir][LUNA on Elixir]].
The Phoenix app =luna-web= is at =https://luna.<tailnet>:8443=.
The page has four panels: Chat, Approvals, Money and System.
Chat accepts a camera image with a message.
Each panel gets its data from [[*Build: the core API][the core API]].

| Tailnet port | Local                     | App                  |
|--------------+---------------------------+----------------------|
| 443          | 127.0.0.1:8008            | Synapse              |
| 8443         | 127.0.0.1:8091            | =luna-web= (Phoenix) |
| none         | =/run/luna-api/api.sock=  | The core API         |

Stage E1 used port 8444 for the Python page. Stage E2 deleted that page.

** Camera

The browser lists each camera that is connected to the computer.
"Show LUNA" takes one frame, and makes the frame 1280 pixels wide or less.
The frame goes with the next message to the Claude API.
LUNA does not keep the image on luna.
When you leave the Chat panel, LUNA forgets the image.

** Safety of the page

The page shows each reply as plain text. HEEx escapes each value.
A reply cannot run a script in the page, even if a web page told the model to write one.
The Content-Security-Policy allows scripts from the app only.

** Login

The login is the same as the login of the first Python page.
The plug =LunaWeb.Owner= accepts a request only when the header =Tailscale-User-Login= is the owner login.
The plug comes before the static files, so each file needs the login.

*CAUTION:* Keep =phoenix_live_view= at 1.2.14 or later.
Earlier versions let a join without ="url"= skip the =on_mount= hooks (EEF-CVE-2026-126334).
=HomeLive.mount/3= checks the owner too, so the check does not depend on the hook only.

Phoenix sends a LiveView socket request to the socket before the endpoint plugs.
Thus the plug cannot check the socket.
The plug writes the login in the signed session cookie.
At each mount, =on_mount/4= compares the session login with the owner login.
=check_origin= refuses a socket from another origin.

** Database and audit log

PostgreSQL 18 listens on the unix socket =/run/postgresql= only.
It has no TCP port.
The only authentication rule is =peer=: the system user =luna_web= is the role =luna_web=.
The app has no database password.

The app runs the migrations at each start, before the endpoint accepts a request.
The table =audit_events= holds the audit events of the app.
A trigger refuses UPDATE, DELETE and TRUNCATE on that table.

*CAUTION:* The trigger stops a fault in the app.
It does not stop an attacker who has the role =luna_web=.
The owner of a table can drop a trigger.

** Secrets

The app needs a secret key base and a release cookie.
These values do not go in sops.
The first start makes the two values with =openssl rand=.
The two values stay in =/var/lib/luna-web=, mode 0700.
To make new values, do these steps on luna:
1. Delete =/var/lib/luna-web/secret_key_base= and =/var/lib/luna-web/cookie=.
2. Run =systemctl restart luna-web=.
A new secret key base stops each open session. Load the page again.

The release has no Erlang distribution (=RELEASE_DISTRIBUTION=none=).
Thus there is no =epmd= and no node port, and the cookie has no use on the network.

** Dependencies

=mix.lock= is not tangled. Mix writes it, as Nix writes =flake.lock=.
=fetchMixDeps= gets the Hex packages in a fixed-output derivation.
Each change to =mix.lock= changes the hash of that derivation.

To change a dependency, do these steps:
1. Edit =deps= in the Mix project below, then tangle.
2. In =nyx/luna=, run =make web-lock=. Mix writes the new =mix.lock=.
3. In the package below, set =hash = lib.fakeHash;=, then tangle.
4. Run =make check=. The build fails and shows the correct hash after =got:=.
5. Write that hash in the package. Tangle again.
6. Run =make check=. The build must pass.

No dependency is for one environment only.
Then one hash serves the release and the tests.
The tests do not use =Phoenix.LiveViewTest=, because it needs a test-only dependency.

The page loads =phoenix.min.js= and =phoenix_live_view.min.js= from the Hex packages.
There is no esbuild, no Tailwind and no npm.

** Package

=mixRelease= builds the release. The check phase runs the tests.
The tests need PostgreSQL, so the check starts a server in the build directory.
That server has a unix socket only.

#+begin_src nix :tangle ../pkgs/luna-web/default.nix
{ lib, beamPackages, postgresql_18 }:

let
  pname = "luna-web";
  version = "0.1.0";
  src = lib.fileset.toSource {
    root = ./.;
    fileset = lib.fileset.unions [ ./mix.exs ./mix.lock ./config ./lib ./priv ./test ];
  };
in
beamPackages.mixRelease {
  inherit pname version src;

  # Change the hash after each change to mix.lock. Refer to "Dependencies"
  # in luna/LUNA.org.
  mixFodDeps = beamPackages.fetchMixDeps {
    pname = "${pname}-deps";
    inherit version src;
    hash = "sha256-4/l12IHvIY9mUvgHOPBlrBy49wSfFYeWneE/KYeOg+Y=";
  };

  # The tests need a PostgreSQL server. The check starts one in the build
  # directory, with a unix socket only.
  doCheck = true;
  nativeCheckInputs = [ postgresql_18 ];
  checkPhase = ''
    runHook preCheck
    export PGDATA=$TMPDIR/pg PGHOST=$TMPDIR/pgsock PGUSER=luna_test
    mkdir -p $PGHOST
    initdb -A trust -U $PGUSER >/dev/null
    pg_ctl -w -o "-k $PGHOST -c listen_addresses=" -l $TMPDIR/pg.log start
    MIX_ENV=test mix test --no-deps-check || { pg_ctl -m fast stop; exit 1; }
    pg_ctl -m fast stop
    runHook postCheck
  '';

  meta = {
    description = "The LUNA web app, in Phoenix LiveView";
    mainProgram = "luna_web";
  };
}
#+end_src

defmodule LunaWeb.MixProject do

  def project do
    [
      app: :luna_web,
      version: "0.1.0",
      elixir: "~> 1.18",
      elixirc_paths: elixirc_paths(Mix.env()),
      start_permanent: Mix.env() == :prod,
      deps: deps(),
      aliases: [test: ["ecto.create --quiet", "test"]],
      releases: [luna_web: [include_executables_for: [:unix]]]
    ]
  end

  def application do
    [mod: {LunaWeb.Application, []}, extra_applications: [:logger]]
  end

  defp elixirc_paths(:test), do: ["lib", "test/support"]
  defp elixirc_paths(_), do: ["lib"]

  # No dependency is for one environment only. Then one fetchMixDeps hash

  defp deps do

** Application

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-web/lib/luna_web/application.ex
defmodule LunaWeb.Application do
  @moduledoc "Starts the database pool, the migrations and the web endpoint."
  use Application

  @impl true
  def start(_type, _args) do
    children = [
      LunaWeb.Repo,
      # Run the migrations before the endpoint accepts a request.
      {Ecto.Migrator, repos: Application.fetch_env!(:luna_web, :ecto_repos)},
      {Phoenix.PubSub, name: LunaWeb.PubSub},
      LunaWeb.Endpoint
    ]

    Supervisor.start_link(children, strategy: :one_for_one, name: LunaWeb.Supervisor)
  end
end

** Core client

=LunaWeb.Core= is the client of [[*Build: the core API][the core API]].
Req sends each request on the unix socket.
The client checks an image and an approval ID before the request.
The core checks them again, because the core is the trust boundary of the agent.

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-web/lib/luna_web/core.ex
defmodule LunaWeb.Core do
  @moduledoc """
  The client of the core API. Refer to "Build: the core API" in LUNA.org.

  The core API is JSON over HTTP on a unix socket. Each function returns
  {:ok, value} or {:error, message}. The message is for the owner.
  """

  @image_types ~w(image/jpeg image/png image/webp)
  @max_image_bytes 5 * 1024 * 1024
  # The agent can use tools for some minutes before it replies.
  @chat_timeout :timer.minutes(10)

  def history do
    with {:ok, %{"messages" => messages}} <- request(:get, "/api/history"), do: {:ok, messages}
  end

  @doc """
  Sends a message and an optional image. The image is checked before the request.
  The channel is "text" or "voice". Refer to the contract in LUNA.org.
  """
  def chat(text, image, channel \\ "text") when is_binary(text) and channel in ["text", "voice"] do
    text = String.trim(text)

    with {:ok, image} <- check_image(image),
         :ok <- check_message(text, image),
         body = %{text: text, channel: channel},
         body = if(image, do: Map.put(body, :image, image), else: body),
         {:ok, %{"reply" => reply}} <-
           request(:post, "/api/chat", json: body, receive_timeout: @chat_timeout) do
      {:ok, reply}
    end
  end

  def approvals do
    with {:ok, %{"approvals" => items}} <- request(:get, "/api/approvals"), do: {:ok, items}
  end

  def decide(id, action) when action in ["approve", "deny"] do
    if Regex.match?(~r/\A[0-9a-f]{1,32}\z/, id) do
      with {:ok, %{"result" => result}} <-
             request(:post, "/api/approvals/#{id}/#{action}", json: %{}),
           do: {:ok, result}
    else
      {:error, "The request ID is not valid."}
    end
  end

  def decide(_id, _action), do: {:error, "Use approve or deny."}

  def money do
    with {:ok, %{"text" => text}} <- request(:get, "/api/money"), do: {:ok, text}
  end

  def system, do: request(:get, "/api/system")

  @doc "The long-term memory graph: titles, tags and links. No note text."
  def memory_graph, do: request(:get, "/api/memory/graph")

  @doc "Checks an image from the page. nil is no image."
  def check_image(nil), do: {:ok, nil}

  def check_image(%{"media_type" => type, "data" => data})
      when type in @image_types and is_binary(data) do
    case Base.decode64(data) do
      {:ok, bytes} when byte_size(bytes) <= @max_image_bytes ->
        {:ok, %{"media_type" => type, "data" => data}}

      {:ok, _} ->
        {:error, "The image is more than 5 MB."}

      :error ->
        {:error, "The image data is not valid base64."}
    end
  end

  def check_image(_), do: {:error, "LUNA accepts JPEG, PNG or WebP images."}

  defp check_message("", nil), do: {:error, "Write a message."}
  defp check_message(_text, _image), do: :ok

  defp request(method, path, opts \\ []) do
    socket = Application.fetch_env!(:luna_web, :core_socket)

    opts =
      Keyword.merge(
        [method: method, url: "http://core" <> path, unix_socket: socket, retry: false],
        opts
      )

    case Req.request(opts) do
      {:ok, %{status: 200, body: body}} when is_map(body) ->
        {:ok, body}

      {:ok, %{body: %{"error" => message}}} when is_binary(message) ->
        {:error, message}

      {:ok, %{status: status}} ->
        {:error, "The core replied with the status #{status}."}

      {:error, _} ->
        {:error, "The LUNA core is not available. Try again in some minutes."}
    end
  end
end
#+end_src

** Voice

A voice turn has five steps:
1. Hold the talk button, or hold Space on the laptop. LUNA plays the "I hear you" earcon.
2. The page sends 16 kHz, 16-bit, mono PCM to the server, in chunks of 200 ms.
3. Release the button. LUNA plays the "I have it" earcon. faster-whisper makes the text.
4. The core gets the text as a voice message. Refer to =channel= in [[*Contract][the contract]].
5. Piper speaks the reply one sentence at a time. The first sentence plays while Piper makes the next.

A press of the talk button stops LUNA at once (barge-in).
Each turn has a number. The page drops the audio of an earlier turn.

The Wyoming servers listen on =127.0.0.1= only. They have no zeroconf.
The site values =voice.sttModel= and =voice.ttsVoice= select the models.

*Voice cannot approve.* A voice turn goes to the chat endpoint only (backlog #5).
The system prompt also tells LUNA that a voice message can never approve a request.
Code makes each approval. Refer to [[*Approvals][Approvals]].

The page plays the voice through Web Audio. An analyser gives the loudness to the presence element.
The page must start the audio context in a user gesture, because iOS requires it.

These backlog items moved out of stage E3:
- #2, fixed phrases in code: the core API has no money or roster endpoint yet. Stage E5.
- #3, the spoken morning brief: it needs the iPhone app. Stage E6.
- #4, the wake word: Coops moved it to the iPhone app. Stage E6.
- #11, voice memos from Matrix: it needs a change to the Python bot. Stage E5.

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-web/lib/luna_web/wyoming.ex
defmodule LunaWeb.Wyoming do
  @moduledoc """
  A client of the Wyoming protocol, for speech to text and text to speech.

  An event is one JSON line, then the optional data bytes, then the optional
  payload bytes. The line gives the lengths in data_length and payload_length.
  Refer to "Voice" in LUNA.org.
  """

  @version "1.11.0"
  @rate 16_000
  @chunk_bytes 6_400
  @timeout :timer.seconds(30)

  @doc "Sends 16 kHz, 16-bit, mono PCM to the STT server. Returns the text."
  def transcribe(pcm, {host, port}) when is_binary(pcm) do
    format = %{"rate" => @rate, "width" => 2, "channels" => 1}

    with {:ok, socket} <- connect(host, port) do
      try do
        :ok = send_event(socket, "transcribe", %{"language" => "en"})
        :ok = send_event(socket, "audio-start", format)

        for <<chunk::binary-size(@chunk_bytes) <- pcm>> do
          :ok = send_event(socket, "audio-chunk", format, chunk)
        end

        rest = binary_part(pcm, div(byte_size(pcm), @chunk_bytes) * @chunk_bytes, rem(byte_size(pcm), @chunk_bytes))
        if rest != "", do: :ok = send_event(socket, "audio-chunk", format, rest)
        :ok = send_event(socket, "audio-stop", %{})

        case read_until(socket, ["transcript"]) do
          {:ok, {"transcript", %{"text" => text}, _}} -> {:ok, String.trim(text)}
          {:error, reason} -> {:error, reason}
        end
      after
        :gen_tcp.close(socket)
      end
    end
  end

  @doc "Sends text to the TTS server. Returns the PCM and its format."
  def synthesize(text, {host, port}) when is_binary(text) do
    with {:ok, socket} <- connect(host, port) do
      try do
        :ok = send_event(socket, "synthesize", %{"text" => text})
        collect(socket, nil, [])
      after
        :gen_tcp.close(socket)
      end
    end
  end

  defp collect(socket, format, chunks) do
    case read_event(socket) do
      {:ok, {"audio-start", data, _}} ->
        collect(socket, data, chunks)

      {:ok, {"audio-chunk", data, payload}} ->
        collect(socket, format || data, [chunks, payload || ""])

      {:ok, {"audio-stop", _, _}} when format != nil ->
        {:ok, %{rate: format["rate"], width: format["width"], channels: format["channels"],
                pcm: IO.iodata_to_binary(chunks)}}

      {:ok, {"error", data, _}} ->
        {:error, Map.get(data, "text", "The voice server sent an error.")}

      {:ok, _other} ->
        collect(socket, format, chunks)

      {:error, reason} ->
        {:error, reason}
    end
  end

  defp read_until(socket, types) do
    case read_event(socket) do
      {:ok, {type, _, _} = event} -> if type in types, do: {:ok, event}, else: read_until(socket, types)
      error -> error
    end
  end

  defp connect(host, port) do
    case :gen_tcp.connect(to_charlist(host), port, [:binary, active: false, packet: :line], @timeout) do
      {:ok, socket} -> {:ok, socket}
      {:error, _} -> {:error, "The voice server is not available."}
    end
  end

  @doc false
  def encode(type, data, payload \\ nil) do
    data_bytes = if data == %{}, do: "", else: Jason.encode!(data)

    header =
      %{"type" => type, "version" => @version}
      |> put_length("data_length", data_bytes)
      |> put_length("payload_length", payload || "")

    [Jason.encode!(header), "\n", data_bytes, payload || ""]
  end

  defp put_length(header, _key, ""), do: header
  defp put_length(header, key, bytes), do: Map.put(header, key, byte_size(bytes))

  defp send_event(socket, type, data, payload \\ nil) do
    case :gen_tcp.send(socket, encode(type, data, payload)) do
      :ok -> :ok
      {:error, _} -> {:error, "The voice server closed the connection."}
    end
  end

  defp read_event(socket) do
    :ok = :inet.setopts(socket, packet: :line)

    with {:ok, line} <- :gen_tcp.recv(socket, 0, @timeout),
         {:ok, header} <- Jason.decode(line),
         :ok <- :inet.setopts(socket, packet: :raw),
         {:ok, data_bytes} <- recv_exact(socket, header["data_length"]),
         {:ok, payload} <- recv_exact(socket, header["payload_length"]) do
      data = Map.merge(header["data"] || %{}, if(data_bytes == "", do: %{}, else: Jason.decode!(data_bytes)))
      {:ok, {header["type"], data, if(payload == "", do: nil, else: payload)}}
    else
      {:error, :timeout} -> {:error, "The voice server did not reply in time."}
      {:error, _} -> {:error, "The voice server sent a bad reply."}
    end
  end

  defp recv_exact(_socket, nil), do: {:ok, ""}
  defp recv_exact(_socket, 0), do: {:ok, ""}
  defp recv_exact(socket, n), do: :gen_tcp.recv(socket, n, @timeout)
end
#+end_src

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-web/lib/luna_web/voice.ex
defmodule LunaWeb.Voice do
  @moduledoc """
  Voice turns: speech to text, the core, then text to speech, one sentence
  at a time. Refer to "Voice" in LUNA.org.

  A voice turn goes to the chat endpoint only. A voice turn can never
  approve a request (backlog #5).
  """

  alias LunaWeb.{Core, Wyoming}

  # 60 seconds of 16 kHz, 16-bit, mono audio.
  @max_pcm_bytes 16_000 * 2 * 60
  # Less than 0.3 seconds of audio is a slip of the finger.
  @min_pcm_bytes 16_000 * 2 * 3 |> div(10)

  def max_pcm_bytes, do: @max_pcm_bytes

  @doc "Speech to text. Returns {:ok, text} or {:error, message}."
  def transcribe(pcm) when byte_size(pcm) < @min_pcm_bytes, do: {:error, "I did not hear anything."}
  def transcribe(pcm) when byte_size(pcm) > @max_pcm_bytes, do: {:error, "Keep a voice message under one minute."}

  def transcribe(pcm) do
    case Wyoming.transcribe(pcm, server(:stt)) do
      {:ok, ""} -> {:error, "I did not hear anything."}
      other -> other
    end
  end

  @doc "Sends the transcript to the core as a voice message."
  def ask(text), do: Core.chat(text, nil, "voice")

  @doc "Text to speech for one sentence."
  def synthesize(sentence), do: Wyoming.synthesize(sentence, server(:tts))

  @doc """
  Makes the text fit for speech, and cuts it into sentences.
  A long first sentence is cut at its first comma, so the first audio comes sooner.
  """
  def sentences(text) do
    text
    |> String.replace(~r/```.*?```/s, " ")
    |> String.split("\n")
    |> Enum.reject(&String.starts_with?(String.trim_leading(&1), "|"))
    |> Enum.map(&String.replace(&1, ~r/^\s*(#+|[-*+]|\d+\.)\s+/, ""))
    |> Enum.join(" ")
    |> String.replace(~r/[*_`#>]/, "")
    |> String.replace(~r/\[([^\]]+)\]\([^)]+\)/, "\\1")
    |> String.replace(~r/\s+/, " ")
    |> String.trim()
    |> String.split(~r/(?<=[.!?])\s+/, trim: true)
    |> split_first()
  end

  defp split_first([first | rest]) when byte_size(first) > 80 do
    case String.split(first, ~r/(?<=,)\s+/, parts: 2) do
      [a, b] -> [a, b | rest]
      _ -> [first | rest]
    end
  end

  defp split_first(list), do: list

  defp server(key) do
    uri = Application.fetch_env!(:luna_web, :voice) |> Keyword.fetch!(key) |> URI.parse()
    {uri.host, uri.port}
  end
end
#+end_src

#+begin_src js :tangle ../pkgs/luna-web/priv/static/mic-worklet.js
// The microphone worklet. It makes 16 kHz, 16-bit, mono PCM from the input,
// and posts one Int16Array each 200 ms. sampleRate is the rate of the context.
class LunaMic extends AudioWorkletProcessor {
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.step = sampleRate / 16000;
    this.sum = 0;
    this.count = 0;
    this.next = this.step;
    this.position = 0;
    this.out = new Int16Array(3200);
    this.length = 0;
  }

  process(inputs) {
    const input = inputs[0] && inputs[0][0];
    if (!input) return true;
    for (let i = 0; i < input.length; i++) {
      // The mean of the input samples for each output sample is a small low-pass filter.
      this.sum += input[i];
      this.count += 1;
      this.position += 1;
      if (this.position >= this.next) {
        const value = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, this.sum / this.count));
        this.out[this.length++] = value < 0 ? value * 0x8000 : value * 0x7fff;
        this.sum = 0;
        this.count = 0;
        this.next += this.step;
        if (this.length === this.out.length) {
          this.port.postMessage(this.out.slice(0));
          this.length = 0;
        }
      }
    }
    return true;
  }
}

registerProcessor("luna-mic", LunaMic);
#+end_src

#+begin_src nix :tangle ../modules/luna/voice.nix
{ lib, site, ... }:
let
  voice = site.voice;
in
{
  # Speech to text. Only 127.0.0.1, so only processes on luna can connect.
  services.wyoming.faster-whisper.servers.luna = {
    enable = true;
    uri = "tcp://127.0.0.1:10300";
    sttLibrary = "faster-whisper";
    model = voice.sttModel;
    language = "en";
    device = "cpu";
    # No mDNS. Zeroconf would advertise the server on the network.
    zeroconf.enable = false;
  };

  # Text to speech. The first start downloads the voice from Hugging Face.
  services.wyoming.piper.servers.luna = {
    enable = true;
    uri = "tcp://127.0.0.1:10200";
    voice = voice.ttsVoice;
    lengthScale = voice.lengthScale;
    zeroconf.enable = false;
  };

  # web.nix gives luna-web the addresses of these two servers.
  systemd.services.luna-web = {
    wants = [ "wyoming-faster-whisper-luna.service" "wyoming-piper-luna.service" ];
    after = [ "wyoming-faster-whisper-luna.service" "wyoming-piper-luna.service" ];
  };
}
#+end_src

** Audit log

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-web/lib/luna_web/audit.ex
defmodule LunaWeb.Audit do
  @moduledoc """
  Writes events to the table audit_events.
  A trigger in the database refuses UPDATE, DELETE and TRUNCATE on that table.
  """
  require Logger

  @doc "Writes one event. A database fault goes to the log. The request continues."
  def write(event, data \\ %{}) when is_binary(event) and is_map(data) do
    LunaWeb.Repo.query!("INSERT INTO audit_events (event, data) VALUES ($1, $2)", [event, data])
    :ok
  rescue
    error ->
      Logger.error("LUNA: the audit log did not write #{event}: #{Exception.message(error)}")
      :error
  end
end
#+end_src

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-web/priv/repo/migrations/20261120000000_create_audit_events.exs
defmodule LunaWeb.Repo.Migrations.CreateAuditEvents do
  use Ecto.Migration

  # The trigger stops a fault in the app. It does not stop an attacker who
  # has the role luna_web, because the owner of a table can drop a trigger.
  def up do
    execute """
    CREATE TABLE audit_events (
      id bigserial PRIMARY KEY,
      time timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
      event text NOT NULL,
      data jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'
    )
    """

    execute """
    CREATE FUNCTION audit_events_refuse() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
    BEGIN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'LUNA: the audit log refuses %', TG_OP;
    END
    $$
    """

    execute """
    CREATE TRIGGER audit_events_no_change BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON audit_events
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION audit_events_refuse()
    """

    execute """
    CREATE TRIGGER audit_events_no_truncate BEFORE TRUNCATE ON audit_events
    FOR EACH STATEMENT EXECUTE FUNCTION audit_events_refuse()
    """
  end

  def down do
    raise "LUNA: the audit log has no down migration."
  end
end
#+end_src

** Owner check

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-web/lib/luna_web/owner.ex
defmodule LunaWeb.Owner do
  @moduledoc """
  Accepts a request only from the owner login.

  tailscale serve adds the header Tailscale-User-Login to each request from
  a tailnet user. The endpoint listens on 127.0.0.1 only, so a request from
  outside luna cannot set the header.

  The LiveView socket does not go through this plug. The plug writes the
  login in the signed session, and on_mount/4 compares the session login
  with the owner login.
  """
  import Plug.Conn

  @header "tailscale-user-login"
  @session_key "owner_login"

  def init(opts), do: opts

  def call(conn, _opts) do
    owner = owner_login()

    case get_req_header(conn, @header) do
      [^owner] ->
        conn
        |> fetch_session()
        |> put_session(@session_key, owner)
        |> assign(:owner_login, owner)

      _ ->
        LunaWeb.Audit.write("web.refused", %{"reason" => "login", "path" => conn.request_path})

        conn
        |> put_resp_content_type("text/plain")
        |> send_resp(403, "LUNA: this page is for the owner only.")
        |> halt()
    end
  end

  def on_mount(:owner, _params, session, socket) do
    owner = owner_login()

    case session do
      %{@session_key => ^owner} ->
        {:cont, Phoenix.Component.assign(socket, :owner_login, owner)}

      _ ->
        LunaWeb.Audit.write("web.refused", %{"reason" => "session", "path" => "/live"})
        {:halt, Phoenix.LiveView.redirect(socket, to: "/")}
    end
  end

  @doc "True when the signed session has the owner login."
  def owner_session?(session), do: Map.get(session, @session_key) == owner_login()

  # An empty owner login would accept an empty header. Refuse to start.
  defp owner_login do
    case Application.fetch_env!(:luna_web, :owner_login) do
      login when is_binary(login) and login != "" -> login
      _ -> raise "LUNA: the owner login is empty."
    end
  end
end
#+end_src

** Endpoint and router

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-web/lib/luna_web/endpoint.ex
defmodule LunaWeb.Endpoint do
  use Phoenix.Endpoint, otp_app: :luna_web

  # tailscale serve gives HTTPS to the browser, so the cookie can be secure.
  @session_options [
    store: :cookie,
    key: "_luna_web",
    signing_salt: "luna-session",
    same_site: "Strict",
    secure: true,
    http_only: true
  ]

  # Phoenix dispatches the socket before the plugs below.
  # LunaWeb.Owner.on_mount/4 checks the owner. check_origin checks the origin.
  socket "/live", Phoenix.LiveView.Socket,
    # A camera frame is 5 MB or less, and base64 makes it 4/3 larger.
    websocket: [connect_info: [session: @session_options], max_frame_size: 8_000_000],
    longpoll: false

  plug Plug.RequestId
  plug Plug.Head
  plug Plug.Session, @session_options
  # The owner check comes before the static files, as in the Python app.
  plug LunaWeb.Owner

  plug Plug.Static, at: "/", from: :luna_web, only: ~w(app.js app.css mic-worklet.js)
  plug Plug.Static, at: "/vendor", from: {:phoenix, "priv/static"}, only: ~w(phoenix.min.js)
  plug Plug.Static, at: "/vendor", from: {:phoenix_live_view, "priv/static"}, only: ~w(phoenix_live_view.min.js)

  plug LunaWeb.Router
end
#+end_src

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-web/lib/luna_web/router.ex
defmodule LunaWeb.Router do
  use Phoenix.Router
  import Plug.Conn
  import Phoenix.Controller
  import Phoenix.LiveView.Router

  # The same policy as the Python app. The page loads scripts from the app only.
  @csp "default-src 'none'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self'; img-src 'self' data: blob:; " <>
         "media-src 'self' blob:; connect-src 'self'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'none'; " <>
         "frame-ancestors 'none'"

  pipeline :browser do
    plug :accepts, ["html"]
    plug :fetch_session
    plug :put_root_layout, html: {LunaWeb.Layouts, :root}
    plug :protect_from_forgery

    plug :put_secure_browser_headers, %{
      "content-security-policy" => @csp,
      "referrer-policy" => "no-referrer",
      "cache-control" => "no-store"
    }
  end

  scope "/", LunaWeb do
    pipe_through :browser

    live_session :owner, on_mount: {LunaWeb.Owner, :owner} do
      live "/", HomeLive
    end
  end
end
#+end_src

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-web/lib/luna_web/error_html.ex

  def render(template, _assigns), do: Phoenix.Controller.status_message_from_template(template)
end

** Page

The LiveView keeps the state of the page on the server.
Two small hooks in =app.js= do the work that needs the browser:
- =Composer= sends the message box. Enter sends, Shift+Enter is a new line.
  The box clears only when the server accepts the message.
- =Camera= opens the camera, takes one frame and shows a preview.

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-web/lib/luna_web/layouts.ex
defmodule LunaWeb.Layouts do
  @moduledoc "The root layout. The page loads no file from the internet."
  use Phoenix.Component

  def root(assigns) do
    ~H"""
    <!DOCTYPE html>
    <html lang="en">
      <head>
        <meta charset="utf-8" />
        <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1" />
        <meta name="csrf-token" content={Phoenix.Controller.get_csrf_token()} />
        <title>LUNA</title>
        <link rel="stylesheet" href="/app.css" />
        <script defer src="/vendor/phoenix.min.js"></script>
        <script defer src="/vendor/phoenix_live_view.min.js"></script>
        <script defer src="/app.js"></script>
      </head>
      <body>
        {@inner_content}
      </body>
    </html>
    """
  end
end
#+end_src

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-web/lib/luna_web/live/home_live.ex
defmodule LunaWeb.HomeLive do
  @moduledoc """
  The page: Chat, Approvals, Money and System. Each panel gets its data
  from the core API through LunaWeb.Core. HEEx shows each value as text.
  """
  use Phoenix.LiveView

  alias LunaWeb.{Core, Voice}

  @panels ~w(chat approvals money memory system)
  # The presence states. "working" comes in stage E4, when the core streams tool events.
  @styles ~w(orb ring)
  @refresh :timer.seconds(30)

  @impl true
  # on_mount checks the owner. mount checks again, because a LiveView bug
  # (EEF-CVE-2026-126334) once let a join skip on_mount.
  def mount(_params, session, socket) do
    if LunaWeb.Owner.owner_session?(session), do: owner_mount(socket), else: {:ok, redirect(socket, to: "/")}
  end

  defp owner_mount(socket) do
    socket =
      assign(socket,
        panel: "chat",
        messages: [],
        waiting: false,
        image: nil,
        approvals: [],
        money: nil,
        system: nil,
        notice: nil,
        presence: "idle",
        presence_style: "orb",
        memory_view: "graph",
        memory_count: 0,
        voice: [],
        voice_bytes: 0,
        turn: 0
      )

    if connected?(socket) do
      LunaWeb.Audit.write("web.connect")
      :timer.send_interval(@refresh, :refresh)
      {:ok, socket |> load_history() |> load_approvals()}
    else
      {:ok, socket}
    end
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_event("show", %{"panel" => panel}, socket) when panel in @panels do
    # The camera preview goes when the chat panel goes. The image goes too.
    socket = assign(socket, panel: panel, notice: nil, image: nil)

    socket =
      case panel do
        "approvals" -> load_approvals(socket)
        "money" -> load(socket, :money, Core.money())
        "system" -> load(socket, :system, Core.system())
        "memory" -> load_memory(socket)
        "chat" -> socket
      end

    {:noreply, socket}
  end

  def handle_event("attach", image, socket) do
    case Core.check_image(image) do
      {:ok, image} -> {:noreply, assign(socket, image: image, notice: nil)}
      {:error, message} -> {:noreply, socket |> assign(image: nil, notice: message) |> push_event("image-cleared", %{})}
    end
  end

  def handle_event("detach", _params, socket), do: {:noreply, assign(socket, image: nil)}

  def handle_event("camera-error", %{"message" => message}, socket) when is_binary(message) do
    {:noreply, assign(socket, notice: "Camera error: " <> String.slice(message, 0, 200))}
  end

  # The reply tells the page to clear the message box. "sent: false" keeps the text.
  def handle_event("send", _params, %{assigns: %{waiting: true}} = socket) do
    {:reply, %{sent: false}, assign(socket, notice: "Wait for the reply to the last message.")}
  end

  def handle_event("send", %{"text" => text}, socket) when is_binary(text) do
    image = socket.assigns.image

    case Core.check_image(image) do
      {:ok, _} when text == "" and is_nil(image) ->
        {:reply, %{sent: false}, socket}

      {:ok, _} ->
        shown = if image, do: String.trim(text) <> "\n[image]", else: String.trim(text)

        socket =
          socket
          |> assign(waiting: true, image: nil, notice: nil)
          |> add_message("user", shown)
          |> push_event("image-cleared", %{})
          |> start_async(:chat, fn -> Core.chat(text, image) end)

        {:reply, %{sent: true}, socket}

      {:error, message} ->
        {:reply, %{sent: false}, assign(socket, notice: message)}
    end
  end

  # Voice. The page sends 16 kHz, 16-bit, mono PCM in base64 chunks while the
  # button is down. A press also stops the voice of LUNA (barge-in).
  def handle_event("voice-start", _params, socket) do
    socket =
      socket
      |> cancel_async(:speak)
      |> assign(presence: "listening", voice: [], voice_bytes: 0, turn: socket.assigns.turn + 1, notice: nil)

    {:noreply, socket}
  end

  def handle_event("voice-chunk", %{"data" => data}, %{assigns: %{presence: "listening"}} = socket)
      when is_binary(data) do
    max = Voice.max_pcm_bytes()

    with {:ok, pcm} <- Base.decode64(data),
         bytes when bytes <= max <- socket.assigns.voice_bytes + byte_size(pcm) do
      {:noreply, assign(socket, voice: [socket.assigns.voice, pcm], voice_bytes: bytes)}
    else
      _ -> {:noreply, socket}
    end
  end

  def handle_event("voice-chunk", _params, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}

  def handle_event("voice-stop", _params, %{assigns: %{presence: "listening"}} = socket) do
    pcm = IO.iodata_to_binary(socket.assigns.voice)

    socket =
      socket
      |> assign(presence: "thinking", voice: [], voice_bytes: 0)
      |> start_async(:transcribe, fn -> Voice.transcribe(pcm) end)

    {:noreply, socket}
  end

  def handle_event("voice-stop", _params, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}

  # The page tells the server when the last audio stops.
  def handle_event("voice-done", %{"turn" => turn}, %{assigns: %{turn: turn, presence: "speaking"}} = socket) do
    {:noreply, assign(socket, presence: "idle")}
  end

  def handle_event("voice-done", _params, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}

  def handle_event("presence-style", %{"style" => style}, socket) when style in @styles do
    {:noreply, assign(socket, presence_style: style)}
  end

  def handle_event("presence-style", _params, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}

  # The page draws the graph. The view is "graph" (force) or "map" (mind map).
  def handle_event("memory-view", %{"view" => view}, socket) when view in ["graph", "map"] do
    {:noreply, socket |> assign(memory_view: view) |> push_event("memory-view", %{view: view})}
  end

  def handle_event("memory-view", _params, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}

  # A tap on a note in the graph opens the mind map around it.
  def handle_event("memory-focus", %{"id" => id}, socket) when is_binary(id) do
    {:noreply, socket |> assign(memory_view: "map") |> push_event("memory-view", %{view: "map", focus: id})}
  end

  def handle_event("decide", %{"id" => id, "action" => action}, socket) do
    notice =
      case Core.decide(id, action) do
        {:ok, result} -> result
        {:error, message} -> message
      end

    {:noreply, socket |> assign(notice: notice) |> load_approvals()}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_async(:transcribe, {:ok, {:ok, text}}, socket) do
    socket =
      socket
      |> add_message("user", "[voice] " <> text)
      |> start_async(:voice_chat, fn -> Voice.ask(text) end)

    {:noreply, socket}
  end

  def handle_async(:transcribe, result, socket), do: {:noreply, voice_error(socket, result)}

  def handle_async(:voice_chat, {:ok, {:ok, reply}}, socket) do
    lv = self()
    turn = socket.assigns.turn
    sentences = Voice.sentences(reply)

    socket =
      socket
      |> assign(presence: "speaking")
      |> add_message("assistant", reply)
      |> load_approvals()
      |> start_async(:speak, fn -> speak(lv, turn, sentences) end)

    {:noreply, socket}
  end

  def handle_async(:voice_chat, result, socket), do: {:noreply, voice_error(socket, result)}

  def handle_async(:speak, {:ok, :ok}, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}
  def handle_async(:speak, {:ok, {:error, message}}, socket), do: {:noreply, voice_error(socket, {:ok, {:error, message}})}
  def handle_async(:speak, {:exit, _}, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}

  def handle_async(:chat, result, socket) do
    text =
      case result do
        {:ok, {:ok, reply}} -> reply
        {:ok, {:error, message}} -> "Error: " <> message
        {:exit, _reason} -> "Error: the request stopped."
      end

    {:noreply, socket |> assign(waiting: false) |> add_message("assistant", text) |> load_approvals()}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(:refresh, socket), do: {:noreply, load_approvals(socket)}

  def handle_info({:voice_audio, turn, audio}, %{assigns: %{turn: turn}} = socket) do
    event = %{turn: turn, rate: audio.rate, data: Base.encode64(audio.pcm)}
    {:noreply, push_event(socket, "voice-audio", event)}
  end

  def handle_info({:voice_end, turn}, %{assigns: %{turn: turn}} = socket) do
    {:noreply, push_event(socket, "voice-end", %{turn: turn})}
  end

  # Audio from an earlier turn. A press of the button stopped that turn.
  def handle_info({:voice_audio, _, _}, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}
  def handle_info({:voice_end, _}, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}

  # Runs in the :speak task. Each sentence goes to the page when it is ready.
  defp speak(lv, turn, sentences) do
    result =
      Enum.reduce_while(sentences, :ok, fn sentence, :ok ->
        case Voice.synthesize(sentence) do
          {:ok, audio} -> send(lv, {:voice_audio, turn, audio}) && {:cont, :ok}
          {:error, message} -> {:halt, {:error, message}}
        end
      end)

    send(lv, {:voice_end, turn})
    result
  end

  defp voice_error(socket, result) do
    message =
      case result do
        {:ok, {:error, message}} -> message
        _ -> "The voice request stopped."
      end

    socket
    |> assign(presence: "error", notice: message)
    |> push_event("voice-error", %{})
  end

  defp load_history(socket) do
    case Core.history() do
      {:ok, messages} ->
        assign(socket, messages: Enum.map(messages, &message(&1["role"], &1["text"])))

      {:error, text} ->
        assign(socket, notice: text)
    end
  end

  defp load_approvals(socket) do
    case Core.approvals() do
      {:ok, items} -> assign(socket, approvals: items)
      {:error, text} -> assign(socket, notice: text)
    end
  end

  defp load_memory(socket) do
    case Core.memory_graph() do
      {:ok, graph} ->
        socket
        |> assign(memory_count: length(graph["nodes"] || []))
        |> push_event("memory-graph", %{nodes: graph["nodes"] || [], links: graph["links"] || [], view: socket.assigns.memory_view})

      {:error, text} ->
        assign(socket, notice: text)
    end
  end

  defp load(socket, key, {:ok, value}), do: assign(socket, key, value)
  defp load(socket, _key, {:error, text}), do: assign(socket, notice: text)

  defp add_message(socket, role, text),
    do: assign(socket, messages: socket.assigns.messages ++ [message(role, text)])

  # Only two roles. A different value from the core becomes "assistant".
  # The core marks a voice message with "[voice] ". The page shows a mark in its place.
  defp message("user", "[voice] " <> text), do: %{role: "user voice", text: text}
  defp message("user", text), do: %{role: "user", text: to_string(text)}
  defp message(_role, text), do: %{role: "assistant", text: to_string(text)}

  defp talk_label("listening"), do: "Listening... release to send"
  defp talk_label("thinking"), do: "LUNA is thinking..."
  defp talk_label("speaking"), do: "Hold to talk (stops LUNA)"
  defp talk_label(_), do: "Hold to talk"

  defp setting(value) when is_binary(value), do: value
  defp setting(value), do: Jason.encode!(value)

  defp event_line(%{"time" => time, "event" => event}) do
    (time |> to_string() |> String.slice(0, 19) |> String.replace("T", " ")) <> "  " <> to_string(event)
  end

  defp event_line(_), do: ""

  @impl true
  def render(assigns) do
    ~H"""
    <header>
      <div
        id="presence"
        phx-hook="Presence"
        class="presence"
        data-state={@presence}
        data-style={@presence_style}
        role="status"
        aria-label={"LUNA is " <> @presence}
      >
        <div class="orb"></div>
        <svg class="ring" viewBox="0 0 100 100" aria-hidden="true">
          <circle class="ring-track" cx="50" cy="50" r="42" />
          <circle class="ring-arc" cx="50" cy="50" r="42" />
        </svg>
      </div>
      <h1>LUNA</h1>
      <div class="styles" aria-label="Presence style">
        <button
          :for={style <- ["orb", "ring"]}
          phx-click="presence-style"
          phx-value-style={style}
          class={if @presence_style == style, do: "active"}
        >
          {String.capitalize(style)}
        </button>
      </div>
      <nav>
        <button
          :for={{panel, label} <- [{"chat", "Chat"}, {"approvals", "Approvals"}, {"money", "Money"}, {"memory", "Memory"}, {"system", "System"}]}
          phx-click="show"
          phx-value-panel={panel}
          class={if @panel == panel, do: "active"}
        >
          {label}<span :if={panel == "approvals" and @approvals != []}> ({length(@approvals)})</span>
        </button>
      </nav>
    </header>
    <main>
      <p :if={@notice} id="notice" class="notice">{@notice}</p>

      <section :if={@panel == "chat"} id="chat">
        <div id="messages" aria-live="polite">
          <div :for={m <- @messages} class={"msg " <> m.role}>{m.text}</div>
          <div :if={@waiting} class="msg assistant wait">LUNA is thinking...</div>
        </div>
        <div id="camera" phx-hook="Camera" phx-update="ignore">
          <div class="camera-view" hidden>
            <video autoplay playsinline muted></video>
            <div class="row">
              <select aria-label="Camera"></select>
              <button type="button" data-action="snap">Show LUNA</button>
              <button type="button" data-action="close">Close camera</button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="attachment" hidden>
            <img alt="Image for LUNA" />
            <button type="button" data-action="drop">Remove image</button>
          </div>
          <button type="button" data-action="open">Camera</button>
        </div>
        <button
          id="talk"
          phx-hook="Talk"
          class={"talk " <> @presence}
          type="button"
          aria-label="Hold to talk to LUNA"
        >
          {talk_label(@presence)}
        </button>
        <form id="composer" phx-hook="Composer" phx-update="ignore">
          <textarea name="text" rows="2" placeholder="Message LUNA. Enter sends, Shift+Enter is a new line."></textarea>
          <button type="submit">Send</button>
        </form>
      </section>

      <section :if={@panel == "approvals"} id="approvals">
        <p :if={@approvals == []} class="muted">No requests wait for you.</p>
        <div :for={item <- @approvals} class="card">
          <div>{item["summary"]}</div>
          <div class="muted">{item["id"]} - {item["created"]}</div>
          <div class="row">
            <button phx-click="decide" phx-value-id={item["id"]} phx-value-action="approve">Approve</button>
            <button phx-click="decide" phx-value-id={item["id"]} phx-value-action="deny">Deny</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section :if={@panel == "money"} id="money">
        <pre>{@money || "Loading..."}</pre>
      </section>

      <section :if={@panel == "memory"} id="memory">
        <div class="row memory-bar">
          <button phx-click="memory-view" phx-value-view="graph" class={if @memory_view == "graph", do: "active"}>Graph</button>
          <button phx-click="memory-view" phx-value-view="map" class={if @memory_view == "map", do: "active"}>Mind map</button>
          <span class="muted">{@memory_count} notes</span>
        </div>
        <div id="memory-graph" phx-hook="MemoryGraph" phx-update="ignore" data-view={@memory_view}>
          <input type="search" placeholder="Find a note" aria-label="Find a note" />
          <canvas aria-label="The long-term memory graph"></canvas>
          <p class="memory-title" aria-live="polite"></p>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section :if={@panel == "system"} id="system">
        <table :if={@system}>
          <tr><td class="muted">Started</td><td>{@system["started"]}</td></tr>
          <tr><td class="muted">Updates</td><td>{@system["update"]}</td></tr>
          <tr><td class="muted">Memory</td><td>{@system["memory"]}</td></tr>
          <tr><td class="muted">Waiting approvals</td><td>{@system["pending_approvals"]}</td></tr>
          <tr :for={{key, value} <- @system["settings"] || %{}}>
            <td class="muted">{key}</td><td>{setting(value)}</td>
          </tr>
        </table>
        <h3 :if={@system}>Recent events</h3>
        <pre :if={@system}>{Enum.map_join(@system["events"] || [], "\n", &event_line/1)}</pre>
      </section>
    </main>
    """
  end
end

#+begin_src js :tangle ../pkgs/luna-web/priv/static/app.js
// The LiveView socket and five hooks. phoenix.min.js and
// phoenix_live_view.min.js load first and set the globals Phoenix and LiveView.
(function () {
  "use strict";

  // The message box. Enter sends, Shift+Enter is a new line.
  var Composer = {
    mounted: function () {
      var form = this.el;
      var input = form.querySelector("textarea");
      var hook = this;
      var send = function (event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        hook.pushEvent("send", { text: input.value.trim() }, function (reply) {
          if (reply && reply.sent) input.value = "";
        });
      };
      form.addEventListener("submit", send);
      input.addEventListener("keydown", function (event) {
        if (event.key === "Enter" && !event.shiftKey) send(event);
      });
    }
  };

  // The camera. "Show LUNA" takes one frame, 1280 pixels wide or less.
  // The frame goes to the server with the next message.
  var Camera = {
    mounted: function () {
      var root = this.el;
      var hook = this;
      var view = root.querySelector(".camera-view");
      var video = root.querySelector("video");
      var list = root.querySelector("select");
      var attachment = root.querySelector(".attachment");
      var preview = attachment.querySelector("img");
      this.stream = null;

      var cameras = function () {
        return navigator.mediaDevices.enumerateDevices().then(function (devices) {
          var current = list.value;
          list.replaceChildren();
          devices.filter(function (d) { return d.kind === "videoinput"; }).forEach(function (d, i) {
            list.appendChild(new Option(d.label || "Camera " + (i + 1), d.deviceId));
          });
          if (current) list.value = current;
        });
      };
      var stop = function () {
        if (hook.stream) hook.stream.getTracks().forEach(function (t) { t.stop(); });
        hook.stream = null;
      };
      var start = function (deviceId) {
        stop();
        var video_options = deviceId ? { deviceId: { exact: deviceId } } : true;
        return navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ video: video_options }).then(function (stream) {
          hook.stream = stream;
          video.srcObject = stream;
          // After the first permission, the browser gives the camera names.
          return cameras();
        });
      };
      var clear = function () {
        attachment.hidden = true;
        preview.removeAttribute("src");
      };
      this.stop = stop;

      root.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
        var action = event.target.dataset && event.target.dataset.action;
        if (action === "open") {
          view.hidden = false;
          cameras().then(function () { return start(list.value); }).catch(function (error) {
            view.hidden = true;
            hook.pushEvent("camera-error", { message: String(error.message || error) });
          });
        } else if (action === "close") {
          stop();
          view.hidden = true;
        } else if (action === "snap") {
          var scale = Math.min(1, 1280 / video.videoWidth);
          var canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
          canvas.width = Math.round(video.videoWidth * scale);
          canvas.height = Math.round(video.videoHeight * scale);
          canvas.getContext("2d").drawImage(video, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
          var url = canvas.toDataURL("image/jpeg", 0.85);
          hook.pushEvent("attach", { media_type: "image/jpeg", data: url.split(",")[1] });
          preview.src = url;
          attachment.hidden = false;
          stop();
          view.hidden = true;
        } else if (action === "drop") {
          clear();
          hook.pushEvent("detach", {});
        }
      });
      list.addEventListener("change", function () { start(list.value); });
      this.handleEvent("image-cleared", clear);
    },
    destroyed: function () {
      if (this.stop) this.stop();
    }
  };

  // One audio context for the page. iOS starts it only in a user gesture,
  // so the first press of the talk button makes it.
  var Sound = {
    context: null,
    analyser: null,
    next: 0,
    sources: [],
    ensure: function () {
      if (!this.context) {
        this.context = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
        this.analyser = this.context.createAnalyser();
        this.analyser.fftSize = 512;
        this.analyser.connect(this.context.destination);
      }
      if (this.context.state === "suspended") this.context.resume();
      return this.context;
    },
    // Three earcons only. Refer to finding 7 of the interface review.
    earcon: function (kind) {
      if (!this.context) return;
      var tones = { listen: [660, 880], got: [520], error: [330, 220] }[kind] || [];
      var context = this.context;
      tones.forEach(function (frequency, i) {
        var start = context.currentTime + i * 0.09;
        var osc = context.createOscillator();
        var gain = context.createGain();
        osc.frequency.value = frequency;
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, start);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.15, start + 0.02);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, start + 0.12);
        osc.connect(gain).connect(context.destination);
        osc.start(start);
        osc.stop(start + 0.14);

    },
    // Plays one sentence of 16-bit mono PCM after the sentence before it.
    play: function (base64, rate, onEnd) {
      var context = this.ensure();
      var bytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(base64), function (c) { return c.charCodeAt(0); });
      var samples = new Int16Array(bytes.buffer, 0, bytes.length >> 1);
      var buffer = context.createBuffer(1, samples.length, rate);
      var channel = buffer.getChannelData(0);
      for (var i = 0; i < samples.length; i++) channel[i] = samples[i] / 0x8000;
      var source = context.createBufferSource();
      source.buffer = buffer;
      source.connect(this.analyser);
      var start = Math.max(context.currentTime + 0.05, this.next);
      source.start(start);
      this.next = start + buffer.duration;
      this.sources.push(source);
      var sound = this;
      source.onended = function () {
        sound.sources = sound.sources.filter(function (s) { return s !== source; });
        if (onEnd) onEnd(sound.sources.length === 0);
