    },
    stop: function () {
      this.sources.forEach(function (s) { s.onended = null; try { s.stop(); } catch (e) {} });
      this.sources = [];
      this.next = 0;
    },
    // The loudness of the voice of LUNA, from 0 to 1.
    level: function () {
      if (!this.analyser || this.sources.length === 0) return 0;
      var data = new Uint8Array(this.analyser.fftSize);
      this.analyser.getByteTimeDomainData(data);
      var sum = 0;
      for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) { var v = (data[i] - 128) / 128; sum += v * v; }
      return Math.min(1, Math.sqrt(sum / data.length) * 4);
    }
  };

  // The presence element: orb or ring. It plays the voice of LUNA, so speech
  // continues when you change the panel. Each device keeps its own style.
  var Presence = {
    mounted: function () {
      var hook = this;
      this.turn = null;
      this.ended = false;
      try {
        var saved = window.localStorage.getItem("luna-presence-style");
        if (saved && saved !== this.el.dataset.style) this.pushEvent("presence-style", { style: saved });
      } catch (e) {}
      this.handleEvent("voice-audio", function (event) {
        if (hook.turn !== event.turn) { Sound.stop(); hook.turn = event.turn; hook.ended = false; }
        Sound.play(event.data, event.rate, function (last) {
          if (last && hook.ended) hook.pushEvent("voice-done", { turn: hook.turn });
        });
      });
      this.handleEvent("voice-end", function (event) {
        hook.ended = true;
        if (Sound.sources.length === 0) hook.pushEvent("voice-done", { turn: event.turn });
      });
      this.handleEvent("voice-error", function () { Sound.earcon("error"); });
      this.handleEvent("voice-stop-audio", function () { Sound.stop(); });
      var frame = function () {
        var level = hook.el.dataset.state === "listening" ? (window.lunaMicLevel || 0) : Sound.level();
        hook.el.style.setProperty("--level", level.toFixed(3));
        hook.frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(frame);
      };
      frame();
    },
    updated: function () {
      try { window.localStorage.setItem("luna-presence-style", this.el.dataset.style); } catch (e) {}
    },
    destroyed: function () { window.cancelAnimationFrame(this.frame); }
  };

  // Push-to-talk. Hold the button, or hold Space when the message box is not
  // in focus. A press stops the voice of LUNA at once.
  var Talk = {
    mounted: function () {
      var hook = this;
      var button = this.el;
      this.active = false;

      var start = function (event) {
        if (event) event.preventDefault();
        if (hook.active) return;
        hook.active = true;
        var context = Sound.ensure();
        Sound.stop();
        hook.pushEvent("voice-start", {});
        Sound.earcon("listen");
        var media = navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
          audio: { echoCancellation: true, noiseSuppression: true, channelCount: 1 }
        });
        var worklet = context.audioWorklet.addModule("/mic-worklet.js");
        Promise.all([media, worklet]).then(function (results) {
          if (!hook.active) { results[0].getTracks().forEach(function (t) { t.stop(); }); return; }
          hook.stream = results[0];
          hook.source = context.createMediaStreamSource(hook.stream);
          hook.node = new AudioWorkletNode(context, "luna-mic");
          hook.node.port.onmessage = function (message) {
            var samples = message.data;
            var sum = 0;
            for (var i = 0; i < samples.length; i++) sum += Math.abs(samples[i]);
            window.lunaMicLevel = Math.min(1, (sum / samples.length / 0x8000) * 8);
            var bytes = new Uint8Array(samples.buffer);
            var text = "";
            for (var j = 0; j < bytes.length; j += 0x8000) {

            }
            hook.pushEvent("voice-chunk", { data: btoa(text) });

        }).catch(function (error) {

          Sound.earcon("error");
          hook.pushEvent("camera-error", { message: "Microphone: " + (error.message || error) });
        });
      };

      var stop = function (event) {
        if (event) event.preventDefault();
        if (!hook.active) return;
        hook.active = false;
        window.lunaMicLevel = 0;

        window.setTimeout(function () {
          if (hook.source) hook.source.disconnect();
          if (hook.node) hook.node.port.onmessage = null;
          if (hook.stream) hook.stream.getTracks().forEach(function (t) { t.stop(); });
          hook.source = hook.node = hook.stream = null;
          hook.pushEvent("voice-stop", {});
          Sound.earcon("got");

      button.addEventListener("pointerup", stop);
      button.addEventListener("pointercancel", stop);
      button.addEventListener("pointerleave", stop);

        if (event.code === "Space" && !typing && !event.repeat) start(event);
      };
      this.keyup = function (event) { if (event.code === "Space" && hook.active) stop(event); };
      window.addEventListener("keydown", this.keydown);
      window.addEventListener("keyup", this.keyup);
    },
    destroyed: function () {
      window.removeEventListener("keydown", this.keydown);
      window.removeEventListener("keyup", this.keyup);
      if (this.stream) this.stream.getTracks().forEach(function (t) { t.stop(); });
    }
  };

  // The long-term memory: a force graph, or a mind map around one note.
  // Our own code: the page loads no graph library, and no file from the internet.
  var MemoryGraph = {
    mounted: function () {
      var hook = this;
      var root = this.el;
      var canvas = root.querySelector("canvas");
      var search = root.querySelector("input");
      var label = root.querySelector(".memory-title");
      var ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
      var reduce = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;
      var nodes = [], links = [], byId = {}, near = {};
      var view = root.dataset.view || "graph";
      var focus = null, hover = null, match = "";
      var cam = { x: 0, y: 0, k: 1 };
      var alpha = 0, frame = null, drag = null, pointers = {};

      var css = function (name, fallback) {
        return getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue(name).trim() || fallback;
      };
      var hue = function (tag) {
        var h = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < tag.length; i++) h = (h * 31 + tag.charCodeAt(i)) % 360;
        return h;
      };
      var colour = function (n) {
        return n.tags && n.tags.length ? "hsl(" + hue(n.tags[0]) + ", 55%, 55%)" : css("--muted", "#888");
      };

      var size = function () {
        var ratio = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
        var w = root.clientWidth, h = Math.max(320, Math.round(window.innerHeight * 0.65));
        canvas.style.width = w + "px";
        canvas.style.height = h + "px";
        canvas.width = Math.round(w * ratio);
        canvas.height = Math.round(h * ratio);
        ctx.setTransform(ratio, 0, 0, ratio, 0, 0);
        hook.w = w;
        hook.h = h;
      };

      var setData = function (data) {
        var old = byId;
        byId = {};
        near = {};
        nodes = (data.nodes || []).map(function (n, i) {
          var o = old[n.id];
          var a = i * 2.399963;
          var node = { id: n.id, title: n.title, tags: n.tags || [], owner: n.owner,
                       x: o ? o.x : Math.cos(a) * 12 * Math.sqrt(i + 1), y: o ? o.y : Math.sin(a) * 12 * Math.sqrt(i + 1),
                       vx: 0, vy: 0, tx: null, ty: null };
          byId[n.id] = node;
          near[n.id] = [];
          return node;
        });
        links = (data.links || []).filter(function (l) { return byId[l.source] && byId[l.target]; })
          .map(function (l) {
            near[l.source].push(l.target);
            near[l.target].push(l.source);
            return { s: byId[l.source], t: byId[l.target] };

        layout();
      };

        var best = nodes[0];
        nodes.forEach(function (n) { if (near[n.id].length > near[best.id].length) best = n; });
        return best.id;
      };

        for (i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
          a = nodes[i];
          for (j = i + 1; j < nodes.length; j++) {
            b = nodes[j];

            // A floor on the distance stops a very large push between near nodes.

          var d = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) || 1;

          l.s.vx += dx * f; l.s.vy += dy * f; l.t.vx -= dx * f; l.t.vy -= dy * f;

          if (drag && drag.node === n) { n.vx = 0; n.vy = 0; return; }
          n.vx *= 0.6; n.vy *= 0.6;

          if (speed > 12) { n.vx *= 12 / speed; n.vy *= 12 / speed; }
          n.x += n.vx * alpha; n.y += n.vy * alpha;

      };

      // The mind map: the focus in the centre, its neighbours on ring 1, theirs on ring 2.
      var mindMap = function () {
        nodes.forEach(function (n) { n.tx = null; n.ring = null; });
        if (!focus) return;
        var centre = byId[focus];
        centre.tx = 0; centre.ty = 0; centre.ring = 0;
        var ring1 = near[focus].filter(function (id, i, all) { return all.indexOf(id) === i && id !== focus; });
        var seen = {};
        seen[focus] = true;
        ring1.forEach(function (id) { seen[id] = true; });
        var step = (Math.PI * 2) / Math.max(ring1.length, 1);
        ring1.forEach(function (id, i) {
          var a = i * step - Math.PI / 2;
          var n = byId[id];
          n.tx = Math.cos(a) * 150; n.ty = Math.sin(a) * 150; n.ring = 1;
          var kids = near[id].filter(function (k) { if (seen[k]) return false; seen[k] = true; return true; });
          kids.forEach(function (k, j) {
            var spread = step * 0.8;
            var b = a - spread / 2 + spread * (kids.length === 1 ? 0.5 : j / (kids.length - 1));
            var c = byId[k];

        });
      };

      var layout = function () {
        fitted = true;
        if (view === "map") {
          mindMap();
          if (reduce) nodes.forEach(function (n) { if (n.tx !== null) { n.x = n.tx; n.y = n.ty; } });
        } else {
          alpha = 1;
          if (reduce) { for (var i = 0; i < 300; i++) tick(); alpha = 0; }
        }
        run();
      };

      var shown = function (n) { return view === "graph" || n.ring !== null; };

      // Fit the camera to the shown nodes, until the owner pans or zooms.
      var fitted = true;
      var fit = function () {
        if (!fitted) return;
        var xs = [], ys = [];
        nodes.forEach(function (n) {
          if (!shown(n)) return;
          xs.push(view === "map" && n.tx !== null ? n.tx : n.x);
          ys.push(view === "map" && n.tx !== null ? n.ty : n.y);
        });
        if (!xs.length) return;
        var minX = Math.min.apply(null, xs), maxX = Math.max.apply(null, xs);
        var minY = Math.min.apply(null, ys), maxY = Math.max.apply(null, ys);
        var k = Math.min((hook.w - 80) / Math.max(maxX - minX, 1), (hook.h - 80) / Math.max(maxY - minY, 1));
        cam.k = Math.min(2, Math.max(0.2, k));
        cam.x = -((minX + maxX) / 2) * cam.k;
        cam.y = -((minY + maxY) / 2) * cam.k;

        var w = hook.w, h = hook.h;
        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
        ctx.save();
        ctx.translate(w / 2 + cam.x, h / 2 + cam.y);

        });

        ctx.font = (12 / cam.k) + "px system-ui, sans-serif";
        ctx.lineJoin = "round";

          if (!shown(n)) return;

          var label = view === "map" ? n.ring <= 1 || hit : cam.k > 1.2 || hit || n === hover || near[n.id].length >= 3;
          if (label) { ctx.fillStyle = text; ctx.fillText(n.title, n.x, n.y - r - 4 / cam.k); }
        });

      };

      var run = function () {
        if (frame) return;
        var step = function () {
          frame = null;
          var moving = false;
          if (view === "graph" && alpha > 0.02) { tick(); moving = true; }
          if (view === "map") {
            nodes.forEach(function (n) {
              if (n.tx === null) return;
              var dx = n.tx - n.x, dy = n.ty - n.y;
              if (Math.abs(dx) + Math.abs(dy) > 0.5) { n.x += dx * 0.18; n.y += dy * 0.18; moving = true; }
            });
          }
          fit();
          draw();
          if (moving || drag) frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(step);
        };
        frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(step);
      };

      var world = function (event) {
        var box = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
        return { x: (event.clientX - box.left - hook.w / 2 - cam.x) / cam.k,

      };
      var nodeAt = function (p) {
        var best = null, bestD = (14 / cam.k) * (14 / cam.k);
        nodes.forEach(function (n) {
          if (!shown(n)) return;
          var d = (n.x - p.x) * (n.x - p.x) + (n.y - p.y) * (n.y - p.y);
          if (d < bestD) { best = n; bestD = d; }

      canvas.addEventListener("pointerdown", function (event) {
        canvas.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);
        pointers[event.pointerId] = { x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY };

        run();
      });
      canvas.addEventListener("pointermove", function (event) {
        var ids = Object.keys(pointers);
        if (pointers[event.pointerId] && ids.length === 2) {
          // Two fingers: zoom by the change in the distance between them.
          var other = pointers[ids[0] == event.pointerId ? ids[1] : ids[0]];
          var before = Math.hypot(pointers[event.pointerId].x - other.x, pointers[event.pointerId].y - other.y);
          var after = Math.hypot(event.clientX - other.x, event.clientY - other.y);
          fitted = false;
          cam.k = Math.min(4, Math.max(0.2, cam.k * (after / (before || after))));
          pointers[event.pointerId] = { x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY };
          draw();
          return;
        }
        if (!drag) {
          var n = nodeAt(world(event));
          if (n !== hover) { hover = n; label.textContent = n ? n.title + (n.tags.length ? "  (" + n.tags.join(", ") + ")" : "") : ""; draw(); }
          return;
        }
        if (Math.abs(event.clientX - drag.sx) + Math.abs(event.clientY - drag.sy) > 4) drag.moved = true;
        if (drag.node && view === "graph") {
          var p = world(event);
          drag.node.x = p.x; drag.node.y = p.y;
          alpha = Math.max(alpha, 0.3);
        } else {
          fitted = false;
          cam.x = drag.cx + event.clientX - drag.sx;
          cam.y = drag.cy + event.clientY - drag.sy;
        }
        pointers[event.pointerId] = { x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY };
        run();
      });
      var release = function (event) {
        delete pointers[event.pointerId];
        if (drag && !drag.moved && drag.node) {
          focus = drag.node.id;
          if (view === "graph") hook.pushEvent("memory-focus", { id: focus });
          else layout();
        }
        drag = null;
        run();
      };
      canvas.addEventListener("pointerup", release);
      canvas.addEventListener("pointercancel", release);
      canvas.addEventListener("wheel", function (event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        fitted = false;
        cam.k = Math.min(4, Math.max(0.2, cam.k * (event.deltaY < 0 ? 1.12 : 1 / 1.12)));
        draw();
      }, { passive: false });

      search.addEventListener("input", function () { match = search.value.trim().toLowerCase(); draw(); });
      search.addEventListener("keydown", function (event) {
        if (event.key !== "Enter" || !match) return;
        var found = nodes.filter(function (n) { return n.title.toLowerCase().indexOf(match) >= 0; })[0];
        if (found) { focus = found.id; view = "map"; hook.pushEvent("memory-focus", { id: found.id }); }
      });

      this.handleEvent("memory-graph", function (data) {
        if (data.view) view = data.view;
        setData(data);
      });
      this.handleEvent("memory-view", function (data) {
        view = data.view;
        if (data.focus && byId[data.focus]) focus = data.focus;
        layout();
      });

      this.resize = function () { size(); draw(); };
      window.addEventListener("resize", this.resize);
      size();
      draw();
    },
    destroyed: function () {
      window.removeEventListener("resize", this.resize);
    }
  };

  var token = document.querySelector("meta[name='csrf-token']").getAttribute("content");
  var socket = new LiveView.LiveSocket("/live", Phoenix.Socket, {
    params: { _csrf_token: token },
    hooks: { Composer: Composer, Camera: Camera, Presence: Presence, Talk: Talk, MemoryGraph: MemoryGraph }
  });
  socket.connect();
})();
#+end_src

#+begin_src css :tangle ../pkgs/luna-web/priv/static/app.css
:root { color-scheme: light dark; --bg: #f6f8fa; --panel: #ffffff; --text: #1f2328; --muted: #57606a;
        --accent: #6f42c1; --mine: #eef1f5; --line: #d0d7de; }
@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) {
  :root { --bg: #0e1116; --panel: #161b22; --text: #e6edf3; --muted: #8b949e; --accent: #b392f0;
          --mine: #1f2937; --line: #30363d; }
}
* { box-sizing: border-box; }
body { margin: 0; font: 16px/1.5 system-ui, sans-serif; background: var(--bg); color: var(--text); }
header { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; padding: 8px 16px;
         background: var(--panel); border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 8px; }
h1 { margin: 0; font-size: 20px; letter-spacing: 4px; color: var(--accent); }
nav { display: flex; gap: 6px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
button { background: none; border: 1px solid var(--line); color: var(--text); padding: 6px 12px;
         border-radius: 6px; cursor: pointer; font: inherit; }
nav button.active { border-color: var(--accent); color: var(--accent); }
main { max-width: 900px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 16px; }
#messages { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 10px; min-height: 50vh; margin-bottom: 12px; }
.msg { padding: 10px 14px; border-radius: 10px; white-space: pre-wrap; overflow-wrap: anywhere; max-width: 90%; }
.msg.user { align-self: flex-end; background: var(--mine); }
.msg.assistant { align-self: flex-start; background: var(--panel); border: 1px solid var(--line); }
.msg.wait { color: var(--muted); font-style: italic; }
#camera { margin-bottom: 8px; }
#composer { display: flex; gap: 8px; align-items: flex-end; }
textarea { flex: 1; resize: vertical; background: var(--panel); color: var(--text); border: 1px solid var(--line);
           border-radius: 6px; padding: 8px; font: inherit; min-width: 0; }
video { width: 100%; max-height: 50vh; background: #000; border-radius: 8px; }
.row { display: flex; gap: 8px; margin: 8px 0; flex-wrap: wrap; }
.attachment img { max-height: 120px; border-radius: 6px; display: block; margin-bottom: 6px; }
.card { background: var(--panel); border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 8px; padding: 12px;
        margin-bottom: 10px; white-space: pre-wrap; }
.notice { border-left: 3px solid var(--accent); padding: 4px 10px; }
pre { background: var(--panel); padding: 12px; border-radius: 8px; white-space: pre-wrap; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
table { border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; }
td { padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
.muted { color: var(--muted); }
/* The presence element. --level is the loudness, from 0 to 1. */
.presence { --level: 0; position: relative; width: 44px; height: 44px; flex: none; }
.presence .orb, .presence .ring { position: absolute; inset: 0; width: 100%; height: 100%; }
.presence[data-style="orb"] .ring, .presence[data-style="ring"] .orb { display: none; }
.orb { border-radius: 50%; background: radial-gradient(circle at 35% 35%, #fff 0, var(--accent) 45%, transparent 72%);
       transform: scale(calc(0.72 + var(--level) * 0.45)); opacity: 0.85; transition: transform 60ms linear; }
.ring-track { fill: none; stroke: var(--line); stroke-width: 6; }
.ring-arc { fill: none; stroke: var(--accent); stroke-width: calc(6px + var(--level) * 6px); stroke-linecap: round;
            stroke-dasharray: 66 198; transform-origin: 50% 50%; }
.presence[data-state="idle"] .orb { animation: breathe 5s ease-in-out infinite; }
.presence[data-state="idle"] .ring-arc { stroke-dasharray: 264 0; opacity: 0.35; }
.presence[data-state="listening"] .orb { background: radial-gradient(circle at 35% 35%, #fff 0, #3fb950 45%, transparent 72%); }
.presence[data-state="listening"] .ring-arc { stroke: #3fb950; stroke-dasharray: 264 0; }
.presence[data-state="thinking"] .orb { animation: pulse 1.1s ease-in-out infinite; }
.presence[data-state="thinking"] .ring-arc { animation: spin 1.2s linear infinite; }
.presence[data-state="speaking"] .ring-arc { stroke-dasharray: 264 0; }
.presence[data-state="error"] .orb { background: radial-gradient(circle at 35% 35%, #fff 0, #f85149 45%, transparent 72%); }
.presence[data-state="error"] .ring-arc { stroke: #f85149; stroke-dasharray: 264 0; }
@keyframes breathe { 50% { transform: scale(0.82); opacity: 0.6; } }
@keyframes pulse { 50% { transform: scale(0.9); opacity: 1; } }
@keyframes spin { to { transform: rotate(360deg); } }
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) { .orb, .ring-arc { animation: none !important; transition: none; } }
.styles { display: flex; gap: 4px; }
.styles button { padding: 2px 8px; font-size: 13px; }
.styles button.active { border-color: var(--accent); color: var(--accent); }
.talk { display: block; width: 100%; padding: 16px; margin: 8px 0; font-size: 17px; touch-action: none;
        user-select: none; -webkit-user-select: none; -webkit-touch-callout: none; }
.talk.listening { border-color: #3fb950; color: #3fb950; }
.msg.voice::before { content: "\1F399  "; }
/* The Memory panel. */
#memory-graph { position: relative; }
#memory-graph input { width: 100%; margin-bottom: 8px; background: var(--panel); color: var(--text);
                      border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 6px; padding: 8px; font: inherit; }
#memory-graph canvas { display: block; width: 100%; background: var(--panel); border: 1px solid var(--line);
                       border-radius: 8px; touch-action: none; cursor: grab; }
.memory-bar button.active { border-color: var(--accent); color: var(--accent); }
.memory-title { min-height: 1.5em; color: var(--muted); margin: 6px 0 0; }
#+end_src

** Tests

The tests run in the Nix build. =make check= runs the tests.
Each test runs in a database transaction, and the transaction rolls back.

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-web/test/test_helper.exs
:ok = LunaWeb.FakeCore.start()
:ok = LunaWeb.FakeWyoming.start()
ExUnit.start()
Ecto.Adapters.SQL.Sandbox.mode(LunaWeb.Repo, :manual)
#+end_src

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-web/test/support/case.ex
defmodule LunaWeb.Case do
  @moduledoc "Each test gets a database transaction. The transaction rolls back at the end."
  use ExUnit.CaseTemplate

  using do
    quote do
      import Plug.Conn
      import Phoenix.ConnTest
      @endpoint LunaWeb.Endpoint
    end
  end

  setup do
    :ok = Ecto.Adapters.SQL.Sandbox.checkout(LunaWeb.Repo)
  end
end
#+end_src

The fake core serves the contract on a real unix socket. Thus the tests also cover Req on a unix socket.

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-web/test/support/fake_core.ex
defmodule LunaWeb.FakeCore do
  @moduledoc """
  A fake core API on a unix socket. It keeps each request that it gets.
  The replies follow the contract in "Build: the core API".
  """
  use Plug.Router

  plug :match
  plug Plug.Parsers, parsers: [:json], json_decoder: Jason
  plug :keep
  plug :dispatch

  def start do
    path = Application.fetch_env!(:luna_web, :core_socket)
    File.rm(path)
    {:ok, _} = Agent.start_link(fn -> [] end, name: __MODULE__.Requests)
    {:ok, _} = Bandit.start_link(plug: __MODULE__, ip: {:local, path}, port: 0, startup_log: false)
    :ok
  end

  def reset, do: Agent.update(__MODULE__.Requests, fn _ -> [] end)
  def requests, do: Agent.get(__MODULE__.Requests, &Enum.reverse/1)

  defp keep(conn, _opts) do
    Agent.update(__MODULE__.Requests, &[{conn.method, conn.request_path, conn.body_params} | &1])
    conn
  end

  get "/api/history" do
    reply(conn, %{messages: [%{role: "user", text: "hello"}, %{role: "assistant", text: "hi"}]})
  end

  post "/api/chat" do
    reply(conn, %{reply: "reply: " <> conn.body_params["text"]})
  end

  get "/api/approvals" do
    reply(conn, %{approvals: [%{id: "a1b2c3", summary: "Test payment", created: "2026-11-20T00:00:00Z"}]})
  end

  post "/api/approvals/:id/approve" do
    reply(conn, %{result: "done"})
  end

  get "/api/money" do
    reply(conn, %{text: "Money is off. Refer to 'The gate for phase 2' in LUNA.org."})
  end

  get "/api/memory/graph" do
    reply(conn, %{
      nodes: [%{id: "a", title: "Polaris1", tags: ["project"], owner: "coops"}, %{id: "b", title: "Perth", tags: ["place"], owner: "luna"}],
      links: [%{source: "a", target: "b"}]
    })
  end

  get "/api/system" do
    reply(conn, %{
      started: "2026-11-20T00:00:00Z",
      update: "nixpkgs is less than 14 days old.",
      memory: "",
      settings: %{model: "claude-opus-5-5"},
      pending_approvals: 0,
      events: []
    })
  end

  defp reply(conn, body) do
    conn |> put_resp_content_type("application/json") |> send_resp(200, Jason.encode!(body))
  end
end
#+end_src

Each Python web test has an Elixir test:

| Python test (E1)                              | Elixir test                                                    |
|-----------------------------------------------+----------------------------------------------------------------|
| =test_no_login_is_refused=                    | a request with no login gets 403 / a different login gets 403  |
| =test_owner_gets_the_page_with_a_strict_policy= | the owner gets the page with the security headers            |
| =test_post_needs_origin_and_header=           | a socket from another origin gets 403                          |
| =test_chat_with_an_image=                     | chat with an image                                             |
| =test_bad_image_is_refused=                   | a bad image is refused                                         |
| =test_approve_from_the_page=                  | approve from the page                                          |
| =test_money_is_off_by_default=                | money is off by default                                        |
| =test_system_panel=                           | the system panel                                               |

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-web/test/core_test.exs
defmodule LunaWeb.CoreTest do
  # One fake core serves all tests, so these tests do not run at the same time.
  use LunaWeb.Case, async: false

  alias LunaWeb.{Core, FakeCore}

  @owner "owner@example.com"

  setup do
    FakeCore.reset()
  end

  @tag :capture_log
  test "a socket from another origin gets 403, and the core gets no request" do
    conn =
      build_conn()
      |> put_req_header("origin", "https://evil.example")
      |> get("/live/websocket", %{"vsn" => "2.0.0"})

    assert conn.status == 403
    assert FakeCore.requests() == []
  end

  test "chat with an image" do
    data = Base.encode64(<<0xFF, 0xD8>> <> "fake-jpeg")
    image = %{"media_type" => "image/jpeg", "data" => data}
    assert Core.chat("what is this?", image) == {:ok, "reply: what is this?"}

    assert [{"POST", "/api/chat", %{"text" => "what is this?", "image" => ^image}}] =
             FakeCore.requests()
  end

  test "a bad image is refused, and the core gets no request" do
    assert {:error, _} = Core.chat("x", %{"media_type" => "image/gif", "data" => "AA=="})
    assert {:error, _} = Core.chat("x", %{"media_type" => "image/png", "data" => "not base64!"})
    assert {:error, "Write a message."} = Core.chat("  ", nil)
    assert FakeCore.requests() == []
  end

  test "approve from the page" do
    assert {:ok, [%{"summary" => "Test payment", "id" => id}]} = Core.approvals()
    assert Core.decide(id, "approve") == {:ok, "done"}
    assert {"POST", "/api/approvals/a1b2c3/approve", %{}} in FakeCore.requests()
  end

  test "a bad approval ID or action is refused, and the core gets no request" do
    assert {:error, _} = Core.decide("../money", "approve")
    assert {:error, _} = Core.decide("a1b2c3", "delete")
    assert FakeCore.requests() == []
  end

  test "money is off by default" do
    assert {:ok, text} = Core.money()
    assert text =~ "Money is off"
  end

  test "the memory graph" do
    assert {:ok, %{"nodes" => [_, _], "links" => [%{"source" => "a", "target" => "b"}]}} = Core.memory_graph()
  end

  test "the system panel" do
    assert {:ok, %{"pending_approvals" => 0, "update" => _}} = Core.system()
  end

  test "the page has the four panels" do
    conn = build_conn() |> put_req_header("tailscale-user-login", @owner) |> get("/")
    body = html_response(conn, 200)

    for label <- ["Chat", "Approvals", "Money", "Memory", "System"], do: assert(body =~ label)

    assert body =~ ~s(phx-hook="Camera")
  end
end
#+end_src

The fake Wyoming server serves STT and TTS on one TCP port.

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-web/test/support/fake_wyoming.ex
defmodule LunaWeb.FakeWyoming do
  @moduledoc """
  A fake Wyoming server for STT and TTS. It keeps the type of each event that
  it gets, and the count of audio bytes.
  STT replies with the transcript in :transcript. TTS replies with the text as the PCM.
  """

  def start do
    {:ok, listen} = :gen_tcp.listen(0, [:binary, active: false, packet: :line, reuseaddr: true, ip: {127, 0, 0, 1}])
    {:ok, port} = :inet.port(listen)
    {:ok, _} = Agent.start_link(fn -> %{events: [], bytes: 0, transcript: "hello luna"} end, name: __MODULE__)
    spawn_link(fn -> accept(listen) end)
    uri = "tcp://127.0.0.1:#{port}"
    Application.put_env(:luna_web, :voice, stt: uri, tts: uri)
    :ok
  end

  def reset(transcript \\ "hello luna"),
    do: Agent.update(__MODULE__, fn _ -> %{events: [], bytes: 0, transcript: transcript} end)

  def state, do: Agent.get(__MODULE__, & &1)

  defp accept(listen) do
    {:ok, socket} = :gen_tcp.accept(listen)
    spawn(fn -> serve(socket) end)
    accept(listen)
  end

  defp serve(socket) do
    case read(socket) do
      {:ok, type, data, payload} ->
        Agent.update(__MODULE__, fn s ->
          %{s | events: s.events ++ [type], bytes: s.bytes + byte_size(payload)}
        end)

        reply(socket, type, data)
        serve(socket)

      :closed ->
        :ok
    end
  end

  defp reply(socket, "audio-stop", _) do
    send_event(socket, "transcript", %{"text" => state().transcript})
  end

  defp reply(socket, "synthesize", %{"text" => text}) do
    format = %{"rate" => 22_050, "width" => 2, "channels" => 1}
    send_event(socket, "audio-start", format)
    send_event(socket, "audio-chunk", format, text)
    send_event(socket, "audio-stop", %{})
  end

  defp reply(_socket, _type, _data), do: :ok

  defp send_event(socket, type, data, payload \\ nil),
    do: :gen_tcp.send(socket, LunaWeb.Wyoming.encode(type, data, payload))

  defp read(socket) do
    :inet.setopts(socket, packet: :line)

    with {:ok, line} <- :gen_tcp.recv(socket, 0),
         header = Jason.decode!(line),
         :ok <- :inet.setopts(socket, packet: :raw),
         {:ok, data} <- exact(socket, header["data_length"]),
         {:ok, payload} <- exact(socket, header["payload_length"]) do
      {:ok, header["type"], if(data == "", do: %{}, else: Jason.decode!(data)), payload}
    else
      _ -> :closed
    end
  end

  defp exact(_socket, n) when n in [nil, 0], do: {:ok, ""}
  defp exact(socket, n), do: :gen_tcp.recv(socket, n)
end
#+end_src

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-web/test/voice_test.exs
defmodule LunaWeb.VoiceTest do
  # One fake core and one fake voice server serve all tests.
  use LunaWeb.Case, async: false

  alias LunaWeb.{FakeCore, FakeWyoming, Voice, Wyoming}

  setup do
    FakeCore.reset()
    FakeWyoming.reset()
  end

  # One second of silence: 16 kHz, 16-bit, mono.
  @second :binary.copy(<<0, 0>>, 16_000)

  test "an event has a JSON line, the data, then the payload" do
    [line, "\n", data, payload] = Wyoming.encode("audio-chunk", %{"rate" => 16_000}, "abc")
    header = Jason.decode!(line)
    assert header["type"] == "audio-chunk"
    assert header["data_length"] == byte_size(data)
    assert header["payload_length"] == 3
    assert payload == "abc"
  end

  test "speech to text sends all the audio and returns the transcript" do
    assert Voice.transcribe(@second) == {:ok, "hello luna"}
    state = FakeWyoming.state()
    assert hd(state.events) == "transcribe"
    assert List.last(state.events) == "audio-stop"
    assert state.bytes == byte_size(@second)
  end

  test "a very short or a very long recording does not go to the server" do
    assert {:error, _} = Voice.transcribe(binary_part(@second, 0, 100))
    assert {:error, _} = Voice.transcribe(:binary.copy(@second, 61))
    assert FakeWyoming.state().events == []
  end

  test "an empty transcript is an error" do
    FakeWyoming.reset("  ")
    assert {:error, "I did not hear anything."} = Voice.transcribe(@second)
  end

  test "text to speech returns the PCM and its rate" do
    assert {:ok, %{rate: 22_050, pcm: "Hi."}} = Voice.synthesize("Hi.")
  end

  test "a voice turn goes to chat as a voice message" do
    assert Voice.ask("next swing?") == {:ok, "reply: next swing?"}
    assert [{"POST", "/api/chat", %{"text" => "next swing?", "channel" => "voice"}}] = FakeCore.requests()
  end

  test "a voice turn cannot approve a request" do
    FakeWyoming.reset("approve a1b2c3")
    {:ok, text} = Voice.transcribe(@second)
    assert {:ok, _} = Voice.ask(text)
    assert [{"POST", "/api/chat", %{"channel" => "voice"}}] = FakeCore.requests()
  end

  test "a text message has the channel text" do
    assert {:ok, _} = LunaWeb.Core.chat("hi", nil)
    assert [{"POST", "/api/chat", %{"channel" => "text"}}] = FakeCore.requests()
  end

  test "the text for speech has no Markdown, and comes in sentences" do
    text = """
    ## Plan
    **Safe to spend:** $120. See [the plan](https://example.com).
    - Rent is due Friday!
    | a | b |
    Next swing starts in 9 days?
    """

    assert Voice.sentences(text) == [
             "Plan Safe to spend: $120.",
             "See the plan.",
             "Rent is due Friday!",
             "Next swing starts in 9 days?"
           ]
  end

  test "a long first sentence is cut at its first comma" do
    long = "Your safe-to-spend number this week is lower than last week, because the car insurance is due."
    assert [first, second] = Voice.sentences(long)
    assert String.ends_with?(first, ",")
    assert String.starts_with?(second, "because")
  end
end
#+end_src

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-web/test/web_test.exs
defmodule LunaWeb.WebTest do
  use LunaWeb.Case, async: true

  @owner "owner@example.com"

  defp audit_events do
    %{rows: rows} = LunaWeb.Repo.query!("SELECT event, data FROM audit_events ORDER BY id")
    rows
  end

  test "a request with no login gets 403 and an audit event" do
    conn = get(build_conn(), "/")
    assert conn.status == 403
    assert audit_events() == [["web.refused", %{"reason" => "login", "path" => "/"}]]
  end

  test "a request with a different login gets 403" do
    conn = build_conn() |> put_req_header("tailscale-user-login", "other@example.com") |> get("/")
    assert conn.status == 403
  end

  test "a request with an empty login gets 403" do
    conn = build_conn() |> put_req_header("tailscale-user-login", "") |> get("/")
    assert conn.status == 403
  end

  test "the static files need the login" do
    assert get(build_conn(), "/app.js").status == 403
    conn = build_conn() |> put_req_header("tailscale-user-login", @owner) |> get("/app.js")
    assert conn.status == 200
  end

  test "the owner gets the page with the security headers" do
    conn = build_conn() |> put_req_header("tailscale-user-login", @owner) |> get("/")
    body = html_response(conn, 200)
    assert body =~ "<title>LUNA</title>"
    [csp] = get_resp_header(conn, "content-security-policy")
    assert csp =~ "script-src 'self'"
    assert get_resp_header(conn, "x-content-type-options") == ["nosniff"]
    assert get_resp_header(conn, "cache-control") == ["no-store"]
  end

  test "the mount itself refuses a session with no owner login" do
    socket = %Phoenix.LiveView.Socket{}
    assert {:ok, %{redirected: {:redirect, %{to: "/"}}}} = LunaWeb.HomeLive.mount(%{}, %{}, socket)
  end

  test "the LiveView mount refuses a session with no owner login" do
    socket = %Phoenix.LiveView.Socket{}
    assert {:halt, _} = LunaWeb.Owner.on_mount(:owner, %{}, %{}, socket)
    assert {:halt, _} = LunaWeb.Owner.on_mount(:owner, %{}, %{"owner_login" => "x"}, socket)
    assert {:cont, _} = LunaWeb.Owner.on_mount(:owner, %{}, %{"owner_login" => @owner}, socket)
  end
end
#+end_src

#+begin_src elixir :tangle ../pkgs/luna-web/test/audit_test.exs
defmodule LunaWeb.AuditTest do
  use LunaWeb.Case, async: true

  alias LunaWeb.Repo

  setup do
    :ok = LunaWeb.Audit.write("test.event", %{"n" => 1})
  end

  test "the audit log refuses UPDATE" do
    assert_raise Postgrex.Error, ~r/refuses UPDATE/, fn ->
      Repo.query!("UPDATE audit_events SET event = 'changed'")
    end
  end

  test "the audit log refuses DELETE" do
    assert_raise Postgrex.Error, ~r/refuses DELETE/, fn ->
      Repo.query!("DELETE FROM audit_events")
    end
  end

  test "the audit log refuses TRUNCATE" do
    assert_raise Postgrex.Error, ~r/refuses TRUNCATE/, fn ->
      Repo.query!("TRUNCATE audit_events")
    end
  end
end
#+end_src

** luna-web service

The service runs as the system user =luna_web=, in the same systemd sandbox as =luna-core=.
The service starts after =postgresql.target=.
That target includes =postgresql-setup=, which makes the role and the database.

#+begin_src nix :tangle ../modules/luna/web.nix
{ config, lib, pkgs, hostName, site, ... }:
let
  luna-web = pkgs.callPackage ../../pkgs/luna-web { };
  fqdn = "${hostName}.${site.tailnet}";
  user = "luna_web";
  stateDir = "/var/lib/luna-web";

  # The secret key base and the release cookie stay on luna. The first start
  # makes them. To make new ones, delete the files and restart the service.
  start = pkgs.writeShellScript "luna-web-start" ''
    set -euo pipefail
    umask 077
    for name in secret_key_base cookie; do
      if [ ! -s "${stateDir}/$name" ]; then
        ${pkgs.openssl}/bin/openssl rand -hex 48 > "${stateDir}/$name"
      fi
    done
    RELEASE_COOKIE="$(cat ${stateDir}/cookie)"
    export RELEASE_COOKIE
    exec ${luna-web}/bin/luna_web start
  '';
in
{
  services.postgresql = {
    enable = true;
    package = pkgs.postgresql_18;
    # The unix socket only. No TCP port.
    settings.listen_addresses = lib.mkForce "";
    # Peer authentication only: the system user name is the role name.
    authentication = lib.mkForce ''
      local all all peer
    '';
    ensureDatabases = [ user ];
    ensureUsers = [ { name = user; ensureDBOwnership = true; } ];
  };

  users.groups.${user} = { };
  users.users.${user} = {
    isSystemUser = true;
    group = user;
    # The only client of the core API. Refer to "Build: the core API".
    extraGroups = [ "luna-api" ];
    home = stateDir;
  };
  # core.nix makes this group too. A test of this module alone needs it here.
  users.groups.luna-api = { };

  systemd.services.luna-web = {
    description = "LUNA web app (Phoenix)";
    # postgresql.target includes postgresql-setup, which makes the role.
    # The page works without the core, and shows a notice for each panel.
    after = [ "network.target" "postgresql.target" "luna-core.service" ];
    requires = [ "postgresql.target" ];
    wantedBy = [ "multi-user.target" ];
    environment = {
      LUNA_OWNER_LOGIN = site.ownerLogin;
      LUNA_WEB_ORIGIN = "https://${fqdn}:8443";
      LUNA_WEB_PORT = "8091";
      LUNA_WEB_SECRET_FILE = "${stateDir}/secret_key_base";
      LUNA_CORE_SOCKET = "/run/luna-api/api.sock";
      # The Wyoming servers in voice.nix. Without them, a voice turn shows a notice.
      LUNA_STT_URI = "tcp://127.0.0.1:10300";
      LUNA_TTS_URI = "tcp://127.0.0.1:10200";
      LUNA_DB_SOCKET_DIR = "/run/postgresql";
      LUNA_DB_USER = user;
      LUNA_DB_NAME = user;
      # No Erlang distribution: no epmd and no node port.
      RELEASE_DISTRIBUTION = "none";
      RELEASE_TMP = "/run/luna-web";
    };
    serviceConfig = {
      ExecStart = start;
      User = user;
      Group = user;
      StateDirectory = "luna-web";
      StateDirectoryMode = "0700";
      RuntimeDirectory = "luna-web";
      RuntimeDirectoryMode = "0700";
      Restart = "on-failure";
      RestartSec = 10;

      ProtectSystem = "strict";
      ProtectHome = true;
      PrivateTmp = true;
      PrivateDevices = true;
      NoNewPrivileges = true;
      CapabilityBoundingSet = "";
      ProtectKernelTunables = true;
      ProtectKernelModules = true;
      ProtectKernelLogs = true;
      ProtectControlGroups = true;
      ProtectClock = true;
      ProtectHostname = true;
      ProtectProc = "invisible";
      RestrictAddressFamilies = [ "AF_INET" "AF_INET6" "AF_UNIX" ];
      RestrictNamespaces = true;
      RestrictRealtime = true;
      RestrictSUIDSGID = true;
      LockPersonality = true;
      SystemCallArchitectures = "native";
      SystemCallFilter = [ "@system-service" "~@privileged" ];
    };
  };
}
#+end_src

** VM test

The VM test starts =modules/luna/web.nix= in a QEMU virtual machine.
The test needs KVM, so it is not a flake check.
To run the test, run =make web-vm-test= in =nyx/luna=.

The test makes sure that:
- the app refuses a request with no login,
- the owner gets the page with the four panels,
- the user =luna_web= is in the group =luna-api=,
- the owner gets the static files,
- PostgreSQL and =epmd= have no TCP port,
- the refusal is in the audit log, and the audit log refuses DELETE,
- the service starts again after a restart.

#+begin_src nix :tangle tests/web-vm.nix
# The VM test of modules/luna/web.nix. Run it with: make web-vm-test
{ testers }:

testers.runNixOSTest {
  name = "luna-web";
  node.specialArgs = {
    hostName = "luna";
    site = { tailnet = "example.ts.net"; ownerLogin = "owner@example.com"; };
  };
  nodes.machine = { pkgs, ... }: {
    imports = [ ../../modules/luna/web.nix ];
    environment.systemPackages = [ pkgs.curl ];
  };
  testScript = ''
    owner = "-H 'Tailscale-User-Login: owner@example.com'"
    machine.wait_for_unit("luna-web.service")
    machine.wait_for_open_port(8091)
    machine.succeed("curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' http://127.0.0.1:8091/ | grep -x 403")
    page = machine.succeed(f"curl -sf {owner} http://127.0.0.1:8091/")
    assert "Approvals" in page, page
    machine.succeed("id -nG luna_web | grep -w luna-api")
    machine.succeed(f"curl -sf {owner} http://127.0.0.1:8091/vendor/phoenix_live_view.min.js >/dev/null")
    machine.fail("ss -ltn | grep -E ':(5432|4369) '")
    events = machine.succeed("sudo -u luna_web psql -tA -c 'SELECT event FROM audit_events' luna_web")
    assert "web.refused" in events, events
    machine.fail("sudo -u luna_web psql -c 'DELETE FROM audit_events' luna_web")
    machine.succeed("systemctl restart luna-web.service")
    machine.wait_for_open_port(8091)
  '';
}
#+end_src

* Memory seed files

LUNA copies these files to the memory on the first start.
After the first start, the files in the memory are the source of truth.

#+begin_src conf :tangle memory-seed/.gitignore
# Raw bank exports and statements do not go in the memory.
# The hub refuses them too. Use 'make money-upload FILES=...' in nyx/luna.
*.csv
*.CSV
*.ofx
*.qif
*.qfx
*.xls
*.xlsx
*.pdf
#+end_src

#+begin_src org :tangle memory-seed/README.org
,#+TITLE: LUNA memory

This directory is the memory of LUNA.
LUNA and Coops both edit these files.
Git records each change.

| File          | Content                                     |
|---------------+---------------------------------------------|
| roster.org    | The FIFO swing dates for 12 months          |
| money.org     | Annual costs, buckets and the spend plan    |
| money/        | The hledger ledger and the category rules   |
| travel.org    | Off-swing preferences and plans             |
| projects.org  | The project portfolio and scores            |
| inventory.org | Hardware parts                              |
| notes.org     | General notes                               |
| briefs/       | The morning briefs                          |
#+end_src

#+begin_src org :tangle memory-seed/roster.org
,#+TITLE: Roster

Pattern: 2 weeks on, 2 weeks off.
Write one row for each swing for the next 12 months.

| Start      | End        | Type | Site | Fly from | Fly to | Notes |
|------------+------------+------+------+----------+--------+-------|
| 2026-12-01 | 2026-12-14 | ON   |      |          |        |       |
| 2026-12-15 | 2026-12-28 | OFF  |      |          |        |       |
#+end_src

#+begin_src org :tangle memory-seed/money.org
,#+TITLE: Money

,* Income
| Source | Net per pay (AUD) | Pays per year | Net per year |
|--------+-------------------+---------------+--------------|
| FIFO   |                   |               |              |

,* Annual costs
One row for each cost. LUNA finds costs in the bank exports that are not here.

| Category  | Item            | Per year (AUD) | Due        | Bucket |
|-----------+-----------------+----------------+------------+--------|
| Housing   | Off-swing stays |                | Each swing | Bills  |
| Transport | Flights         |                | Each swing | Bills  |
| Phone     |                 |                | Monthly    | Bills  |
| Insurance |                 |                |            | Bills  |

,* Buckets
| Bucket | Account | Rule                                |
|--------+---------+-------------------------------------|
| Income |         | Pay lands here                      |
| Bills  |         | Annual costs / pays per year        |
| Buffer |         | Fill to three months of costs, then stop |
| Spend  |         | Safe-to-spend × weeks per pay       |
| Invest |         | All surplus after the buffer        |

,* Safe to spend
spend per week = (net per year − annual costs − buffer per year − invest per year) / 52
LUNA calculates the number from the Income, Annual costs and Targets tables.

,* Targets
| Target | Per year (AUD) |
|--------+----------------|
| Buffer |                |
| Invest |                |

,* Investment plan
Write the plan here before LUNA gives rebalance alerts.

,* Base or hotels
| Option          | Nights per year | Cost per night | Cost per year |
|-----------------+-----------------+----------------+---------------|
| Hotels          |             182 |                |               |
| A rented base   |             365 |                |               |
#+end_src

#+begin_src ledger :tangle memory-seed/money/main.journal
; LUNA money ledger, in hledger format. Amounts carry a currency code.
; LUNA writes tx/<year>.journal. Do not edit those files by hand.
; Edit accounts.journal, tx/opening.journal and categories.org.
; Run: hledger -f money/main.journal balance
include accounts.journal
include tx/*.journal
#+end_src

#+begin_src ledger :tangle memory-seed/money/accounts.journal
; The accounts. The type tag tells hledger the kind of account.
account assets                ; type: A
account assets:bank           ; type: C
account assets:transfers      ; type: A
account liabilities           ; type: L
account liabilities:afterpay  ; type: L
account income                ; type: R
account income:unknown        ; type: R
account expenses              ; type: X
account expenses:unknown      ; type: X
account equity                ; type: E
account equity:opening        ; type: E
#+end_src

#+begin_src ledger :tangle memory-seed/money/tx/opening.journal
; Opening balances. Write one entry for each balance that has no bank export.
; Example (Afterpay owes money, so the liability is negative):
;
; 2026-12-01 Opening balance Afterpay
;     liabilities:afterpay   AUD -0.00
;     equity:opening
#+end_src

#+begin_src org :tangle memory-seed/money/categories.org
,#+TITLE: Categories

Each rule is a pattern and an account. The match ignores case.
The first rule that matches a description wins.
A pattern cannot contain "|". Write two rules instead.
LUNA adds rules here when you agree to a suggestion.

,* Rules
| Pattern       | Account              |
|---------------+----------------------|
| transfer to   | assets:transfers     |
| transfer from | assets:transfers     |
| afterpay      | liabilities:afterpay |
#+end_src

#+begin_src org :tangle memory-seed/travel.org
,#+TITLE: Travel

,* Preferences
- Price per night (AUD): less than
- Must have: clean, comfortable, a kitchen, fast Wi-Fi
- Good to have: a gym near, quiet, walkable, interesting
- Start airport: the site airport for each swing
- Maximum flight time:

,* Off-swing plans
#+end_src

#+begin_src org :tangle memory-seed/projects.org
,#+TITLE: Projects

Rule: exactly one project has the stage SHIP.
Score = (confidence × reach × 10) / (weeks to first payment + hours per week).

| Project | Stage | Weeks to first payment | Confidence 1-5 | Reach 1-5 | Hours per week | Score |
|---------+-------+------------------------+----------------+-----------+----------------+-------|
|         | SHIP  |                        |                |           |                |       |
|         | IDEA  |                        |                |           |                |       |
#+end_src

#+begin_src org :tangle memory-seed/inventory.org
,#+TITLE: Inventory

| Part | Value | Package | Quantity | Location | Supplier | Supplier part |
|------+-------+---------+----------+----------+----------+---------------|
#+end_src

#+begin_src org :tangle memory-seed/notes.org
,#+TITLE: Notes
#+end_src

* Makefile

#+begin_src makefile :tangle Makefile
# Makefile for LUNA. Run it in nyx/luna. The flake is the nyx flake, one level up.
SHELL := bash
.SHELLFLAGS := -euo pipefail -c
.ONESHELL:

ORG       := LUNA.org
# An absolute path, so that a recipe can change directory.
FLAKE     := $(abspath ..)
HOST      ?= luna
OWNER     ?= coops
# The public IP address of the Vultr instance. Only the install uses it.
VPS_IP    ?= 108.160.135.211
SSH_KEY   ?= $(HOME)/.ssh/luna_ed25519
# Keep the host key outside the flake source. Nix copies the source to the store.
BOOTSTRAP := $(HOME)/.local/state/luna/bootstrap
DEV       := nix develop $(FLAKE) --command

.PHONY: help tangle check build install deploy host-key forget-host-key secrets matrix-users money-upload logs web-logs web-lock web-vm-test deps-audit audit

help:
	@echo "tangle           Make the build files from $(ORG)."
	@echo "check            Run the tests of luna-core, luna-web and luna-core-ex. Evaluate the luna host."
	@echo "build            Build the full luna system on this laptop."
	@echo "host-key         Make the SSH host key for the install."
	@echo "forget-host-key  Delete the local copy of the host key."
	@echo "secrets          Edit secrets/luna.yaml with sops."
	@echo "install          Install NixOS on the VPS. Set VPS_IP. ERASES THE VPS DISK."
	@echo "matrix-users     Make the Matrix accounts for $(OWNER) and luna."
	@echo "deploy           Deploy the configuration over the tailnet."
	@echo "money-upload     Send bank exports to the luna inbox. Set FILES."
	@echo "logs             Show the luna-core log."
	@echo "web-logs         Show the luna-web log."
	@echo "web-lock         Write pkgs/luna-web/mix.lock again with mix deps.get."
	@echo "web-vm-test      Run the luna-web VM test. The test needs KVM."
	@echo "deps-audit       Show retired Hex packages and security advisories."
	@echo "audit            Show the end of the audit log."

tangle:
	emacs --batch --eval "(progn (require 'org) (require 'ob-tangle) \
	  (setq org-src-preserve-indentation t) \
	  (org-babel-tangle-file \"$(ORG)\"))"

check:
	nix build $(FLAKE)#luna-core --no-link --print-build-logs
	nix build $(FLAKE)#luna-web --no-link --print-build-logs
	nix build $(FLAKE)#luna-core-ex --no-link --print-build-logs
	nix eval --raw $(FLAKE)#nixosConfigurations.$(HOST).config.system.build.toplevel.drvPath
	echo

build:
	nix build $(FLAKE)#nixosConfigurations.$(HOST).config.system.build.toplevel --print-build-logs

host-key:
	mkdir -p -m 700 $(BOOTSTRAP)/etc/ssh
	test -f $(BOOTSTRAP)/etc/ssh/ssh_host_ed25519_key || \
	  ssh-keygen -q -t ed25519 -N "" -C "root@$(HOST)" -f $(BOOTSTRAP)/etc/ssh/ssh_host_ed25519_key
	chmod 600 $(BOOTSTRAP)/etc/ssh/ssh_host_ed25519_key
	echo "Host public key (write it as &luna in nyx/.sops.yaml):"
	$(DEV) ssh-to-age < $(BOOTSTRAP)/etc/ssh/ssh_host_ed25519_key.pub

forget-host-key:
	rm -rf $(BOOTSTRAP)

secrets:
	if grep -q REPLACEWITH $(FLAKE)/.sops.yaml; then
	  echo "Write the luna host key in nyx/.sops.yaml first."
	  exit 1
	fi
	cd $(FLAKE)
	$(DEV) sops secrets/luna.yaml

# WARNING: install erases the disk of the VPS at VPS_IP.
install: check
	test -n "$(VPS_IP)" || { echo "Set VPS_IP. Example: make install VPS_IP=203.0.113.10"; exit 1; }
	test -f $(FLAKE)/secrets/luna.yaml || { echo "Run make secrets first."; exit 1; }
	test -f $(BOOTSTRAP)/etc/ssh/ssh_host_ed25519_key || { echo "Run make host-key first."; exit 1; }
	$(DEV) nixos-anywhere --flake $(FLAKE)#$(HOST) --extra-files $(BOOTSTRAP) -i $(SSH_KEY) --target-host root@$(VPS_IP)
	echo "The install is done. Delete the local host key: make forget-host-key"

matrix-users:
	echo "Write a password for the Matrix account $(OWNER). Keep it in your password manager."
	ssh -t root@$(HOST) luna-register-user -u $(OWNER) --admin
	ssh root@$(HOST) 'luna-register-user -u luna --no-admin -p "$$(cat /run/secrets/luna-matrix-password)"'
	ssh root@$(HOST) systemctl restart luna-core

deploy: check
	nixos-rebuild switch --flake $(FLAKE)#$(HOST) --target-host root@$(HOST)

# The inbox is outside git. The import deletes each file after the import.
money-upload:
	test -n "$(FILES)" || { echo "Set FILES. Example: make money-upload FILES='~/Downloads/*.csv'"; exit 1; }
	scp $(FILES) $(OWNER)@$(HOST):/var/lib/luna/inbox/
	echo "Uploaded. Delete the local copies when you no longer need them."

logs:
	ssh root@$(HOST) journalctl -u luna-core -n 200 --no-pager

web-logs:
	ssh root@$(HOST) journalctl -u luna-web -n 200 --no-pager

# Mix needs Hex. The nixpkgs Hex goes in MIX_PATH, so Mix does not download Hex.
web-lock:
	cd $(FLAKE)/pkgs/luna-web
	hex="$$(nix eval --raw --inputs-from $(FLAKE) nixpkgs#beamPackages.hex.outPath)"
	export MIX_PATH="$$(dirname "$$(find "$$hex" -name hex.app)")"
	nix shell --inputs-from $(FLAKE) nixpkgs#beamPackages.elixir --command mix deps.get

# The test file uses paths in this tree, so the build is impure.
web-vm-test:
	nix build --impure --no-link --print-build-logs --expr \
	  '(builtins.getFlake "$(FLAKE)").inputs.nixpkgs.legacyPackages.x86_64-linux.callPackage $(abspath tests/web-vm.nix) { }'

# mix hex.audit reads the advisories from hex.pm, so it needs the network.
deps-audit:
	hex="$$(nix eval --raw --inputs-from $(FLAKE) nixpkgs#beamPackages.hex.outPath)"
	export MIX_PATH="$$(dirname "$$(find "$$hex" -name hex.app)")"
	for app in luna-web luna-core-ex; do
	  echo "== $$app"
	  (cd $(FLAKE)/pkgs/$$app && nix shell --inputs-from $(FLAKE) nixpkgs#beamPackages.elixir --command mix hex.audit)
	done

audit:
	ssh root@$(HOST) tail -n 50 /var/lib/luna/audit/audit.jsonl
#+end_src

* Local variables :noexport:

# Local Variables:
# org-src-preserve-indentation: t
# End:
